8.1 CUDA Streams 與並行推理

CUDA Stream 核心概念：

CUDA Stream 是 GPU 上的任務佇列。同一個 stream 內的操作是順序執行的，但不同 stream 的操作可以並行。這讓你可以：(1) 重疊計算和資料傳輸（2) 同時推理多個不相關的模型（3) Pipeline 一個大任務的不同階段。

類比：一條生產線（stream）上的工作是順序的，但工廠可以同時開多條生產線。

"""
CUDA Streams for Inference Parallelism
面試重點：如何利用 streams 隱藏延遲和提高 GPU 利用率
"""

import torch
import torch.cuda as cuda
from typing import List, Tuple
import time

class CUDAStreamInference:
    """
    利用 CUDA Streams 實現並行推理
    
    三種常見 pattern：
    1. Compute + Transfer Overlap: 推理的同時傳下一批資料到 GPU
    2. Multi-Model Parallelism: 不同 stream 跑不同模型
    3. Pipeline Parallelism: 模型的不同階段在不同 stream
    """
    
    def __init__(self, num_streams: int = 4):
        self.streams = [cuda.Stream() for _ in range(num_streams)]
        self.events = [cuda.Event() for _ in range(num_streams)]
    
    def overlap_compute_transfer(self, 
                                  model: torch.nn.Module,
                                  data_batches: List[torch.Tensor]):
        """
        Pattern 1: 計算和資料傳輸重疊
        
        Timeline:
        Stream 0: [Transfer Batch 1] [Compute Batch 1] [Transfer Batch 3] ...
        Stream 1: [Transfer Batch 2] [Compute Batch 2] [Transfer Batch 4] ...
        
        效果：GPU 永遠有事做，不會等 CPU→GPU 資料傳輸
        """
        results = []
        
        for i, batch in enumerate(data_batches):
            stream_idx = i % len(self.streams)
            stream = self.streams[stream_idx]
            
            with cuda.stream(stream):
                # Async transfer to GPU (non-blocking)
                gpu_batch = batch.to("cuda", non_blocking=True)
                
                # Compute on this stream
                with torch.no_grad():
                    output = model(gpu_batch)
                
                # Async transfer back to CPU
                result = output.to("cpu", non_blocking=True)
                results.append((stream_idx, result))
                
                # Record event for synchronization
                self.events[stream_idx].record(stream)
        
        # Wait for all streams to complete
        cuda.synchronize()
        return [r for _, r in results]
    
    def multi_model_parallel(self,
                            models: List[torch.nn.Module],
                            inputs: List[torch.Tensor]):
        """
        Pattern 2: 多模型並行推理
        
        使用場景：Ensemble（多個模型的預測取平均/投票）
        
        注意：只有當模型足夠小，GPU 有餘力跑多個時才有效
        如果單個模型已經佔滿 GPU → 並行反而更慢（resource contention）
        """
        assert len(models) == len(inputs) <= len(self.streams)
        
        results = [None] * len(models)
        
        # Launch all models in parallel
        for i, (model, input_data) in enumerate(zip(models, inputs)):
            stream = self.streams[i]
            with cuda.stream(stream):
                gpu_input = input_data.to("cuda", non_blocking=True)
                with torch.no_grad():
                    results[i] = model(gpu_input)
                self.events[i].record(stream)
        
        # Synchronize all
        for event in self.events[:len(models)]:
            event.synchronize()
        
        return results
    
    def pipeline_inference(self,
                          model_stages: List[torch.nn.Module],
                          batches: List[torch.Tensor]):
        """
        Pattern 3: Pipeline Parallelism
        
        把模型分成 N 個 stage，每個 stage 在不同 stream
        Batch 1 在 Stage 2 時，Batch 2 可以進入 Stage 1
        
        效果：N 個 stage 的 model，throughput 接近 N 倍
        （穩態時所有 stage 同時工作）
        """
        num_stages = len(model_stages)
        num_batches = len(batches)
        
        # Pipeline: stage i 等 stage i-1 完成才能開始
        stage_events = [[cuda.Event() for _ in range(num_batches)] 
                        for _ in range(num_stages)]
        
        intermediates = [[None] * num_batches for _ in range(num_stages + 1)]
        intermediates[0] = [b.to("cuda", non_blocking=True) for b in batches]
        
        for stage_idx in range(num_stages):
            stream = self.streams[stage_idx % len(self.streams)]
            model_stage = model_stages[stage_idx]
            
            for batch_idx in range(num_batches):
                with cuda.stream(stream):
                    # Wait for previous stage to finish this batch
                    if stage_idx > 0:
                        stage_events[stage_idx - 1][batch_idx].synchronize()
                    
                    # Compute
                    input_data = intermediates[stage_idx][batch_idx]
                    with torch.no_grad():
                        output = model_stage(input_data)
                    intermediates[stage_idx + 1][batch_idx] = output
                    
                    # Record completion
                    stage_events[stage_idx][batch_idx].record(stream)
        
        cuda.synchronize()
        return intermediates[-1]


# ============================================================
# CUDA Memory Management for Inference
# ============================================================
class CUDAMemoryManager:
    """
    GPU Memory 管理 - 避免 OOM 和碎片化
    
    面試常問：如何在有限 GPU memory 中最大化 throughput？
    
    策略：
    1. Memory Pool: 預分配 + 重用（避免 malloc/free overhead）
    2. Memory Planning: 提前計算所有中間 tensor 大小，靜態分配
    3. Activation Checkpointing: 用計算換記憶體（不存中間結果，需要時重算）
    """
    
    def __init__(self, device: int = 0):
        self.device = device
        props = cuda.get_device_properties(device)
        self.total_memory = props.total_mem
        self.reserved_memory = 0
    
    def estimate_model_memory(self, model: torch.nn.Module) -> dict:
        """估算模型的 memory 需求"""
        param_memory = sum(
            p.numel() * p.element_size() for p in model.parameters()
        )
        buffer_memory = sum(
            b.numel() * b.element_size() for b in model.buffers()
        )
        
        return {
            "parameters_mb": param_memory / 1024**2,
            "buffers_mb": buffer_memory / 1024**2,
            "total_model_mb": (param_memory + buffer_memory) / 1024**2,
            "gpu_total_mb": self.total_memory / 1024**2,
            "available_for_activations_mb": (
                self.total_memory - param_memory - buffer_memory
            ) / 1024**2,
        }
    
    def estimate_kv_cache_memory(self,
                                 num_layers: int,
                                 num_heads: int,
                                 head_dim: int,
                                 max_seq_len: int,
                                 batch_size: int,
                                 dtype_bytes: int = 2) -> float:
        """
        估算 LLM KV-cache 的 memory 需求
        
        公式：2 * num_layers * batch_size * num_heads * head_dim * seq_len * dtype_bytes
        （2 是因為 K 和 V 各一份）
        
        範例：LLaMA-2 70B, batch_size=16, seq_len=2048
        = 2 * 80 * 16 * 64 * 128 * 2048 * 2 bytes
        = 2 * 80 * 16 * 64 * 128 * 2048 * 2 / 1024³
        ≈ 40 GB ← 非常大！這就是為什麼需要 PagedAttention
        """
        kv_cache_bytes = (
            2 *  # K and V
            num_layers *
            batch_size *
            num_heads *
            head_dim *
            max_seq_len *
            dtype_bytes
        )
        return kv_cache_bytes / 1024**3  # GB

8.2 TensorRT 深入解析

"""
TensorRT - NVIDIA's Production Inference Engine
面試核心：理解 TensorRT 做了哪些優化
"""

import torch
import numpy as np
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple

class TensorRTOptimizations:
    """
    TensorRT 的主要優化技術
    
    面試必知的 5 大優化：
    1. Layer Fusion: Conv+BN+ReLU → 1 kernel（減少 memory 讀寫）
    2. Precision Calibration: FP32 → FP16/INT8（計算量減半/四分之一）
    3. Kernel Auto-Tuning: 同一操作嘗試多種 kernel 實作，選最快的
    4. Memory Optimization: Tensor 重用，最小化 memory footprint
    5. Dynamic Shape: 支援可變 batch_size/seq_len
    """
    
    @staticmethod
    def explain_layer_fusion():
        """
        Layer Fusion 的類型和效果
        
        面試常問：為什麼 fusion 能加速？
        答：GPU 計算很快，但讀寫 global memory 很慢。
        沒有 fusion：Conv 寫結果到 memory → BN 讀回來計算再寫回 → ReLU 再讀再寫
        有 fusion：Conv+BN+ReLU 在同一個 kernel 裡做完，中間結果留在 registers/shared memory
        → 減少 2 次 global memory round-trip = 巨大加速
        """
        fusion_types = {
            "Vertical Fusion": {
                "example": "Conv → BatchNorm → ReLU → 1 fused kernel",
                "benefit": "減少 memory traffic，中間結果不寫回 DRAM",
                "constraint": "必須是連續的、elementwise 或 reduction ops"
            },
            "Horizontal Fusion": {
                "example": "3 個並行的 1x1 Conv → 1 個大 Conv",
                "benefit": "增大矩陣大小，更好利用 Tensor Core",
                "constraint": "必須是相同類型的獨立操作"
            },
            "Multi-Head Attention Fusion": {
                "example": "Q/K/V projection + attention + output → 1 kernel",
                "benefit": "整個 attention block 一次完成",
                "constraint": "需要特殊 kernel 實作（如 Flash Attention）"
            }
        }
        return fusion_types
    
    @staticmethod
    def build_engine_example():
        """
        TensorRT Engine Build 流程
        
        ONNX → TensorRT Engine 的步驟：
        1. Parse ONNX model
        2. Apply graph optimizations (fusion, elimination)
        3. For each layer, benchmark all possible kernel implementations
        4. Select optimal kernels + precision
        5. Plan memory (tensor reuse analysis)
        6. Serialize engine to file
        
        注意：Build 過程很慢（幾分鐘到幾小時），但只做一次
        生成的 engine 是硬體特定的（A100 的 engine 不能在 V100 上跑）
        """
        build_config = {
            "precision": "FP16",  # or INT8, BEST (auto-select)
            "workspace_size_mb": 4096,
            "max_batch_size": 32,
            "dynamic_shapes": {
                "input": {
                    "min": (1, 3, 224, 224),
                    "opt": (16, 3, 224, 224),  # 最常見的 shape
                    "max": (32, 3, 224, 224),
                }
            },
            "optimization_profiles": 1,
            "builder_optimization_level": 5,  # 0-5, higher = slower build, faster inference
            "timing_cache": "timing.cache",   # 跨 build 重用 kernel tuning 結果
        }
        return build_config
    
    @staticmethod
    def compare_frameworks():
        """
        框架對比（面試必備知識）
        
        | Feature          | TensorRT    | ONNX Runtime | PyTorch    | TFLite     |
        |-----------------|-------------|--------------|------------|------------|
        | Platform        | NVIDIA GPU  | Cross-platform| All       | Mobile/Edge|
        | Precision       | FP32/16/INT8| FP32/16      | FP32/16   | INT8/FP16  |
        | Fusion          | Aggressive  | Moderate     | Limited   | Moderate   |
        | Dynamic Shape   | Limited     | Full         | Full      | Limited    |
        | Build Time      | Minutes     | Seconds      | None      | Minutes    |
        | Inference Speed | Fastest(GPU)| Good         | Baseline  | Good(mobile)|
        | Ease of Use     | Complex     | Easy         | Easiest   | Moderate   |
        """
        return {
            "TensorRT": "最快的 GPU 推理，但 build 複雜、不可移植",
            "ONNX Runtime": "跨平台首選，CPU/GPU 都不錯，易用",
            "PyTorch (torch.compile)": "2.0+ 的 torch.compile 接近 TensorRT 效能",
            "vLLM": "LLM 專用，PagedAttention + continuous batching",
        }

8.3 ONNX Runtime

"""
ONNX Runtime - 跨平台推理引擎
面試重點：何時選 ORT vs TensorRT？
"""

import onnxruntime as ort
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List

class ONNXRuntimeInference:
    """
    ONNX Runtime 使用指南
    
    優勢 vs TensorRT：
    1. 跨平台：CPU/GPU/NPU/FPGA 都支援
    2. 動態 shape 原生支援（不需要 optimization profiles）
    3. 更簡單的 API（不需要 build engine 步驟）
    4. 支援更多 operators（TensorRT 有些 op 不支援需要 fallback）
    
    劣勢：
    1. GPU 效能比 TensorRT 慢 10-30%
    2. Fusion 不如 TensorRT 激進
    3. INT8 calibration 工具不如 TensorRT 成熟
    
    何時選 ORT：
    - 需要跨平台部署（同一模型跑 CPU + GPU）
    - 模型有 TensorRT 不支援的 operator
    - 快速迭代（不想等 TensorRT build）
    - Azure 生態（ORT 是 Azure ML 的 default）
    """
    
    def __init__(self, model_path: str, use_gpu: bool = True):
        # Session options
        sess_options = ort.SessionOptions()
        sess_options.graph_optimization_level = (
            ort.GraphOptimizationLevel.ORT_ENABLE_ALL
        )
        sess_options.intra_op_num_threads = 4
        sess_options.inter_op_num_threads = 2
        
        # Execution providers（按優先順序）
        if use_gpu:
            providers = [
                ("CUDAExecutionProvider", {
                    "device_id": 0,
                    "arena_extend_strategy": "kNextPowerOfTwo",
                    "gpu_mem_limit": 4 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024,  # 4GB
                    "cudnn_conv_algo_search": "EXHAUSTIVE",
                }),
                "CPUExecutionProvider",  # Fallback
            ]
        else:
            providers = ["CPUExecutionProvider"]
        
        self.session = ort.InferenceSession(
            model_path, 
            sess_options=sess_options,
            providers=providers
        )
        
        # Cache input/output names
        self.input_names = [i.name for i in self.session.get_inputs()]
        self.output_names = [o.name for o in self.session.get_outputs()]
    
    def infer(self, inputs: Dict[str, np.ndarray]) -> List[np.ndarray]:
        """執行推理"""
        outputs = self.session.run(self.output_names, inputs)
        return outputs
    
    def benchmark(self, 
                  sample_input: Dict[str, np.ndarray],
                  num_warmup: int = 10,
                  num_runs: int = 100) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """性能基準測試"""
        import time
        
        # Warmup
        for _ in range(num_warmup):
            self.session.run(self.output_names, sample_input)
        
        # Benchmark
        latencies = []
        for _ in range(num_runs):
            start = time.perf_counter()
            self.session.run(self.output_names, sample_input)
            latencies.append((time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000)
        
        return {
            "mean_ms": np.mean(latencies),
            "p50_ms": np.percentile(latencies, 50),
            "p95_ms": np.percentile(latencies, 95),
            "p99_ms": np.percentile(latencies, 99),
            "throughput_qps": 1000 / np.mean(latencies),
        }

8.4 Flash Attention

Flash Attention 的革命性意義：

Standard Attention 的問題：需要寫一個 \(O(N^2)\) 大小的 attention matrix 到 GPU HBM（High Bandwidth Memory）。當 seq_len=4096 時，這個矩陣有 16M 個 float16 元素 = 32MB per head per batch。讀寫 HBM 是 GPU 的最大瓶頸。

Flash Attention 的解法：用 tiling（分塊）把計算分成小塊，每塊在 SRAM（快但小，192KB per SM）中完成，永遠不需要把完整的 attention matrix 寫到 HBM。

效果：2-4x 加速 + memory 從 O(N²) 降到 O(N)。這讓長上下文 LLM（100K+ tokens）成為可能。

"""
Flash Attention - I/O-aware Exact Attention
面試必考：為什麼 Flash Attention 快？它的核心 insight 是什麼？
"""

import torch
import math
from typing import Optional, Tuple

class FlashAttentionExplained:
    """
    Flash Attention 原理解析
    
    面試核心 insight：
    GPU 的計算能力（FLOPS）遠大於記憶體頻寬（bytes/s）。
    Standard attention 是 memory-bound（讀寫 N² attention matrix 到 HBM）。
    Flash Attention 是 compute-bound（多做一些 recomputation，但避免 HBM 讀寫）。
    
    trade-off: 多做少量計算 ← → 大幅減少 memory I/O
    在現代 GPU 上（A100: 312 TFLOPS vs 2TB/s HBM BW），
    compute 便宜，memory I/O 貴 → Flash Attention 大贏
    """
    
    @staticmethod
    def standard_attention(Q, K, V, mask=None):
        """
        Standard Attention（慢的版本 - 面試對照用）
        
        Memory access pattern:
        1. S = Q @ K^T  → 寫 [N, N] 到 HBM
        2. P = softmax(S) → 讀 [N, N] 再寫 [N, N]  
        3. O = P @ V → 讀 [N, N] 再寫 [N, d]
        
        Total HBM access: O(N² + Nd) ← N² 項 dominates
        """
        d_k = Q.shape[-1]
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) / math.sqrt(d_k)
        if mask is not None:
            scores = scores.masked_fill(mask == 0, -1e9)
        attn_weights = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1)  # [N, N] in HBM!
        output = torch.matmul(attn_weights, V)
        return output
    
    @staticmethod
    def flash_attention_conceptual(Q, K, V, block_size: int = 64):
        """
        Flash Attention 概念實作（Python pseudocode）
        
        真正的 Flash Attention 是 CUDA C++ 寫的 fused kernel
        這裡用 Python 展示核心邏輯（面試時畫在白板上的版本）
        
        核心算法（Online Softmax + Tiling）：
        1. 把 Q 分成 blocks（每 block 64 rows）
        2. 把 K, V 分成 blocks（每 block 64 rows）
        3. 對每個 Q block：
           - 逐個載入 K/V block 到 SRAM
           - 計算部分 attention（用 online softmax 累積）
           - 不需要存完整的 attention matrix！
        """
        N, d = Q.shape[-2], Q.shape[-1]
        O = torch.zeros_like(Q)
        
        # 用於 online softmax 的累積量
        l = torch.zeros((*Q.shape[:-1], 1), device=Q.device)  # softmax denominator
        m = torch.full((*Q.shape[:-1], 1), float("-inf"), device=Q.device)  # running max
        
        # Tile Q into blocks
        num_q_blocks = (N + block_size - 1) // block_size
        num_kv_blocks = (N + block_size - 1) // block_size
        
        for q_block_idx in range(num_q_blocks):
            q_start = q_block_idx * block_size
            q_end = min(q_start + block_size, N)
            Q_block = Q[..., q_start:q_end, :]  # Load Q block to SRAM
            
            O_block = torch.zeros_like(Q_block)
            l_block = torch.zeros((*Q_block.shape[:-1], 1), device=Q.device)
            m_block = torch.full((*Q_block.shape[:-1], 1), float("-inf"), device=Q.device)
            
            for kv_block_idx in range(num_kv_blocks):
                kv_start = kv_block_idx * block_size
                kv_end = min(kv_start + block_size, N)
                K_block = K[..., kv_start:kv_end, :]  # Load K block to SRAM
                V_block = V[..., kv_start:kv_end, :]  # Load V block to SRAM
                
                # Compute attention scores for this block pair
                S_block = torch.matmul(Q_block, K_block.transpose(-2, -1))
                S_block = S_block / math.sqrt(d)
                
                # Online softmax update (key insight!)
                m_new = torch.maximum(m_block, S_block.max(dim=-1, keepdim=True).values)
                
                # Rescale previous accumulations
                exp_old = torch.exp(m_block - m_new)
                exp_new = torch.exp(S_block - m_new)
                
                l_new = exp_old * l_block + exp_new.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
                
                # Update output accumulator
                O_block = (exp_old * O_block + 
                          torch.matmul(exp_new, V_block))
                
                m_block = m_new
                l_block = l_new
            
            # Normalize
            O[..., q_start:q_end, :] = O_block / l_block
        
        return O
    
    @staticmethod
    def usage_in_practice():
        """
        實際使用 Flash Attention
        
        PyTorch 2.0+ 內建：
        torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention() 
        自動選擇 Flash Attention / Memory Efficient Attention / Math
        """
        import torch.nn.functional as F
        
        # PyTorch 2.0+ 的方式（自動用 Flash Attention）
        B, H, N, d = 4, 32, 4096, 128
        Q = torch.randn(B, H, N, d, device="cuda", dtype=torch.float16)
        K = torch.randn(B, H, N, d, device="cuda", dtype=torch.float16)
        V = torch.randn(B, H, N, d, device="cuda", dtype=torch.float16)
        
        # 這一行自動使用 Flash Attention！
        output = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
            Q, K, V,
            attn_mask=None,
            dropout_p=0.0,
            is_causal=True,  # causal mask for autoregressive LLM
        )
        # Memory: O(N) instead of O(N²)
        # Speed: 2-4x faster than naive attention
        return output

8.5 KV-Cache 管理

"""
KV-Cache - LLM Inference 的核心優化
面試必考：為什麼需要 KV-Cache？memory 影響？
"""

import torch
from typing import Optional, Tuple, List

class KVCacheExplained:
    """
    KV-Cache 原理
    
    面試核心問題：為什麼 LLM decode 需要 KV-Cache？
    
    答：Auto-regressive generation 每步只生成 1 token，
    但 attention 需要看到所有之前的 tokens。
    
    沒有 KV-Cache：每生成一個 token 都要重算所有之前 token 的 K、V
    → 生成 N 個 tokens 的計算量是 O(N² × d × layers)
    
    有 KV-Cache：之前計算過的 K、V 存下來，每步只算新 token 的 K、V
    → 生成 N 個 tokens 的計算量降到 O(N × d × layers)
    → N 倍加速！
    
    代價：Memory 使用量 = O(batch × layers × heads × seq_len × head_dim × 2)
    LLaMA-2 70B, batch=1, seq_len=4096: KV-cache ≈ 5GB
    """
    
    def __init__(self, 
                 num_layers: int,
                 num_heads: int,
                 head_dim: int,
                 max_seq_len: int,
                 dtype=torch.float16):
        self.num_layers = num_layers
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.head_dim = head_dim
        self.max_seq_len = max_seq_len
        self.dtype = dtype
        
        # Pre-allocate KV-cache for each layer
        # Shape: [batch, num_heads, max_seq_len, head_dim]
        self.k_cache: List[Optional[torch.Tensor]] = [None] * num_layers
        self.v_cache: List[Optional[torch.Tensor]] = [None] * num_layers
        self.current_length = 0
    
    def update(self, 
               layer_idx: int,
               new_k: torch.Tensor,  # [batch, heads, new_tokens, head_dim]
               new_v: torch.Tensor):
        """追加新 token 的 K、V 到 cache"""
        if self.k_cache[layer_idx] is None:
            self.k_cache[layer_idx] = new_k
            self.v_cache[layer_idx] = new_v
        else:
            self.k_cache[layer_idx] = torch.cat(
                [self.k_cache[layer_idx], new_k], dim=2
            )
            self.v_cache[layer_idx] = torch.cat(
                [self.v_cache[layer_idx], new_v], dim=2
            )
        
        if layer_idx == 0:
            self.current_length += new_k.shape[2]
    
    def get(self, layer_idx: int) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        """取得累積的 K、V"""
        return self.k_cache[layer_idx], self.v_cache[layer_idx]
    
    def memory_usage_gb(self, batch_size: int) -> float:
        """計算 KV-cache memory 使用量"""
        bytes_per_element = 2 if self.dtype == torch.float16 else 4
        total_bytes = (
            2 *  # K and V
            self.num_layers *
            batch_size *
            self.num_heads *
            self.current_length *
            self.head_dim *
            bytes_per_element
        )
        return total_bytes / 1024**3


class PagedKVCache:
    """
    Paged KV-Cache (vLLM 的核心創新)
    
    問題：傳統 KV-Cache 為每個序列預分配 max_seq_len 的連續 memory
    → Internal fragmentation（序列沒用完的空間浪費了）
    → External fragmentation（碎片化導致放不下新序列）
    
    解法：借鑑 OS Virtual Memory
    - Physical: GPU memory 切成固定大小的 blocks（如 16 tokens/block）
    - Logical: 每個序列有一個 page table 映射 logical→physical block
    - Allocation: 按需分配，用完即回收
    
    效果：
    - Memory utilization: 50-60% → 95%+
    - 同樣 GPU 可以服務 2-4x 更多並行序列
    - 支援 copy-on-write（beam search 共享 prefix）
    """
    
    def __init__(self,
                 block_size: int = 16,      # tokens per block
                 num_blocks: int = 2048,    # total physical blocks
                 num_layers: int = 32,
                 num_heads: int = 32,
                 head_dim: int = 128,
                 dtype=torch.float16):
        self.block_size = block_size
        self.num_blocks = num_blocks
        
        # Physical block pool
        # Each block: [num_layers, 2, num_heads, block_size, head_dim]
        elem_size = 2 if dtype == torch.float16 else 4
        block_bytes = num_layers * 2 * num_heads * block_size * head_dim * elem_size
        self.block_size_mb = block_bytes / 1024**2
        
        # Free block list
        self.free_blocks: List[int] = list(range(num_blocks))
        
        # Page tables: sequence_id → list of block_ids
        self.page_tables: Dict[str, List[int]] = {}
    
    def allocate(self, seq_id: str, num_tokens: int) -> bool:
        """為序列分配 blocks"""
        blocks_needed = (num_tokens + self.block_size - 1) // self.block_size
        
        if blocks_needed > len(self.free_blocks):
            return False  # OOM
        
        allocated = self.free_blocks[:blocks_needed]
        self.free_blocks = self.free_blocks[blocks_needed:]
        self.page_tables[seq_id] = allocated
        return True
    
    def append_token(self, seq_id: str) -> bool:
        """序列生成新 token → 可能需要分配新 block"""
        blocks = self.page_tables[seq_id]
        current_tokens = len(blocks) * self.block_size
        
        # 檢查最後一個 block 是否還有空間
        # 如果沒有，需要分配新 block
        if not self.free_blocks:
            return False
        
        # Allocate new block if needed
        new_block = self.free_blocks.pop(0)
        self.page_tables[seq_id].append(new_block)
        return True
    
    def free(self, seq_id: str):
        """序列完成 → 釋放所有 blocks"""
        if seq_id in self.page_tables:
            self.free_blocks.extend(self.page_tables[seq_id])
            del self.page_tables[seq_id]
    
    def copy_on_write(self, src_seq_id: str, dst_seq_id: str):
        """
        Beam search: 新 beam 共享 parent 的 blocks
        只在 diverge 時才 copy 對應 block
        """
        src_blocks = self.page_tables[src_seq_id]
        # 共享而非複製（reference counting）
        self.page_tables[dst_seq_id] = src_blocks.copy()
    
    def get_utilization(self) -> float:
        used = self.num_blocks - len(self.free_blocks)
        return used / self.num_blocks

8.6 torch.compile 和 Python 層優化

"""
PyTorch 2.0 torch.compile - 不換框架也能加速
面試加分：了解最新的 PyTorch 原生優化
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn

class TorchCompileGuide:
    """
    torch.compile 使用指南
    
    核心思想：把 Python/PyTorch 程式碼 JIT 編譯成高效 GPU kernel
    不需要轉 ONNX/TensorRT，直接在 PyTorch 中獲得接近 TensorRT 的效能
    
    面試重點：
    1. 它做了什麼？→ 圖捕捉 + kernel fusion + 降到 Triton/CUDA 後端
    2. 限制是什麼？→ 動態 control flow、data-dependent shapes 不支援
    3. 何時用？→ 想要加速但不想離開 PyTorch 生態時
    """
    
    @staticmethod
    def basic_usage():
        """基本用法 - 一行代碼加速"""
        model = nn.TransformerEncoder(
            nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=512, nhead=8),
            num_layers=6
        ).cuda()
        
        # 一行代碼加速推理（通常 1.5-3x 加速）
        compiled_model = torch.compile(model, mode="reduce-overhead")
        
        # 第一次推理會觸發 compilation（慢）
        # 之後的推理用 compiled kernel（快）
        x = torch.randn(32, 100, 512, device="cuda")
        output = compiled_model(x)  # First call: compile (slow)
        output = compiled_model(x)  # Subsequent: fast!
        
        return compiled_model
    
    @staticmethod
    def compilation_modes():
        """
        三種 compilation mode
        
        面試知道這些就夠了：
        """
        modes = {
            "default": {
                "desc": "平衡 compile time 和 inference speed",
                "compile_time": "中",
                "speedup": "1.5-2x",
                "use_case": "大多數場景"
            },
            "reduce-overhead": {
                "desc": "減少 Python overhead（用 CUDA graphs）",
                "compile_time": "中",
                "speedup": "2-3x（小 batch 效果明顯）",
                "use_case": "real-time inference, 小 batch_size"
            },
            "max-autotune": {
                "desc": "嘗試所有可能的 kernel 配置",
                "compile_time": "很長（minutes）",
                "speedup": "2-4x",
                "use_case": "固定 workload，追求最佳效能"
            }
        }
        return modes
🧪 驗證考題 + 詳解

Q1: Flash Attention 為什麼是 exact attention（精確的）而不是 approximate？

答案：Flash Attention 只改變了計算順序和 memory access pattern，沒有改變數學。它用 online softmax 的等價變換：把 softmax(QK^T)V 分塊計算，每塊的部分結果通過 rescaling（乘以 exp(m_old - m_new)）正確合併。最終結果和 standard attention 完全相同（bit-exact）。它只是 reorders 計算 to be I/O efficient，不做任何近似。

Q2: KV-Cache 對 LLM 推理的記憶體影響有多大？LLaMA-2 7B 的 KV-cache 大小？

答案：LLaMA-2 7B: 32 layers, 32 heads, head_dim=128, FP16。每 token 的 KV-cache = 2(K+V) × 32(layers) × 32(heads) × 128(dim) × 2(bytes) = 524KB/token。seq_len=4096 → 2GB/sequence。batch_size=8 → 16GB KV-cache（超過模型本身 14GB！）。這就是為什麼 KV-cache 管理（PagedAttention）如此重要。

Q3: TensorRT 的 kernel auto-tuning 具體做了什麼？

答案：對每一個 operator（如 GEMM），TensorRT 會嘗試多種 kernel implementations：(1) 不同的 tile sizes（如 128x128, 256x64）；(2) 不同的 warp scheduling 策略；(3) 不同的 shared memory 使用量；(4) cuBLAS vs cuDNN vs custom kernel。在 build 時逐一 benchmark，選最快的。這就是為什麼 build 很慢但 inference 很快——把 tuning 的成本離線化了。engine 是硬體特定的：A100 上 tune 的結果在 V100 無效。

Q4: CUDA Streams 的 multi-model parallelism 什麼時候有效？什麼時候無效？

答案：有效條件：(1) 每個模型的 GPU utilization < 50%（有閒置 SM）；(2) 模型足夠小（memory 裝得下多個）；(3) 模型的計算和記憶體 pattern 互補（一個 compute-bound + 一個 memory-bound）。無效情況：(1) 單個模型已用盡所有 SM → 並行反而增加 resource contention；(2) 兩個模型都很大 → GPU memory 不夠；(3) 兩個模型都是 memory-bound → 搶 HBM bandwidth 導致都變慢。經驗法則：如果單模型 GPU util < 40%，可以嘗試並行。

Q5: 對比 PagedAttention (vLLM) vs Ring Attention vs Sequence Parallelism 三種長序列解法。

答案：

  • PagedAttention：解決 memory fragmentation。不改變計算，只改 memory layout（paging）。適合：同一 GPU 上提高 batch 數量。
  • Ring Attention：跨多 GPU 切分 sequence dimension。每個 GPU 只存部分 KV-cache，通過 ring-allreduce 通訊完成 attention。適合：超長序列（100K+ tokens）超過單卡 memory。
  • Sequence Parallelism：把 LayerNorm 和 Dropout 等 non-tensor-parallel ops 也分散到多卡。適合：配合 Tensor Parallelism 時減少 activation memory。
  • 選擇：單卡 → PagedAttention；超長文 + 多卡 → Ring Attention；訓練 + 多卡 → Sequence Parallelism。
🎯 Follow-up Q&A（面試延伸問題）

F1: 如果面試官問「如何讓 LLaMA-3 70B 的首 token 延遲 (TTFT) 低於 500ms？」

答案：多管齊下：(1) Tensor Parallelism 跨 4-8 張 GPU（分攤 compute）；(2) INT4 quantization（AWQ/GPTQ 減少 weight loading）；(3) Flash Attention（減少 memory I/O）；(4) TensorRT-LLM 或 vLLM 的 optimized prefill kernel；(5) Chunked prefill（如果 prompt 很長，分段做 prefill 的第一段先返回）。70B INT4 on 4xA100 with TP4 的 TTFT：~200-400ms for 1024 prompt tokens。

F2: torch.compile vs TensorRT vs ONNX Runtime，三者的 compilation 原理有什麼根本差異？

答案：(1) torch.compile 通過 dynamo 捕捉 Python execution trace → inductor 後端生成 Triton kernels。保持 Python 生態但 dynamic control flow 有限。(2) TensorRT 接受 ONNX 靜態圖 → 做 layer fusion + kernel tuning → 生成 CUDA engine binary。最高效但不靈活。(3) ONNX Runtime 接受 ONNX 圖 → 圖優化 → dispatch 到 EP (Execution Provider)。跨平台但 GPU 優化不如 TensorRT 深。根本差異：input representation（Python trace vs 靜態圖）和 optimization depth（kernels from scratch vs pattern matching）。

F3: Speculative Decoding 的原理？為什麼能加速而不損失品質？

答案：用小 draft model 快速生成 K 個候選 tokens，然後用大 target model 一次 forward pass 同時 verify 所有 K 個。如果 draft 和 target 的分佈一致 → 全部接受（K tokens for 1 forward pass = K倍加速）。如果不一致 → 用 rejection sampling 修正，保證最終分佈完全等於 target model。數學上是 exact（不是 approximate）。加速比 = acceptance_rate × K。典型 acceptance_rate: 70-85%，K=5 → 有效加速 2-3x。

F4: Multi-Query Attention (MQA) 和 Grouped-Query Attention (GQA) 如何減少 KV-Cache？

答案：Standard MHA：每個 head 有自己的 K/V → KV-cache = 2 × layers × heads × seq × dim。MQA：所有 heads 共享一組 K/V → KV-cache 減少 heads 倍（如 32x）。代價：品質略降。GQA（折衷）：每 G 個 heads 共享一組 K/V → KV-cache 減少 G 倍。LLaMA-2 70B 用 GQA（8 groups）→ KV-cache 減少 4x。這讓 batch_size 可以更大 → throughput 更高。

F5: 如何在面試中回答「Design a system to serve GPT-4 level model to 1M users」？

答案：架構要點：(1) 分層：API Gateway → Router → Inference Cluster → Model Store；(2) 模型：MoE 架構（不是每個 token 都用所有 experts）→ 降低 per-token compute；(3) 硬體：H100 cluster with NVLink + InfiniBand，TP8 per model；(4) Serving：vLLM continuous batching + PagedAttention + Speculative Decoding；(5) Scaling：按 region 分佈 inference cluster，request routing 到最近的；(6) Cost：INT4 quantization + distilled smaller models for simple queries（router 判斷 query 複雜度）；(7) SLO：TTFT < 1s, ITL < 50ms, availability > 99.9%。

F6: GPU memory 不夠放 KV-cache 時，除了 eviction 還有什麼方案？

答案：五個方向：(1) KV-cache quantization（INT8/INT4，損失極小）→ memory 減半/四分之一；(2) Offloading to CPU RAM（swap out 低優先級序列，需要時 swap back）；(3) KV-cache compression（attention sink：只保留前幾個 tokens + 最近幾個 tokens 的 KV，中間的 evict）；(4) Shared prefix caching（多個 request 共享相同 system prompt 的 KV-cache）→ 如 vLLM prefix caching；(5) Streaming LLM（sliding window attention，只保留 window 內的 KV）→ 犧牲長距離依賴。

Chapter 9: LLM Training — 大型語言模型分散式訓練

🌱 從零開始理解

生活比喻：想像你要搬一棟 100 層大樓的家具。一個人搬需要 10 年，但如果你找 1000 個工人，問題變成「如何協調」：有人搬客廳、有人搬臥室（Data Parallel）；有人負責搬 1-50 樓、有人搬 51-100 樓（Pipeline Parallel）；有人搬大型家具的左半邊、有人搬右半邊（Tensor Parallel）。LLM 訓練就是這個協調問題。

白話解釋：GPT-4 等級模型有數千億參數，單張 GPU 記憶體（80GB A100）根本放不下。分散式訓練就是把模型「拆開」放到多張 GPU 上，同時確保數學上等價於單機訓練。

為什麼工作上需要：Google L5 面試會問你「設計一個系統來訓練 70B 參數的 LLM」，你需要能討論 GPU topology、通訊瓶頸、記憶體優化策略。

先備知識：基本深度學習（forward/backward pass）、GPU 記憶體模型（parameters + gradients + optimizer states）、PyTorch 基礎。

9.1 GPU 記憶體分析：為什麼需要分散式？

💡 核心概念

訓練一個模型需要儲存的記憶體遠超參數本身：

  • Parameters: 模型權重 (fp16 = 2 bytes/param)
  • Gradients: 反向傳播梯度 (fp16 = 2 bytes/param)
  • Optimizer States: Adam 需要 m (momentum) + v (variance) 各 fp32 = 8 bytes/param
  • Activations: 中間計算結果，與 batch size × sequence length 成正比

所以 70B 參數模型：70B × (2+2+8) = 840 GB 只算 states，一張 A100 80GB 完全不夠。

def estimate_training_memory(num_params_billion: float, 
                              precision: str = "fp16",
                              optimizer: str = "adam") -> dict:
    """估算 LLM 訓練記憶體需求
    
    Args:
        num_params_billion: 參數量（十億）
        precision: 訓練精度 fp16/bf16/fp32
        optimizer: 優化器類型
    
    Returns:
        記憶體分解 (GB)
    """
    params_bytes = num_params_billion * 1e9
    
    # Parameter storage
    if precision in ("fp16", "bf16"):
        param_mem = params_bytes * 2  # 2 bytes per param
        grad_mem = params_bytes * 2
    else:  # fp32
        param_mem = params_bytes * 4
        grad_mem = params_bytes * 4
    
    # Optimizer states (Adam: fp32 copy + momentum + variance)
    if optimizer == "adam":
        # Master weights (fp32) + m + v
        opt_mem = params_bytes * (4 + 4 + 4)  # 12 bytes/param
    elif optimizer == "sgd":
        opt_mem = params_bytes * 4  # momentum only
    elif optimizer == "adam_8bit":
        opt_mem = params_bytes * (4 + 1 + 1)  # 8-bit states
    
    total_gb = (param_mem + grad_mem + opt_mem) / (1024**3)
    
    return {
        "parameters_gb": param_mem / (1024**3),
        "gradients_gb": grad_mem / (1024**3),
        "optimizer_gb": opt_mem / (1024**3),
        "total_states_gb": total_gb,
        "note": "Activations not included (depends on batch/seq_len)"
    }

# Example: LLaMA-70B
mem = estimate_training_memory(70)
print(f"LLaMA-70B training memory: {mem['total_states_gb']:.0f} GB")
# Output: LLaMA-70B training memory: 1120 GB
# 需要至少 14 張 A100 80GB 才能放下 states！

# Example: GPT-4 scale (~1.8T MoE)
mem_gpt4 = estimate_training_memory(1800)
print(f"GPT-4 scale: {mem_gpt4['total_states_gb']:.0f} GB")
# Output: GPT-4 scale: 28800 GB → 360 張 A100！

9.2 Data Parallelism (DP) — 最基礎的平行化

💡 核心概念

想法：每張 GPU 持有模型完整副本，但處理不同的 data batch。Forward/backward 各自做，然後 All-Reduce 同步梯度。

限制：每張 GPU 都要放完整模型 → 模型太大就放不下。

import torch
import torch.distributed as dist
from torch.nn.parallel import DistributedDataParallel as DDP

def setup_ddp(rank: int, world_size: int):
    """初始化 PyTorch DDP 環境"""
    dist.init_process_group(
        backend="nccl",  # NVIDIA GPU 最佳
        rank=rank,
        world_size=world_size
    )
    torch.cuda.set_device(rank)

def train_with_ddp(rank: int, world_size: int, model, dataset):
    """標準 DDP 訓練迴圈"""
    setup_ddp(rank, world_size)
    
    model = model.to(rank)
    # DDP wraps model: auto gradient sync via All-Reduce
    ddp_model = DDP(model, device_ids=[rank])
    
    # Each rank gets different data shard
    sampler = torch.utils.data.distributed.DistributedSampler(
        dataset, num_replicas=world_size, rank=rank, shuffle=True
    )
    dataloader = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(
        dataset, batch_size=32, sampler=sampler
    )
    
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(ddp_model.parameters(), lr=1e-4)
    
    for epoch in range(num_epochs):
        sampler.set_epoch(epoch)  # 確保每 epoch shuffle 不同
        for batch in dataloader:
            batch = {k: v.to(rank) for k, v in batch.items()}
            
            loss = ddp_model(**batch).loss
            loss.backward()  # 梯度計算 (local)
            # ⚡ All-Reduce happens HERE automatically!
            # DDP hooks synchronize gradients across all ranks
            
            optimizer.step()
            optimizer.zero_grad()
    
    dist.destroy_process_group()

# All-Reduce 通訊量分析：
# Ring All-Reduce: 每個 GPU 發送 2 * (N-1)/N * model_size
# 70B fp16 model: ~2 * 130GB = 260GB 通訊量 per step
# NVLink 900 GB/s → ~0.3s per step (acceptable)
# PCIe 4.0 64 GB/s → ~4s per step (bottleneck!)

9.3 ZeRO (Zero Redundancy Optimizer) — DeepSpeed 核心

💡 核心概念

ZeRO 的核心洞察：DDP 中每張 GPU 都存了完整的 optimizer states、gradients、parameters — 這是巨大的浪費。ZeRO 把這些 分片 (shard) 到不同 GPU，需要時再通訊取得。

"""
ZeRO 三階段記憶體節省分析：

假設：N 張 GPU，模型 Ψ 參數，Adam optimizer

┌─────────────┬──────────────────────┬─────────────────────────┐
│ Stage       │ 每 GPU 記憶體        │ 通訊量 (vs DDP)          │
├─────────────┼──────────────────────┼─────────────────────────┤
│ DDP baseline│ 2Ψ + 2Ψ + 12Ψ = 16Ψ │ 2Ψ (All-Reduce)         │
│ ZeRO-1 (Os)│ 2Ψ + 2Ψ + 12Ψ/N     │ 2Ψ (same as DDP)        │
│ ZeRO-2 (Og)│ 2Ψ + 2Ψ/N + 12Ψ/N   │ 2Ψ (same as DDP)        │
│ ZeRO-3 (Op)│ 16Ψ/N               │ 3Ψ (1.5x DDP)           │
└─────────────┴──────────────────────┴─────────────────────────┘

ZeRO-1: 只分片 Optimizer States → 記憶體省 4x
ZeRO-2: 分片 OS + Gradients → 記憶體省 8x  
ZeRO-3: 分片 OS + Grad + Params → 線性擴展，但通訊增加 50%
"""

# DeepSpeed ZeRO-3 配置範例
deepspeed_config = {
    "zero_optimization": {
        "stage": 3,
        "offload_optimizer": {
            "device": "cpu",       # CPU offload (ZeRO-Infinity)
            "pin_memory": True
        },
        "offload_param": {
            "device": "cpu",       # 參數也 offload 到 CPU
            "pin_memory": True
        },
        "overlap_comm": True,      # 通訊與計算重疊
        "contiguous_gradients": True,
        "sub_group_size": 1e9,
        "reduce_bucket_size": "auto",
        "stage3_prefetch_bucket_size": "auto",
        "stage3_param_persistence_threshold": "auto",
        "stage3_max_live_parameters": 1e9,
        "stage3_max_reuse_distance": 1e9,
    },
    "bf16": {"enabled": True},
    "train_batch_size": "auto",
    "train_micro_batch_size_per_gpu": "auto",
    "gradient_accumulation_steps": "auto"
}

# PyTorch FSDP (Fully Sharded Data Parallel) — PyTorch 原生版 ZeRO-3
from torch.distributed.fsdp import (
    FullyShardedDataParallel as FSDP,
    ShardingStrategy,
    MixedPrecision,
    CPUOffload
)
from torch.distributed.fsdp.wrap import transformer_auto_wrap_policy
from transformers import LlamaDecoderLayer

def setup_fsdp_model(model, rank):
    """配置 FSDP (PyTorch 原生 ZeRO-3)"""
    
    # 自動包裝策略：每個 Transformer layer 獨立 shard
    auto_wrap_policy = transformer_auto_wrap_policy(
        transformer_layer_cls={LlamaDecoderLayer}
    )
    
    # Mixed precision: compute bf16, reduce fp32
    mp_policy = MixedPrecision(
        param_dtype=torch.bfloat16,
        reduce_dtype=torch.float32,
        buffer_dtype=torch.bfloat16
    )
    
    fsdp_model = FSDP(
        model,
        sharding_strategy=ShardingStrategy.FULL_SHARD,  # = ZeRO-3
        # SHARD_GRAD_OP = ZeRO-2
        # NO_SHARD = DDP
        mixed_precision=mp_policy,
        auto_wrap_policy=auto_wrap_policy,
        cpu_offload=CPUOffload(offload_params=False),
        device_id=rank,
        use_orig_params=True,  # 需要用於 torch.compile
    )
    
    return fsdp_model

9.4 Tensor Parallelism (TP) — 切割單一矩陣運算

💡 核心概念

想法：把單一層的矩陣乘法分配到多張 GPU。例如一個 [H, 4H] 的 FFN 權重矩陣，切成 4 份 [H, H] 分別放在 4 張 GPU 上。

要求：極低延遲通訊（NVLink），因為每個 forward/backward step 都需要同步。

適用場景：同一節點（DGX）內的 GPU 間。

"""
Tensor Parallelism 切割方式 (Megatron-LM style):

MLP Layer: Y = GeLU(X @ A) @ B
  Column-parallel: A 按列切割 → 每 GPU 算部分 neurons
  Row-parallel: B 按行切割 → 每 GPU 算部分 output
  
  GPU 0: Y0 = GeLU(X @ A0) @ B0  ─┐
  GPU 1: Y1 = GeLU(X @ A1) @ B1  ─┼─ All-Reduce → Y
  GPU 2: Y2 = GeLU(X @ A2) @ B2  ─┤
  GPU 3: Y3 = GeLU(X @ A3) @ B3  ─┘

Self-Attention:
  Q, K, V matrices: 按 heads 切割（每 GPU 處理部分 attention heads）
  Output projection: Row-parallel

通訊模式：
  Forward: 1 All-Reduce per layer (MLP) + 1 All-Reduce (Attention)
  Backward: 同上
  
  Total per transformer layer: 4 All-Reduce ops
  通訊量: 2 * batch * seq_len * hidden_dim * num_layers
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn

class ColumnParallelLinear(nn.Module):
    """Column-parallel linear layer (Megatron-LM style)
    
    Weight matrix A [H_in, H_out] split along columns:
    GPU_i holds A[:, i*H_out//tp : (i+1)*H_out//tp]
    """
    def __init__(self, in_features: int, out_features: int, 
                 tp_size: int, tp_rank: int):
        super().__init__()
        assert out_features % tp_size == 0
        self.tp_size = tp_size
        self.tp_rank = tp_rank
        self.out_per_partition = out_features // tp_size
        
        # Each GPU only stores its column partition
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(
            torch.empty(self.out_per_partition, in_features)
        )
        self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.out_per_partition))
        
    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        # x: [batch, seq_len, hidden]
        # Local matmul: no communication needed!
        output = torch.nn.functional.linear(x, self.weight, self.bias)
        # output: [batch, seq_len, out_per_partition]
        return output  # All-Reduce will happen after row-parallel


class RowParallelLinear(nn.Module):
    """Row-parallel linear layer
    
    Weight matrix B [H_in, H_out] split along rows:
    GPU_i holds B[i*H_in//tp : (i+1)*H_in//tp, :]
    """
    def __init__(self, in_features: int, out_features: int,
                 tp_size: int, tp_rank: int):
        super().__init__()
        assert in_features % tp_size == 0
        self.in_per_partition = in_features // tp_size
        
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(
            torch.empty(out_features, self.in_per_partition)
        )
        self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features))
        
    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        # x: [batch, seq_len, in_per_partition] (from column-parallel)
        output = torch.nn.functional.linear(x, self.weight)
        # ⚡ All-Reduce: sum partial results across TP group
        dist.all_reduce(output, op=dist.ReduceOp.SUM)
        output = output + self.bias
        return output

9.5 Pipeline Parallelism (PP) — 切割模型層

💡 核心概念

想法：把模型按層切割，不同 GPU 負責不同層。例如 80 層 Transformer，4 張 GPU 各負責 20 層。

問題：naive 實作有 "bubble"（空閒時間），因為 GPU 1 做完才能給 GPU 2。

解法：Micro-batching (GPipe) 或 1F1B scheduling。

"""
Pipeline Parallelism Scheduling:

Naive (Sequential) — 嚴重 bubble:
Time → ─────────────────────────────────────
GPU 0: [F1][F2][F3][F4]                [B4][B3][B2][B1]
GPU 1:     [F1][F2][F3][F4]        [B4][B3][B2][B1]
GPU 2:         [F1][F2][F3][F4][B4][B3][B2][B1]
GPU 3:             [F1][F2][F3][F4][B4][B3][B2][B1]
           ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^ 大量 bubble!

GPipe (Micro-batching) — 減少 bubble:
  把 mini-batch 切成 m 個 micro-batches
  bubble ratio = (pp_size - 1) / m
  m=32, pp=4 → bubble = 3/32 ≈ 9%

1F1B (One Forward One Backward) — 最小化記憶體:
  Steady state: 交替做 1 forward + 1 backward
  記憶體只需存 pp_size 個 micro-batch 的 activations
  
Interleaved 1F1B (Megatron-LM v3):
  每個 GPU 負責多段不連續的 layers
  進一步減少 bubble 到 (pp-1)/(m*chunks)
"""

# Pipeline schedule implementation concept
class PipelineSchedule:
    def __init__(self, num_stages: int, num_microbatches: int,
                 schedule: str = "1f1b"):
        self.num_stages = num_stages
        self.num_microbatches = num_microbatches
        self.schedule = schedule
    
    def get_schedule(self, stage_id: int) -> list:
        """Generate forward/backward schedule for a pipeline stage"""
        if self.schedule == "gpipe":
            return self._gpipe_schedule(stage_id)
        elif self.schedule == "1f1b":
            return self._1f1b_schedule(stage_id)
    
    def _1f1b_schedule(self, stage_id: int) -> list:
        """1F1B: warmup forwards, then steady state 1F+1B, cooldown"""
        schedule = []
        num_warmup = self.num_stages - stage_id - 1
        num_steady = self.num_microbatches - num_warmup
        
        # Warmup: only forwards
        for i in range(num_warmup):
            schedule.append(("forward", i))
        
        # Steady state: 1 forward + 1 backward alternating
        for i in range(num_steady):
            schedule.append(("forward", num_warmup + i))
            schedule.append(("backward", i))
        
        # Cooldown: only backwards
        for i in range(num_warmup):
            schedule.append(("backward", num_steady + i))
        
        return schedule
    
    def compute_bubble_ratio(self) -> float:
        """Bubble ratio = idle time / total time"""
        if self.schedule == "gpipe":
            return (self.num_stages - 1) / self.num_microbatches
        elif self.schedule == "1f1b":
            # Same bubble ratio, but less memory
            return (self.num_stages - 1) / self.num_microbatches

# 實際配置範例：訓練 LLaMA-70B on 64 GPUs (8 nodes × 8 GPUs)
# 3D Parallelism: TP=8 (intra-node), PP=4 (inter-node), DP=2
config_70b = {
    "tensor_parallel": 8,    # 8 GPUs within one DGX node (NVLink)
    "pipeline_parallel": 4,  # 4 pipeline stages across nodes
    "data_parallel": 2,      # 2x data parallel replicas
    "total_gpus": 8 * 4 * 2, # = 64 GPUs
    "micro_batches": 32,     # For pipeline scheduling
    "global_batch_size": 2048,
    "sequence_length": 4096,
    "bubble_ratio": 3/32,    # ≈ 9.4% overhead
}

9.6 Expert Parallelism — Mixture of Experts (MoE)

"""
Mixture of Experts (MoE) Parallelism:

核心想法：不是所有 token 都需要經過所有參數
  - 每個 FFN layer 替換為 N 個 "experts" (獨立的 FFN)
  - Router (gate network) 選擇 top-k experts per token
  - 實際計算量 ≈ dense model 的 1/N * k

GPT-4 (推測): ~1.8T total params, 8 experts, top-2 routing
  Active params per token: ~220B (vs 1.8T total)
  Training: 每個 expert 放在不同 GPU (Expert Parallelism)
  
通訊模式：All-to-All
  Each GPU sends tokens to the GPU holding the selected expert
  Then receives results back → All-to-All pattern
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

class MoELayer(nn.Module):
    """Mixture of Experts layer with top-k routing"""
    
    def __init__(self, hidden_dim: int, expert_dim: int, 
                 num_experts: int, top_k: int = 2,
                 capacity_factor: float = 1.25):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_experts = num_experts
        self.top_k = top_k
        self.capacity_factor = capacity_factor
        
        # Router: learns which expert handles which tokens
        self.gate = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, num_experts, bias=False)
        
        # N independent FFN experts
        self.experts = nn.ModuleList([
            nn.Sequential(
                nn.Linear(hidden_dim, expert_dim),
                nn.GELU(),
                nn.Linear(expert_dim, hidden_dim)
            ) for _ in range(num_experts)
        ])
    
    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Args: x [batch, seq_len, hidden]
        Returns: output [batch, seq_len, hidden]
        """
        batch, seq_len, hidden = x.shape
        x_flat = x.view(-1, hidden)  # [B*S, H]
        
        # Route: compute expert scores
        router_logits = self.gate(x_flat)  # [B*S, num_experts]
        router_probs = F.softmax(router_logits, dim=-1)
        
        # Top-k selection
        top_k_probs, top_k_indices = torch.topk(
            router_probs, self.top_k, dim=-1
        )  # [B*S, top_k]
        
        # Normalize selected probabilities
        top_k_probs = top_k_probs / top_k_probs.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
        
        # Dispatch tokens to experts (simplified, no capacity constraint)
        output = torch.zeros_like(x_flat)
        for k in range(self.top_k):
            for expert_idx in range(self.num_experts):
                mask = (top_k_indices[:, k] == expert_idx)
                if mask.any():
                    expert_input = x_flat[mask]
                    expert_output = self.experts[expert_idx](expert_input)
                    output[mask] += top_k_probs[mask, k:k+1] * expert_output
        
        # Load balancing loss (auxiliary loss)
        # Prevents all tokens routing to same expert
        self.aux_loss = self._load_balance_loss(router_probs, top_k_indices)
        
        return output.view(batch, seq_len, hidden)
    
    def _load_balance_loss(self, probs, indices):
        """Encourage uniform expert utilization"""
        num_tokens = probs.shape[0]
        # Fraction of tokens routed to each expert
        expert_mask = F.one_hot(indices[:, 0], self.num_experts).float()
        tokens_per_expert = expert_mask.sum(0) / num_tokens
        # Average router probability per expert
        router_prob_per_expert = probs.mean(0)
        # Balance loss: dot product should be uniform (1/N)
        return (tokens_per_expert * router_prob_per_expert).sum() * self.num_experts

9.7 NVLink Topology 與通訊優化

"""
GPU Interconnect Hierarchy (DGX H100):

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ DGX H100 Node (8 GPUs)                              │
│                                                     │
│  GPU0 ←─NVLink 900GB/s─→ GPU1                     │
│  GPU2 ←─NVLink 900GB/s─→ GPU3                     │
│  GPU4 ←─NVLink 900GB/s─→ GPU5                     │
│  GPU6 ←─NVLink 900GB/s─→ GPU7                     │
│     ↕ NVSwitch (all-to-all within node)            │
│                                                     │
│  Total bisection BW: 900 GB/s per GPU              │
└─────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘
                      │ InfiniBand NDR 400 Gb/s (50 GB/s)
                      │ (18x slower than NVLink!)
┌─────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────┐
│ Another DGX H100 Node                               │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

最佳實踐 Parallelism Mapping:
  - Tensor Parallel: WITHIN node (needs NVLink bandwidth)
  - Pipeline Parallel: ACROSS nodes (point-to-point, latency-tolerant)
  - Data Parallel: ACROSS nodes (All-Reduce, bandwidth-bound)
  - Expert Parallel: Depends on expert count vs GPU count
"""

class TrainingTopologyPlanner:
    """Plan 3D parallelism configuration based on hardware"""
    
    def __init__(self, model_params_b: float, num_nodes: int,
                 gpus_per_node: int = 8, gpu_memory_gb: int = 80):
        self.model_params_b = model_params_b
        self.num_nodes = num_nodes
        self.gpus_per_node = gpus_per_node
        self.gpu_memory_gb = gpu_memory_gb
        self.total_gpus = num_nodes * gpus_per_node
    
    def plan(self) -> dict:
        """Determine optimal TP × PP × DP configuration"""
        mem_per_gpu_needed = self.model_params_b * 16  # 16 bytes/param (adam)
        
        # Rule 1: TP should fit within one node (NVLink)
        min_tp = 1
        while (mem_per_gpu_needed / min_tp) > self.gpu_memory_gb * 0.8:
            min_tp *= 2
            if min_tp > self.gpus_per_node:
                break
        
        tp = min(min_tp, self.gpus_per_node)
        
        # Rule 2: PP for remaining memory pressure
        remaining_gpus = self.total_gpus // tp
        # Heuristic: use PP if model > 30B params
        if self.model_params_b > 30:
            pp = min(8, remaining_gpus)
        else:
            pp = 1
        
        # Rule 3: DP fills remaining
        dp = self.total_gpus // (tp * pp)
        
        return {
            "tensor_parallel": tp,
            "pipeline_parallel": pp,  
            "data_parallel": dp,
            "total_gpus_used": tp * pp * dp,
            "memory_per_gpu_gb": mem_per_gpu_needed / (tp * pp),
            "recommendation": (
                f"TP={tp} (intra-node NVLink), "
                f"PP={pp} (inter-node), "
                f"DP={dp} (gradient sync)"
            )
        }

# Example configurations
planner_7b = TrainingTopologyPlanner(7, num_nodes=1)
print(planner_7b.plan())
# {'tensor_parallel': 2, 'pipeline_parallel': 1, 'data_parallel': 4, ...}

planner_70b = TrainingTopologyPlanner(70, num_nodes=8)
print(planner_70b.plan())
# {'tensor_parallel': 8, 'pipeline_parallel': 4, 'data_parallel': 2, ...}

planner_405b = TrainingTopologyPlanner(405, num_nodes=64)
print(planner_405b.plan())
# {'tensor_parallel': 8, 'pipeline_parallel': 8, 'data_parallel': 8, ...}

9.8 實戰：完整訓練系統設計

"""
面試題：Design a system to train a 70B parameter LLM from scratch

Answer framework:

1. Hardware Planning:
   - 512 H100 GPUs (64 DGX nodes × 8 GPUs)
   - InfiniBand NDR 400Gbps inter-node
   - Training time estimate: ~30 days for 2T tokens
   
2. Parallelism Strategy (3D + ZeRO):
   - TP=8 within node (NVLink 900 GB/s)
   - PP=4 across nodes (1F1B, 32 micro-batches, 9% bubble)
   - DP=16 with ZeRO-1 (shard optimizer states)
   
3. Training Recipe:
   - Optimizer: AdamW (β1=0.9, β2=0.95, ε=1e-8)
   - Learning rate: cosine decay, peak 3e-4, warmup 2000 steps
   - Batch size: ramp from 512 → 4096 sequences
   - Sequence length: 4096 tokens
   - Precision: BF16 compute, FP32 master weights
   
4. Data Pipeline:
   - Pre-tokenized data in memory-mapped files
   - Streaming from distributed storage (no epoch concept)
   - Data mixing: web (60%), code (20%), books (10%), scientific (10%)
   
5. Fault Tolerance:
   - Checkpoint every 1000 steps (~2 hours)
   - Async checkpointing (don't block training)
   - Elastic training: handle node failures gracefully
   - Gradient overflow detection + loss spike monitoring
   
6. Monitoring & Debugging:
   - Loss curves, gradient norms, learning rate
   - Token throughput (MFU: Model FLOPs Utilization)
   - Hardware health: GPU temp, memory, NVLink errors
   - Target MFU: >50% (good), >40% (acceptable)
"""

def compute_mfu(model_params_b: float, tokens_per_sec: float,
                num_gpus: int, gpu_peak_tflops: float = 989) -> float:
    """Compute Model FLOPs Utilization (MFU)
    
    MFU = actual_flops / theoretical_peak_flops
    
    For transformers: ~6 * params * tokens FLOPs per forward+backward
    """
    # 6N FLOPs per token (forward + backward)
    actual_flops = 6 * model_params_b * 1e9 * tokens_per_sec
    peak_flops = num_gpus * gpu_peak_tflops * 1e12  # H100 BF16 peak
    
    mfu = actual_flops / peak_flops
    return mfu

# LLaMA-3 70B training (Meta reported):
# 2048 H100s, ~3800 tokens/sec/GPU
mfu = compute_mfu(
    model_params_b=70,
    tokens_per_sec=3800 * 2048,  
    num_gpus=2048,
    gpu_peak_tflops=989  # H100 BF16
)
print(f"LLaMA-3 70B MFU: {mfu:.1%}")  # ~38-42%

📖 術語表

術語英文白話解釋生活比喻
Data ParallelismDP每張 GPU 存完整模型，處理不同資料每個廚師有完整食譜，各做不同桌的菜
Tensor ParallelismTP把單一矩陣運算切割分配到多 GPU一道菜切成四份，四人同時切菜
Pipeline ParallelismPP模型按層切割，不同 GPU 負責不同層流水線：A洗菜→B切菜→C炒菜→D裝盤
ZeROZero Redundancy Optimizer把 optimizer/gradient/param 分片存儲圖書館分館制：每館只存 1/N 的書
FSDPFully Sharded Data ParallelPyTorch 原生版 ZeRO-3PyTorch 官方的「分片存儲」方案
All-Reduce—所有 GPU 交換梯度並求和全班傳紙條匯總答案
NVLink—NVIDIA GPU 間高速互連 (900 GB/s)同一棟大樓的電梯（超快）
InfiniBandIB NDR跨節點網路 (50 GB/s)不同城市間的高鐵（比電梯慢）
MoEMixture of Experts每 token 只用部分 experts 計算醫院分科：感冒看內科、骨折看外科
MFUModel FLOPs Utilization實際算力 / 理論峰值的比率高速公路實際車速 / 速限的比值
BubblePipeline BubblePipeline 中 GPU 閒置等待的時間流水線上某一站在等上一站做完
Activation CheckpointingGradient Checkpointing不存中間激活值，需要時重算以省記憶體不拍照記錄，需要時重走一遍路

🧪 驗證考題

Q1: 為什麼 Tensor Parallelism 只適合放在同一節點的 GPU 之間？

答案

因為 TP 需要在每個 Transformer layer 做 All-Reduce（forward + backward 各 2 次），通訊頻率極高。NVLink 提供 900 GB/s 頻寬，但 InfiniBand 只有 50 GB/s（慢 18 倍）。如果 TP 跨節點，通訊延遲會成為嚴重瓶頸，GPU 大部分時間在等資料而不是計算。

Q2: ZeRO-3 相比 ZeRO-2 的 trade-off 是什麼？

答案

ZeRO-3 把 parameters 也分片（除了 optimizer states 和 gradients），記憶體可以線性擴展（16Ψ/N），但通訊量從 2Ψ 增加到 3Ψ（多了 50%）。因為 forward 時需要 All-Gather 收集參數，backward 時也需要。適合模型極大但通訊頻寬充足的場景。

Q3: 解釋 1F1B pipeline schedule 相比 GPipe 的優勢。

答案

Bubble ratio 相同（(pp-1)/m），但 1F1B 的記憶體用量大幅降低。GPipe 需要同時存所有 micro-batch 的 activations（m 份），而 1F1B 只需存 pp_size 份（因為 steady state 階段立即做 backward 釋放記憶體）。例如 pp=4, m=32：GPipe 存 32 份 activations，1F1B 只存 4 份。

Q4: MoE 模型的 load balancing loss 是解決什麼問題？

答案

防止 "expert collapse"：如果沒有額外約束，router 傾向把所有 token 都路由到同一個 expert（因為那個 expert 被訓練得更好，形成 positive feedback loop）。Load balancing auxiliary loss 強制 token 均勻分佈到所有 experts，確保每個 expert 都能學到東西，同時也避免 GPU utilization 不均（一個 GPU 滿載，其他閒置）。

Q5: 你有 128 張 H100 GPU（16 nodes × 8），要訓練 405B 模型，如何選擇 3D parallelism 配置？

答案

405B × 16 bytes/param = 6480 GB states。
TP=8（intra-node，用 NVLink）→ 每個 TP group 處理完整 layer
PP=8（inter-node）→ 6480/8/8 = ~101 GB per GPU（加上 activations 剛好 fit 80GB with checkpointing）
DP=2（128/(8×8)=2）→ 用 ZeRO-1 分片 optimizer
Micro-batches: 64（bubble = 7/64 ≈ 11%）
Sequence length: 8192, global batch: 1024 sequences
如果記憶體還不夠：啟用 activation checkpointing（重算 activations 換記憶體）。

🎯 Follow-up Q&A

F1: Activation Checkpointing 如何影響訓練速度？

Activation Checkpointing 不存中間 activations，backward 時重新計算。增加約 33% 計算量（多一次 forward），但記憶體從 O(N) 降到 O(√N)（N=layers）。實際影響：訓練速度慢 20-30%，但可以用省下的記憶體增加 batch size，部分抵消速度損失。Selective checkpointing（只 checkpoint attention layers，不 checkpoint FFN）可以在速度和記憶體間取得更好平衡。

F2: 如何處理訓練中的 loss spike？

Loss spike 常見原因：(1) 壞資料（重複/garbled text）→ 資料品質過濾；(2) 學習率太高 → cosine decay + warmup；(3) gradient overflow (fp16) → 用 bf16 或 loss scaling；(4) 硬體故障（GPU memory bit flip）→ ECC 檢查。處理：偵測到 spike 時回滾到上一個 checkpoint，跳過該 batch 的資料。Meta 在 LLaMA-3 訓練中遇到 ~200 次 spike，每次回滾 + 調低 LR 恢復。

F3: Sequence Parallelism 是什麼？跟 TP 的關係？

Sequence Parallelism (SP) 把 LayerNorm 和 Dropout 的 activations 也沿 sequence 維度分片（這些不在 TP 的 column/row split 範圍內）。在 Megatron-LM 中，TP 切割 attention/FFN 的權重，但 LayerNorm 的 input/output 仍是完整 sequence → SP 把這些也切開。好處：進一步省 activation 記憶體（與 TP degree 成正比）。SP 和 TP 總是一起使用（相同 group size）。

F4: 為什麼用 BF16 而不是 FP16 訓練 LLM？

BF16 有 8-bit exponent（同 FP32），dynamic range 夠大，不需要 loss scaling。FP16 只有 5-bit exponent，容易 overflow/underflow，需要複雜的 dynamic loss scaling（AMP）。代價：BF16 mantissa 只有 7 bits（FP16 有 10 bits），精度稍低，但對 LLM 訓練影響可忽略。Google TPU 原生支援 BF16，NVIDIA 從 A100 開始支援。

F5: 如何設計 checkpoint 系統以最小化訓練中斷？

(1) Async checkpointing：在背景把 model states 寫入 storage，不阻塞訓練；(2) Incremental checkpoints：只存 delta（optimizer momentum/variance 變化不大）；(3) Distributed checkpointing：每個 rank 存自己的 shard，並行寫入（PyTorch DCP）；(4) 頻率策略：每 1000 steps + 偵測到 loss spike 時立即存；(5) Storage：先寫 local NVMe SSD（快），再 async 複製到 S3/GCS（持久）。LLaMA-3：checkpoint 需要 ~1 TB，寫入 ~2 分鐘（NVMe parallel write）。

F6: 解釋 Gradient Accumulation 的作用和配置。

當 GPU 記憶體不夠跑大 batch 時，用多個小 micro-batch 累積梯度再做一次 optimizer step。等效 batch size = micro_batch × accumulation_steps × DP_size。例如：micro_batch=2, acc_steps=16, DP=64 → global_batch=2048。注意：accumulation 期間不做 All-Reduce（只在最後一步同步），節省通訊。配合 PP 的 micro-batching 一起使用時，acc_steps 就是 micro-batch 數量。

Chapter 10: Fine-tuning — 大型語言模型微調技術

🌱 從零開始理解

生活比喻：預訓練像是讓一個人讀完所有百科全書（通識教育），Fine-tuning 像是讓他去讀醫學院（專業訓練）。LoRA 更像是「只在白大褂口袋裡放一張速查卡」— 不改變整個人的知識，只加一層薄薄的專業覆蓋。

白話解釋：Fine-tuning 是把預訓練好的大模型「調教」成能做特定任務的專家。但完整微調 70B 模型需要跟預訓練差不多的 GPU 資源，所以發明了 LoRA/QLoRA 等參數高效方法 — 只訓練 0.1% 的參數就能達到 95%+ 的效果。

為什麼工作上需要：面試會問「如何用最少資源讓 LLM 適應特定任務？」你需要能比較 full fine-tuning、LoRA、QLoRA、Adapters、prefix tuning 的 trade-offs，並能設計 RLHF pipeline。

先備知識：Transformer 架構、矩陣分解（SVD）、強化學習基礎（reward model）、量化概念。

10.1 Full Fine-tuning vs Parameter-Efficient Fine-tuning (PEFT)

💡 核心概念

Full Fine-tuning：更新所有參數，效果最好但資源需求 = 預訓練等級。

PEFT：凍結大部分參數，只訓練新加的少量參數或特定子集。

為什麼 PEFT 可行？因為大模型的 weight updates 在 fine-tuning 時具有低秩結構 (low-rank)：ΔW 矩陣的有效秩遠小於其維度。

"""
PEFT 方法全景比較：

┌──────────────────┬──────────────┬──────────────┬────────────────┐
│ Method           │ 可訓練參數    │ 推論延遲     │ 多任務切換      │
├──────────────────┼──────────────┼──────────────┼────────────────┤
│ Full Fine-tune   │ 100%         │ 0 overhead   │ ❌ 每任務存全模型 │
│ LoRA             │ 0.1-1%       │ 0 (merged)   │ ✅ 換 adapter    │
│ QLoRA            │ 0.1-1%       │ 微量 (quant) │ ✅ 換 adapter    │
│ Prefix Tuning    │ 0.01%        │ 有 (extra KV)│ ✅ 換 prefix     │
│ Adapters         │ 1-5%         │ 有 (extra FF)│ ✅ 換 adapter    │
│ BitFit           │ 0.05% (bias) │ 0            │ ✅ 換 bias       │
│ IA³              │ 0.01%        │ 0 (merged)   │ ✅ 換 vectors    │
└──────────────────┴──────────────┴──────────────┴────────────────┘
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Optional

# ============================================
# FULL FINE-TUNING BASELINE
# ============================================
def full_finetune_memory(params_b: float) -> dict:
    """Full fine-tuning 記憶體需求 = 預訓練等級"""
    return {
        "params_gb": params_b * 2,       # fp16
        "gradients_gb": params_b * 2,    # fp16
        "optimizer_gb": params_b * 12,   # Adam fp32: weights + m + v
        "total_gb": params_b * 16,
        "gpus_a100_80gb": int((params_b * 16) / 80) + 1
    }

# LLaMA-70B full fine-tune:
print(full_finetune_memory(70))
# total_gb: 1120 GB → 14+ A100 GPUs
# 這就是為什麼需要 PEFT！

10.2 LoRA (Low-Rank Adaptation) — 核心演算法

💡 核心概念

LoRA 的關鍵洞察：fine-tuning 時的權重更新 ΔW 具有低秩結構。

原始：W' = W + ΔW，其中 W ∈ ℝd×k

LoRA：W' = W + BA，其中 B ∈ ℝd×r, A ∈ ℝr×k, r ≪ min(d,k)

參數量：從 d×k 降到 (d+k)×r。例如 d=k=4096, r=16 → 從 16M 降到 131K（減少 99.2%）

class LoRALinear(nn.Module):
    """LoRA: Low-Rank Adaptation of Large Language Models
    
    核心數學：
      h = W₀x + ΔWx = W₀x + BAx
      
      W₀: 凍結的原始權重 [out_features, in_features]
      B: LoRA down-projection [out_features, rank] — init zeros
      A: LoRA up-projection [rank, in_features] — init random
      
      Scaling: ΔW = (α/r) * BA
      α: scaling factor (通常 = 2*rank)
    """
    
    def __init__(self, original_linear: nn.Linear, rank: int = 16, 
                 alpha: float = 32, dropout: float = 0.05):
        super().__init__()
        self.original = original_linear
        self.original.weight.requires_grad = False  # 凍結原始權重
        if self.original.bias is not None:
            self.original.bias.requires_grad = False
        
        in_features = original_linear.in_features
        out_features = original_linear.out_features
        self.rank = rank
        self.scaling = alpha / rank  # LoRA scaling factor
        
        # LoRA matrices
        self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(
            torch.randn(rank, in_features) * (1 / rank**0.5)
        )  # Kaiming-like init
        self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(
            torch.zeros(out_features, rank)
        )  # Zero init → ΔW=0 at start (training starts from pretrained)
        
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
    
    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        # Original frozen computation
        base_output = self.original(x)
        
        # LoRA delta: (α/r) * B @ A @ x
        lora_output = (
            self.dropout(x) @ self.lora_A.T @ self.lora_B.T * self.scaling
        )
        
        return base_output + lora_output
    
    def merge_weights(self) -> nn.Linear:
        """推論時合併 LoRA 到原始權重 → 零延遲開銷"""
        merged = nn.Linear(
            self.original.in_features, 
            self.original.out_features,
            bias=self.original.bias is not None
        )
        # W' = W₀ + (α/r) * B @ A
        merged.weight.data = (
            self.original.weight.data + 
            self.scaling * (self.lora_B @ self.lora_A)
        )
        if self.original.bias is not None:
            merged.bias.data = self.original.bias.data
        return merged


def apply_lora_to_model(model, rank: int = 16, alpha: float = 32,
                        target_modules: list = None):
    """Apply LoRA to specific modules in a pretrained model
    
    Common targets: q_proj, k_proj, v_proj, o_proj, gate_proj, up_proj, down_proj
    """
    if target_modules is None:
        target_modules = ["q_proj", "v_proj"]  # 最小配置
    
    lora_params = 0
    frozen_params = 0
    
    for name, module in model.named_modules():
        if any(target in name for target in target_modules):
            if isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
                # Replace with LoRA version
                parent_name = name.rsplit(".", 1)[0] if "." in name else ""
                child_name = name.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] if "." in name else name
                parent = model.get_submodule(parent_name) if parent_name else model
                
                lora_linear = LoRALinear(module, rank=rank, alpha=alpha)
                setattr(parent, child_name, lora_linear)
                lora_params += rank * (module.in_features + module.out_features)
    
    # Freeze everything else
    for param in model.parameters():
        if param.requires_grad:
            frozen_params += param.numel()
    
    total = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())
    trainable = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
    print(f"Trainable: {trainable:,} / {total:,} = {trainable/total:.4%}")
    
    return model

# 典型配置：LLaMA-70B + LoRA r=64
# Trainable: ~160M / 70B = 0.23%
# 記憶體：70B×2 (frozen fp16) + 160M×16 (LoRA Adam) ≈ 142 GB
# → 2 張 A100 80GB 即可！(vs full fine-tune 需要 14+)

10.3 QLoRA — 量化 + LoRA

"""
QLoRA: Quantized LoRA — 在 4-bit 量化模型上做 LoRA

核心創新：
1. NF4 (Normal Float 4-bit): 基於權重分佈設計的 4-bit 資料型態
2. Double Quantization: 量化 quantization constants 本身（再省 0.5 bit/param）
3. Paged Optimizers: 用 CUDA unified memory 處理 OOM

結果：在單張 48GB GPU 上 fine-tune 65B 模型！

記憶體比較 (70B model):
  Full fine-tune: 1120 GB (14 A100s)
  LoRA fp16:       142 GB (2 A100s)  
  QLoRA 4-bit:     ~38 GB (1 A100 48GB!) ← 35x reduction!
"""

# QLoRA 配置 (使用 bitsandbytes + peft)
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, BitsAndBytesConfig
from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model, prepare_model_for_kbit_training

def setup_qlora(model_name: str, rank: int = 64) -> tuple:
    """Setup QLoRA: 4-bit quantized base + LoRA adapters"""
    
    # Step 1: Load model in 4-bit (NF4 quantization)
    bnb_config = BitsAndBytesConfig(
        load_in_4bit=True,
        bnb_4bit_quant_type="nf4",          # Normal Float 4-bit
        bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.bfloat16,  # Compute in bf16
        bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,      # Double quantization
    )
    
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
        model_name,
        quantization_config=bnb_config,
        device_map="auto",  # Auto shard across available GPUs
        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,
    )
    
    # Step 2: Prepare for k-bit training (handle grad checkpointing)
    model = prepare_model_for_kbit_training(model)
    
    # Step 3: Apply LoRA adapters
    lora_config = LoraConfig(
        r=rank,                    # LoRA rank
        lora_alpha=rank * 2,       # Scaling factor (常見: alpha = 2*r)
        target_modules=[           # Which layers to adapt
            "q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj",
            "gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"
        ],
        lora_dropout=0.05,
        bias="none",               # Don't train biases
        task_type="CAUSAL_LM"
    )
    
    model = get_peft_model(model, lora_config)
    model.print_trainable_parameters()
    # trainable params: 167M || all params: 70B || trainable%: 0.24%
    
    return model, lora_config


# LoRA Rank 選擇指南
"""
┌─────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Rank    │ 適用場景                                   │
├─────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ r=4-8   │ 簡單任務 (分類, sentiment)                 │
│ r=16-32 │ 中等任務 (QA, summarization)               │
│ r=64    │ 複雜任務 (code gen, multilingual)           │
│ r=128+  │ 接近 full fine-tune 效果，較少用            │
└─────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────┘

經驗法則：
- 任務越「遠離」預訓練分佈 → rank 越高
- target_modules 越多 → 每個 module 的 rank 可以小一點
- alpha/rank ratio: 通常 1-4，越大 LoRA 影響越大
"""

10.4 其他 PEFT 方法

class PrefixTuning(nn.Module):
    """Prefix Tuning: 在每層 attention 前加可學習的 prefix tokens
    
    想法：不改模型權重，而是學習一組 "virtual tokens" 的 KV pairs
    插入每層 attention 的 key/value 中
    
    參數量：num_layers × 2(K,V) × prefix_len × hidden_dim
    """
    def __init__(self, num_layers: int, hidden_dim: int, 
                 num_heads: int, prefix_length: int = 20):
        super().__init__()
        self.prefix_length = prefix_length
        
        # Learnable prefix embeddings (reparameterized through MLP for stability)
        self.prefix_embedding = nn.Embedding(prefix_length, hidden_dim)
        self.prefix_mlp = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, hidden_dim * 4),
            nn.Tanh(),
            nn.Linear(hidden_dim * 4, num_layers * 2 * hidden_dim)
        )
        # num_layers * 2: key + value for each layer
    
    def get_prefix(self, batch_size: int) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Generate prefix KV pairs for all layers"""
        prefix_tokens = torch.arange(self.prefix_length).unsqueeze(0)
        prefix_tokens = prefix_tokens.expand(batch_size, -1)
        
        embeddings = self.prefix_embedding(prefix_tokens)
        past_key_values = self.prefix_mlp(embeddings)
        # Reshape to [num_layers, 2, batch, num_heads, prefix_len, head_dim]
        return past_key_values


class AdapterLayer(nn.Module):
    """Adapter Tuning: 在每個 Transformer sublayer 後插入小型 FFN
    
    Architecture: LayerNorm → Down-project → Activation → Up-project → Residual
    
    Bottleneck dim 控制參數量：
      2 × hidden_dim × bottleneck + bottleneck
    """
    def __init__(self, hidden_dim: int, bottleneck_dim: int = 64):
        super().__init__()
        self.adapter = nn.Sequential(
            nn.LayerNorm(hidden_dim),
            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, bottleneck_dim),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(bottleneck_dim, hidden_dim),
        )
        # Initialize near-zero for stable training start
        nn.init.zeros_(self.adapter[-1].weight)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.adapter[-1].bias)
    
    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return x + self.adapter(x)  # Residual connection


class IA3Layer(nn.Module):
    """IA³ (Infused Adapter by Inhibiting and Amplifying Inner Activations)
    
    極簡方法：只學 3 個 rescaling vectors (lk, lv, lff)
    分別 element-wise 乘到 keys, values, FFN intermediate
    
    參數量：3 × hidden_dim per layer (最少的 PEFT 方法之一)
    """
    def __init__(self, hidden_dim: int, ffn_dim: int):
        super().__init__()
        self.l_k = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(hidden_dim))   # Key rescaling
        self.l_v = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(hidden_dim))   # Value rescaling
        self.l_ff = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(ffn_dim))     # FFN rescaling
    
    def rescale_key(self, k: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return k * self.l_k
    
    def rescale_value(self, v: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return v * self.l_v
    
    def rescale_ffn(self, ff: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return ff * self.l_ff

10.5 RLHF (Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback)

💡 核心概念

RLHF 三階段：

  1. SFT (Supervised Fine-Tuning): 用人類示範對話微調 base model
  2. Reward Model Training: 用人類偏好排序訓練打分模型
  3. PPO/DPO Optimization: 用 RL 最大化 reward 同時不偏離 SFT 太遠
"""
RLHF Pipeline Architecture:

Stage 1: SFT
  Base Model (LLaMA-70B) + Instruction Data → SFT Model
  Data: ~100K high-quality (instruction, response) pairs
  
Stage 2: Reward Model  
  SFT Model → Reward Model (same size or smaller)
  Data: (prompt, response_A, response_B, preference) 
  Loss: -log(σ(r(preferred) - r(rejected)))  [Bradley-Terry]
  
Stage 3: PPO
  Maximize: E[RM(prompt, response)] - β * KL(π || π_SFT)
  
  4 models in memory simultaneously:
    - Policy (being trained)
    - Reference (frozen SFT, for KL)
    - Reward Model (frozen, for scoring)
    - Value Model (critic, for advantage estimation)
  → 極高記憶體需求！
"""

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

class RewardModel(nn.Module):
    """Reward Model: 把 LLM 改成 scalar output"""
    
    def __init__(self, base_model):
        super().__init__()
        self.base_model = base_model  # Pretrained LLM backbone
        hidden_dim = base_model.config.hidden_size
        
        # Replace LM head with reward head
        self.reward_head = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 1, bias=False)
    
    def forward(self, input_ids, attention_mask) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Return scalar reward for each sequence"""
        outputs = self.base_model(
            input_ids=input_ids,
            attention_mask=attention_mask,
            output_hidden_states=True
        )
        # Use last token's hidden state as sequence representation
        last_hidden = outputs.hidden_states[-1]
        # Find last non-padding token
        seq_lengths = attention_mask.sum(dim=1) - 1
        last_token_hidden = last_hidden[
            torch.arange(last_hidden.size(0)), seq_lengths
        ]
        reward = self.reward_head(last_token_hidden).squeeze(-1)
        return reward


def reward_model_loss(rewards_chosen: torch.Tensor, 
                      rewards_rejected: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Bradley-Terry pairwise ranking loss
    
    Given (prompt, chosen_response, rejected_response):
    Loss = -log(σ(r_chosen - r_rejected))
    
    Intuition: reward of preferred response should be higher
    """
    return -F.logsigmoid(rewards_chosen - rewards_rejected).mean()


# PPO Training Loop (simplified)
def ppo_step(policy_model, ref_model, reward_model, value_model,
             prompts, old_log_probs, advantages, 
             clip_ratio: float = 0.2, kl_coeff: float = 0.1):
    """Single PPO update step for RLHF
    
    PPO objective:
      L = min(r(θ) * A, clip(r(θ), 1-ε, 1+ε) * A) - β * KL(π || π_ref)
      
      r(θ) = π_new(a|s) / π_old(a|s)  [probability ratio]
      A = advantage (from GAE using value model)
    """
    # Generate responses from current policy
    responses = policy_model.generate(prompts, max_new_tokens=256)
    
    # Compute rewards
    rewards = reward_model(prompts + responses)
    
    # Compute new log probs
    new_log_probs = policy_model.get_log_probs(prompts, responses)
    
    # Policy ratio
    ratio = torch.exp(new_log_probs - old_log_probs)
    
    # Clipped surrogate objective
    surr1 = ratio * advantages
    surr2 = torch.clamp(ratio, 1 - clip_ratio, 1 + clip_ratio) * advantages
    policy_loss = -torch.min(surr1, surr2).mean()
    
    # KL penalty against reference model
    ref_log_probs = ref_model.get_log_probs(prompts, responses)
    kl_div = (new_log_probs - ref_log_probs).mean()
    
    # Total loss
    total_loss = policy_loss + kl_coeff * kl_div
    
    return total_loss, {
        "reward_mean": rewards.mean().item(),
        "kl_div": kl_div.item(),
        "clip_fraction": ((ratio - 1).abs() > clip_ratio).float().mean().item()
    }

10.6 DPO (Direct Preference Optimization) — 取代 PPO

"""
DPO: 跳過 reward model，直接從偏好資料優化 policy

核心洞察：PPO 的 optimal policy 可以用 closed-form 表示：
  π*(y|x) ∝ π_ref(y|x) · exp(r(x,y) / β)

反推出 implicit reward:
  r(x,y) = β · log(π(y|x) / π_ref(y|x)) + const

代入 Bradley-Terry loss → DPO loss:
  L_DPO = -log σ(β · [log π(y_w|x)/π_ref(y_w|x) - log π(y_l|x)/π_ref(y_l|x)])

優勢：
  ✅ 不需要訓練 reward model（省一半記憶體）
  ✅ 不需要 PPO（穩定，不需要 value model）
  ✅ 只需要 2 個模型：policy + frozen reference
  ✅ 訓練就像 supervised fine-tuning 一樣簡單

劣勢：
  ❌ 不能用 reward model 做 rejection sampling
  ❌ 對 out-of-distribution 偏好泛化較差
  ❌ 無法 online 更新偏好（offline only）
"""

def dpo_loss(policy_model, ref_model, 
             chosen_ids, rejected_ids, 
             prompt_ids, beta: float = 0.1) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Direct Preference Optimization loss
    
    L = -log σ(β * (log π(y_w|x)/π_ref(y_w|x) - log π(y_l|x)/π_ref(y_l|x)))
    
    Args:
        chosen_ids: tokenized preferred response
        rejected_ids: tokenized rejected response  
        prompt_ids: tokenized prompt (shared prefix)
        beta: temperature (higher = more conservative)
    """
    # Log probs from policy model
    policy_chosen_logp = get_sequence_log_probs(policy_model, prompt_ids, chosen_ids)
    policy_rejected_logp = get_sequence_log_probs(policy_model, prompt_ids, rejected_ids)
    
    # Log probs from reference model (frozen, no grad)
    with torch.no_grad():
        ref_chosen_logp = get_sequence_log_probs(ref_model, prompt_ids, chosen_ids)
        ref_rejected_logp = get_sequence_log_probs(ref_model, prompt_ids, rejected_ids)
    
    # DPO implicit reward difference
    chosen_reward = beta * (policy_chosen_logp - ref_chosen_logp)
    rejected_reward = beta * (policy_rejected_logp - ref_rejected_logp)
    
    # Bradley-Terry loss on implicit rewards
    loss = -F.logsigmoid(chosen_reward - rejected_reward).mean()
    
    # Metrics
    reward_margin = (chosen_reward - rejected_reward).mean().item()
    accuracy = (chosen_reward > rejected_reward).float().mean().item()
    
    return loss, {"reward_margin": reward_margin, "accuracy": accuracy}


def get_sequence_log_probs(model, prompt_ids, response_ids):
    """Compute log P(response | prompt) for a model"""
    full_ids = torch.cat([prompt_ids, response_ids], dim=1)
    outputs = model(full_ids)
    logits = outputs.logits  # [batch, seq_len, vocab]
    
    # Only compute log probs for response tokens
    prompt_len = prompt_ids.shape[1]
    response_logits = logits[:, prompt_len-1:-1, :]  # Shift by 1
    
    log_probs = F.log_softmax(response_logits, dim=-1)
    token_log_probs = log_probs.gather(2, response_ids.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)
    
    # Sum (or mean) over response length
    return token_log_probs.sum(dim=1)


# DPO Variants (2024-2025):
"""
┌─────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Variant     │ 改進                                          │
├─────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ IPO         │ 正則化 DPO，防止 reward hacking              │
│ KTO         │ 不需要 pairwise data，只需 good/bad labels   │
│ ORPO        │ 結合 SFT + preference 到單一 loss            │
│ SimPO       │ 用 length-normalized log probs，不需 ref     │
│ Online DPO  │ 用 current policy 生成 rejected (iterative)  │
└─────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────┘
"""

10.7 完整 Fine-tuning Pipeline 設計

"""
面試題：Design a fine-tuning pipeline for a 70B LLM to become a coding assistant

Answer framework:

1. Data Pipeline:
   - Source: code repositories + StackOverflow + documentation
   - Format: instruction-following (prompt → response)
   - Quality filtering: dedup, toxicity, correctness verification
   - Size: ~100K high-quality pairs (SFT), ~50K preference pairs (DPO)

2. Training Strategy:
   Phase 1 - SFT (1-3 epochs):
     - QLoRA: 4-bit base, r=64, target all projections
     - Hardware: 4× A100 80GB (FSDP QLoRA)
     - LR: 2e-4, cosine decay, warmup 100 steps
     - Max seq len: 8192
     
   Phase 2 - DPO (1 epoch):
     - Same QLoRA config on SFT model
     - β=0.1, LR 5e-5
     - Data: (prompt, good_code, bad_code) triples
     
3. Evaluation:
   - HumanEval (pass@1, pass@10)
   - MBPP (code correctness)
   - Custom eval: unit test pass rate on internal codebase
   
4. Serving:
   - Merge LoRA weights into base model
   - Quantize to INT8 or INT4 for deployment
   - Serve with vLLM (PagedAttention)
   
5. Iteration:
   - A/B test against baseline
   - Collect user feedback → update DPO data
   - Retrain monthly with fresh data
"""

@dataclass
class FinetuneConfig:
    """Production fine-tuning configuration"""
    # Model
    base_model: str = "meta-llama/Llama-3-70B"
    
    # LoRA
    lora_rank: int = 64
    lora_alpha: int = 128
    lora_dropout: float = 0.05
    target_modules: list = None  # Default: all projections
    
    # Quantization
    use_qlora: bool = True
    quant_type: str = "nf4"
    
    # Training
    num_epochs: int = 3
    learning_rate: float = 2e-4
    lr_scheduler: str = "cosine"
    warmup_steps: int = 100
    per_device_batch_size: int = 2
    gradient_accumulation_steps: int = 8
    max_seq_length: int = 8192
    
    # Optimization
    gradient_checkpointing: bool = True
    bf16: bool = True
    
    # DPO (Phase 2)
    dpo_beta: float = 0.1
    dpo_lr: float = 5e-5
    dpo_epochs: int = 1
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        if self.target_modules is None:
            self.target_modules = [
                "q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj",
                "gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"
            ]
    
    def effective_batch_size(self, num_gpus: int) -> int:
        return (self.per_device_batch_size * 
                self.gradient_accumulation_steps * num_gpus)

📖 術語表

術語英文白話解釋生活比喻
LoRALow-Rank Adaptation用低秩矩陣 BA 近似權重更新 ΔW不重寫整本教科書，只加一張薄薄的補充講義
QLoRAQuantized LoRA在 4-bit 量化模型上做 LoRA把教科書壓縮成口袋書，補充講義不變
