Rank (r)LoRA Rank低秩分解的維度，控制 adapter 容量補充講義的頁數——越多頁能記越多
RLHFRL from Human Feedback用人類偏好訓練 reward model 再用 RL 優化學徒做菜→評審打分→依分數調整手法
DPODirect Preference Optimization直接從偏好資料優化，跳過 reward model直接比較兩道菜哪個好，不需要分數
SFTSupervised Fine-Tuning用示範資料做有監督微調師傅做一遍讓學徒照著練
PPOProximal Policy OptimizationRL 演算法，限制每步更新幅度每次調整不超過一小步，避免跑偏
KL Divergence—衡量 fine-tuned model 偏離原始多遠學徒風格偏離師傅多遠的尺
NF4Normal Float 4-bit為正態分佈權重設計的 4-bit 格式專為鐘形分佈設計的壓縮編碼
Prefix Tuning—在 attention 前加可學習的虛擬 tokens在每段對話前加一段隱形提示詞
Adapter—在 Transformer 層間插入小型可訓練網路在流水線各站間加一個小型「調味站」

🧪 驗證考題

Q1: LoRA 為什麼用 B=0 初始化而不是隨機初始化？

答案

因為 B=0 → ΔW = BA = 0 at initialization。這意味著訓練開始時，LoRA 模型的行為完全等同於原始預訓練模型。如果隨機初始化，一開始就會有隨機擾動，可能破壞預訓練學到的知識，導致訓練不穩定。A 用隨機初始化是因為只有 B·A 的乘積有意義。

Q2: DPO 相比 PPO 的主要限制是什麼？

答案

(1) Offline only: DPO 只能用預先收集的偏好資料，不能像 PPO 那樣 online 生成→打分→更新。(2) Distribution shift: 偏好資料是舊 policy 生成的，新 policy 可能生成完全不同的 responses，但這些 responses 沒有偏好標籤。(3) No reward model: 無法做 Best-of-N sampling（生成 N 個回答，用 RM 選最好的）。(4) Reward hacking 檢測較難: 沒有顯式 reward 可以 monitor。

Q3: 如何選擇 LoRA rank？rank 太大或太小分別有什麼問題？

答案

太小 (r=1-4): 表達能力不足，無法學到複雜的任務特定 patterns，underfitting。太大 (r=256+): 接近 full fine-tune 的參數量，失去 PEFT 的優勢（節省記憶體、快速切換），且容易 overfit 小型 fine-tune 資料集。選擇方法: (1) 任務離預訓練分佈越遠 → rank 越高；(2) 資料量越少 → rank 越低（避免 overfit）；(3) 實驗：從 r=16 開始，2x 遞增直到 validation loss 不再改善。

Q4: RLHF 中的 KL penalty 為什麼必要？去掉會怎樣？

答案

沒有 KL penalty，policy 會 "reward hack"：找到 reward model 的漏洞生成高分但垃圾的 responses（例如重複特定 token pattern 騙到高 reward）。KL penalty 確保 policy 不會偏離 SFT model 太遠，保持語言品質。β（KL 係數）太大 → 幾乎不學新東西；β 太小 → reward hacking。通常 β=0.01-0.1。

Q5: 你有一張 A100 80GB，要 fine-tune LLaMA-70B 做客服 chatbot，請設計方案。

答案

方案：QLoRA
Base model: NF4 量化 → 70B × 0.5 bytes = 35 GB
LoRA adapters (r=64, all projections): ~160M params × 16 bytes (Adam) = 2.5 GB
Activations + overhead: ~30 GB (with gradient checkpointing)
Total: ~68 GB ✓ fits in 80GB

Config: r=64, alpha=128, all projection layers, LR=2e-4, batch=1, grad_accum=16, seq_len=2048, 3 epochs on 50K conversations. 後接 DPO (β=0.1) on 10K preference pairs from human annotation。

🎯 Follow-up Q&A

F1: LoRA adapters 可以組合嗎？例如一個模型同時做多種任務？

可以！幾種方式：(1) LoRA switching: 根據 request routing 載入不同 adapter（vLLM 支援）；(2) LoRA merging: 多個 adapter 加權平均合併（task arithmetic）；(3) LoRA stacking: 串聯多個 adapter（先 A 再 B）。實務上 switching 最常見——同一 base model serve 多客戶，每客戶有自己的 adapter，per-request 切換幾乎零成本（只改 LoRA weights，不改 base）。

F2: 如何判斷 fine-tuning 是否有效？避免 catastrophic forgetting？

(1) Task eval: 目標任務的 benchmark score 應該提升；(2) General eval: 在 MMLU/HellaSwag 等通識 benchmark 上分數不應大幅下降（>2% drop = warning）；(3) Perplexity on hold-out: fine-tune data 的 PPL 下降但 general text PPL 不應暴增。避免 catastrophic forgetting：用低 LR（2e-5 for full, 2e-4 for LoRA）、短 epoch（1-3）、KL regularization、混入少量 general data（5-10%）。LoRA 天然較不容易 catastrophic forget，因為 base weights 被凍結。

F3: Constitutional AI (CAI) 和 RLHF 的區別？

CAI (Anthropic) 用 AI 自己生成偏好資料取代人類 annotator：(1) 生成 response；(2) 用 "constitution"（一組原則如 "be helpful, be honest, be harmless"）讓 AI 自我批評並修改；(3) 用修改前/後作為 rejected/chosen 訓練 DPO/PPO。優勢：scalable（不需要大量人工標注），consistent（不受 annotator 主觀性影響）。劣勢：AI 可能有 systematic bias，某些 edge cases 需要人類判斷。

F4: Multi-task LoRA vs 單一通用 LoRA，如何選擇？

單一通用 LoRA: 混合所有任務資料一起訓練，簡單但不同任務可能互相干擾（negative transfer），適合任務相似的場景。Multi-task LoRA (per-task): 每個任務獨立 adapter，inference 時 routing，無干擾但管理複雜。經驗法則: 任務 < 5 且資料量差異不大 → 單一 LoRA（混合資料）；任務多且異質性大 → per-task LoRA + routing。也可以先練一個 base LoRA（通用），再針對每個任務練 delta adapter。

F5: 為什麼 LLaMA-3 的 fine-tuning 效果比 LLaMA-2 好那麼多？

關鍵差異：(1) 更多預訓練資料（15T vs 2T tokens）→ 更好的 base 知識；(2) GQA (Grouped Query Attention) → 更省記憶體，允許更長 context fine-tuning；(3) 更好的 tokenizer（128K vocab vs 32K）→ 更高效編碼各語言；(4) RoPE scaling → 支持更長 sequence（128K）。Base model 越好，fine-tuning 需要「教」的東西越少，用更少資料就能 elicit 出能力。

F6: 如何處理 fine-tuning 資料中的噪音和 label 錯誤？

(1) 資料品質過濾: 用現有 LLM 打分（self-consistency, coherence），移除低分 samples；(2) Deduplication: MinHash/SimHash 去除近似重複；(3) Length filtering: 移除過短（< 50 tokens）或過長 responses；(4) Label smoothing: 偏好資料中不用 hard label，改用 soft（70%/30% 而非 100%/0%）；(5) Training tricks: 低 LR + 短 epoch 讓模型不完全 memorize 噪音。Noisy label 影響：DPO 比 PPO 更敏感（因為 DPO 直接用 preference pairs），建議偏好資料品質 > 數量。

Chapter 11: LLM Serving — 大型語言模型推論系統設計

🌱 從零開始理解

生活比喻：LLM 推論像是一個超級慢的打字機（每秒只能打 30-50 字），但有 1000 人同時排隊等它打字。Serving 系統的挑戰是：如何讓這 1000 人都覺得自己在「即時」收到回覆？答案是「打字機記憶管理」(PagedAttention) + 「讓快打字機先猜測幾個字」(Speculative Decoding)。

白話解釋：LLM 生成是 autoregressive 的——一次只產一個 token，每個 token 都需要讀取之前所有 token 的 KV cache。瓶頸不是計算而是記憶體頻寬（memory-bound）。Serving 系統的核心是管理 KV cache 記憶體、最大化 GPU 利用率（batching）、降低延遲。

為什麼工作上需要：Google L5 面試會問「設計一個 LLM serving system 支撐 10K QPS」。你需要理解 prefill vs decode phase、KV cache 管理、batching 策略、量化加速等。

先備知識：Transformer attention 機制、GPU 記憶體階層 (HBM/SRAM)、基本 serving 系統設計（load balancer, auto-scaling）。

11.1 LLM 推論的兩個階段

💡 核心概念

LLM inference 分為兩個截然不同的階段：

  • Prefill (Prompt Processing): 一次處理完整 prompt（compute-bound, 可平行）
  • Decode (Token Generation): 逐一生成 token（memory-bound, 無法平行）

關鍵指標：TTFT (Time To First Token) = prefill 時間；TPS (Tokens Per Second) = decode 速度

"""
LLM Inference 效能特性分析：

Prefill Phase:
  - 輸入: 完整 prompt [batch, seq_len, hidden]
  - 計算: 所有 token 的 Q, K, V 一次算完
  - 瓶頸: Compute-bound (大量矩陣乘法)
  - 可以高效利用 GPU (high arithmetic intensity)
  - FLOPS: ~2 * model_params * prompt_tokens

Decode Phase:  
  - 輸入: 單一新 token [batch, 1, hidden]
  - 計算: 只算新 token 的 Q，但需要讀所有舊 K, V
  - 瓶頸: Memory-bound (讀 KV cache 的頻寬)
  - GPU 利用率低 (大量讀取，少量計算)
  - FLOPS: ~2 * model_params (per token)
  - 但需要讀取: 2 * num_layers * seq_len * hidden_dim bytes

效能公式：
  Decode throughput ≈ HBM_bandwidth / KV_cache_size_per_token
  A100 80GB: HBM BW = 2 TB/s
  LLaMA-70B, seq=4096: KV per token ≈ 80 layers × 2(K,V) × 8192 × 2 bytes = 2.6 MB
  Theoretical max: 2 TB/s / 2.6 MB ≈ 769 tokens/s (single request)
"""

import time
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class ServingMetrics:
    """LLM Serving 核心指標"""
    ttft_ms: float          # Time to First Token (ms)
    tps: float              # Tokens per second (decode phase)
    total_latency_ms: float # End-to-end latency
    throughput_tps: float   # System throughput (all requests)
    
    # SLO targets (typical)
    TTFT_P99_TARGET_MS = 500    # < 500ms for interactive use
    TPS_MIN = 30                # > 30 TPS feels "real-time"
    
    @property
    def meets_slo(self) -> bool:
        return (self.ttft_ms < self.TTFT_P99_TARGET_MS and 
                self.tps > self.TPS_MIN)


def analyze_kv_cache_memory(model_params_b: float, num_layers: int,
                            hidden_dim: int, num_kv_heads: int,
                            seq_len: int, batch_size: int,
                            dtype_bytes: int = 2) -> dict:
    """Analyze KV cache memory requirements
    
    KV cache per token per layer = 2 * num_kv_heads * head_dim * dtype_bytes
    Total = batch * seq_len * num_layers * above
    """
    head_dim = hidden_dim // (num_kv_heads * 8)  # Approximate
    # For GQA: num_kv_heads < num_attention_heads
    
    kv_per_token_per_layer = 2 * num_kv_heads * head_dim * dtype_bytes
    kv_per_token = kv_per_token_per_layer * num_layers
    total_kv = batch_size * seq_len * kv_per_token
    
    return {
        "kv_per_token_bytes": kv_per_token,
        "kv_per_token_mb": kv_per_token / (1024**2),
        "total_kv_cache_gb": total_kv / (1024**3),
        "model_weights_gb": model_params_b * 2,  # fp16
        "total_gpu_mem_gb": (total_kv / (1024**3)) + model_params_b * 2,
    }

# LLaMA-3 70B (GQA: 8 KV heads, 64 Q heads, 80 layers, hidden=8192)
kv = analyze_kv_cache_memory(
    model_params_b=70, num_layers=80, hidden_dim=8192,
    num_kv_heads=8, seq_len=4096, batch_size=32, dtype_bytes=2
)
print(f"KV cache for batch=32, seq=4096: {kv['total_kv_cache_gb']:.1f} GB")
print(f"Model weights: {kv['model_weights_gb']:.0f} GB")
print(f"Total GPU memory: {kv['total_gpu_mem_gb']:.1f} GB")
# KV cache: ~10 GB, Model: 140 GB, Total: ~150 GB → 2 A100s minimum

11.2 PagedAttention (vLLM) — 革命性 KV Cache 管理

💡 核心概念

問題：傳統系統為每個 request 預分配 max_seq_len 的連續 KV cache 記憶體。如果 max=4096 但實際只用 200 tokens → 95% 記憶體浪費。

解法：PagedAttention 借鑑 OS 虛擬記憶體的 paging 機制，把 KV cache 切成固定大小的 "pages"(blocks)，動態分配，非連續存儲。

效果：記憶體利用率從 20-40% 提升到 >95%，同樣 GPU 可以 serve 3-5x 更多並行 requests。

"""
PagedAttention Architecture:

Traditional (contiguous KV cache):
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Request 1: [██████████░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░] │ ← max_len allocated
│ Request 2: [████░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░░] │ ← 90% wasted!
│ Request 3: [Cannot allocate — OOM]               │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

PagedAttention (block-based):
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Block Pool: [B0][B1][B2][B3][B4][B5][B6][B7]...  │
│                                                   │
│ Request 1 page table: B0 → B3 → B5 (3 blocks)    │
│ Request 2 page table: B1 → B4 (2 blocks)         │
│ Request 3 page table: B2 → B6 → B7 (3 blocks)    │ ← fits!
│                                                   │
│ No fragmentation! Only allocate what's needed.    │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Optional
import numpy as np

@dataclass
class KVBlock:
    """A fixed-size block of KV cache (analogous to OS memory page)"""
    block_id: int
    block_size: int = 16  # tokens per block
    ref_count: int = 0    # For copy-on-write (beam search sharing)
    
    # Actual KV tensors would be stored in GPU memory
    # Shape per block: [num_layers, 2(K,V), block_size, num_heads, head_dim]

class PagedKVCacheManager:
    """vLLM's PagedAttention KV cache manager (simplified)"""
    
    def __init__(self, num_blocks: int, block_size: int = 16):
        self.block_size = block_size
        self.num_blocks = num_blocks
        
        # Free block pool
        self.free_blocks: List[int] = list(range(num_blocks))
        
        # Per-request page tables: request_id → [block_ids]
        self.page_tables: Dict[int, List[int]] = {}
    
    def allocate(self, request_id: int) -> int:
        """Allocate a new block for a request (called when current block is full)"""
        if not self.free_blocks:
            raise MemoryError("No free blocks — need to preempt a request")
        
        block_id = self.free_blocks.pop(0)
        
        if request_id not in self.page_tables:
            self.page_tables[request_id] = []
        self.page_tables[request_id].append(block_id)
        
        return block_id
    
    def free(self, request_id: int):
        """Free all blocks for a completed request"""
        if request_id in self.page_tables:
            self.free_blocks.extend(self.page_tables[request_id])
            del self.page_tables[request_id]
    
    def get_utilization(self) -> float:
        """Memory utilization = allocated / total"""
        allocated = self.num_blocks - len(self.free_blocks)
        return allocated / self.num_blocks
    
    def can_allocate(self, num_blocks_needed: int) -> bool:
        return len(self.free_blocks) >= num_blocks_needed
    
    def copy_on_write(self, request_id: int, block_idx: int) -> int:
        """For beam search: fork a block (copy-on-write semantics)"""
        old_block = self.page_tables[request_id][block_idx]
        new_block = self.free_blocks.pop(0)
        # Copy KV data from old_block to new_block (GPU memcpy)
        self.page_tables[request_id][block_idx] = new_block
        return new_block


class ContinuousBatchingScheduler:
    """vLLM's continuous batching scheduler
    
    Key insight: Don't wait for a batch to complete!
    As soon as one request finishes, immediately add a new one.
    
    Traditional: [R1, R2, R3] → wait all finish → [R4, R5, R6]
    Continuous:  [R1, R2, R3] → R2 finishes → [R1, R4, R3] → ...
    """
    
    def __init__(self, max_batch_size: int, kv_manager: PagedKVCacheManager):
        self.max_batch_size = max_batch_size
        self.kv_manager = kv_manager
        self.running: List[dict] = []    # Currently generating
        self.waiting: List[dict] = []    # Queue
        self.prefill_queue: List[dict] = []  # Need prefill first
    
    def schedule_step(self) -> dict:
        """Determine what to do this iteration"""
        # Priority 1: Prefill new requests (if GPU has capacity)
        prefill_batch = []
        while self.prefill_queue and len(self.running) < self.max_batch_size:
            req = self.prefill_queue.pop(0)
            # Check if we can allocate KV blocks
            blocks_needed = (req["prompt_len"] + self.kv_manager.block_size - 1) // self.kv_manager.block_size
            if self.kv_manager.can_allocate(blocks_needed):
                for _ in range(blocks_needed):
                    self.kv_manager.allocate(req["id"])
                prefill_batch.append(req)
                self.running.append(req)
            else:
                # Preempt: swap out lowest priority running request
                self.prefill_queue.insert(0, req)
                break
        
        # Priority 2: Decode for all running requests
        decode_batch = [r for r in self.running if r not in prefill_batch]
        
        return {
            "prefill": prefill_batch,
            "decode": decode_batch,
            "total_batch": len(self.running)
        }

11.3 Speculative Decoding — 投機解碼加速

💡 核心概念

問題：大模型 decode 一個 token 需要整個 forward pass，但 forward pass 的大部分時間花在 memory read（讀權重），不是計算。一次生成 1 token 和生成 5 tokens 的 FLOPS 差不多！

解法：用一個小模型（draft model）快速「猜」K 個 tokens，然後大模型一次性 verify 這 K 個 tokens。如果猜對了 → 一步生成 K tokens；猜錯了 → 從錯誤位置重新開始。

數學保證：使用 rejection sampling，最終分佈嚴格等於大模型的分佈（無品質損失）。

"""
Speculative Decoding (投機解碼):

Algorithm:
1. Draft model generates K tokens: t1, t2, ..., tK
2. Target model verifies all K tokens in ONE forward pass
3. For each position i:
   - If P_target(ti) >= P_draft(ti): ACCEPT (keep token)
   - Else: accept with probability P_target(ti) / P_draft(ti)
   - If rejected: sample from adjusted distribution, STOP
4. If all K accepted: bonus sample one more token from target

Speedup = K * acceptance_rate / (1 + K * draft_time/target_time)
Typical: K=5, acceptance_rate=0.7-0.85 → 2-3x speedup

Key: Target model forward for K tokens costs ≈ same as 1 token 
     (memory-bound, not compute-bound in decode phase)
"""

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

def speculative_decode(target_model, draft_model, 
                       input_ids: torch.Tensor,
                       max_new_tokens: int = 256,
                       num_speculative: int = 5,
                       temperature: float = 1.0) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Speculative decoding with rejection sampling
    
    Guarantees: output distribution = target model distribution (exact)
    Speedup: typically 2-3x for well-matched draft models
    """
    generated = input_ids.clone()
    
    tokens_generated = 0
    while tokens_generated < max_new_tokens:
        # Step 1: Draft model generates K tokens (fast)
        draft_tokens = []
        draft_probs = []
        draft_input = generated.clone()
        
        for _ in range(num_speculative):
            with torch.no_grad():
                draft_logits = draft_model(draft_input).logits[:, -1, :]
                draft_p = F.softmax(draft_logits / temperature, dim=-1)
                token = torch.multinomial(draft_p, 1)
                draft_tokens.append(token)
                draft_probs.append(draft_p.gather(1, token))
                draft_input = torch.cat([draft_input, token], dim=1)
        
        # Step 2: Target model verifies ALL K tokens in one forward pass
        verify_input = torch.cat([generated] + draft_tokens, dim=1)
        with torch.no_grad():
            target_logits = target_model(verify_input).logits
        
        # Step 3: Rejection sampling for each position
        accepted = 0
        for i in range(num_speculative):
            pos = generated.shape[1] + i - 1  # Position in verify output
            target_p = F.softmax(target_logits[:, pos, :] / temperature, dim=-1)
            target_prob = target_p.gather(1, draft_tokens[i])
            draft_prob = draft_probs[i]
            
            # Accept with probability min(1, p_target / p_draft)
            accept_ratio = (target_prob / draft_prob).clamp(max=1.0)
            
            if torch.rand(1) < accept_ratio:
                accepted += 1
                generated = torch.cat([generated, draft_tokens[i]], dim=1)
            else:
                # Rejected: sample from adjusted distribution
                # P_adjusted = max(0, P_target - P_draft) normalized
                adjusted = torch.clamp(target_p - F.softmax(
                    draft_model(generated).logits[:, -1, :] / temperature, dim=-1
                ), min=0)
                adjusted = adjusted / adjusted.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
                token = torch.multinomial(adjusted, 1)
                generated = torch.cat([generated, token], dim=1)
                break
        else:
            # All K tokens accepted! Bonus: sample one more from target
            bonus_p = F.softmax(target_logits[:, -1, :] / temperature, dim=-1)
            bonus_token = torch.multinomial(bonus_p, 1)
            generated = torch.cat([generated, bonus_token], dim=1)
            accepted += 1
        
        tokens_generated += accepted + 1
    
    return generated


# Speculative Decoding 配置選擇：
"""
┌────────────────────┬─────────────────────┬──────────────┐
│ Draft Model        │ Target Model         │ Acceptance % │
├────────────────────┼─────────────────────┼──────────────┤
│ LLaMA-7B          │ LLaMA-70B           │ ~70%         │
│ Distilled 1B      │ LLaMA-13B           │ ~80%         │
│ Medusa heads      │ Same model (self)    │ ~75%         │
│ EAGLE (feature)   │ Same model (self)    │ ~85%         │
│ Lookahead (n-gram)│ Same model           │ ~60%         │
└────────────────────┴─────────────────────┴──────────────┘

Self-speculative variants (no separate draft model):
- Medusa: 多個額外 prediction heads 同時猜多個位置
- EAGLE: 用 feature-level speculation (hidden states)
- Lookahead: 用 n-gram cache 從歷史生成中找 patterns
"""

11.4 Prefix Caching & Prompt Caching

"""
Prefix Caching: 共享相同 prefix 的 KV cache

場景：多個 requests 有相同的 system prompt (e.g., ChatGPT system message)
  Request 1: [system_prompt] + "What is Python?"
  Request 2: [system_prompt] + "Explain recursion"
  Request 3: [system_prompt] + "Write a sort function"
  
  System prompt = 2000 tokens → 計算一次，cache KV，所有 requests 共用
  
vLLM: Automatic Prefix Caching (APC)
  - Hash prefix tokens → lookup cached KV blocks
  - Block-level granularity (16 tokens/block)
  - LRU eviction when memory full
  
SGLang: RadixAttention
  - Radix tree (trie) structure for prefix matching
  - Supports partial prefix matches (not just exact)
  - Better for few-shot examples where prefixes vary
"""

from collections import OrderedDict
import hashlib

class PrefixCache:
    """Automatic Prefix Caching (APC) for KV cache reuse"""
    
    def __init__(self, max_cache_blocks: int, block_size: int = 16):
        self.block_size = block_size
        self.max_cache_blocks = max_cache_blocks
        # LRU cache: hash(token_block) → GPU block_id
        self.cache: OrderedDict[str, int] = OrderedDict()
    
    def _hash_block(self, token_ids: list) -> str:
        """Hash a block of tokens for lookup"""
        return hashlib.md5(str(token_ids).encode()).hexdigest()
    
    def lookup_prefix(self, prompt_tokens: list) -> tuple:
        """Find cached prefix blocks
        
        Returns: (num_cached_tokens, cached_block_ids)
        """
        cached_blocks = []
        for i in range(0, len(prompt_tokens), self.block_size):
            block_tokens = prompt_tokens[i:i + self.block_size]
            if len(block_tokens) < self.block_size:
                break  # Incomplete block — can't cache
            
            block_hash = self._hash_block(block_tokens)
            if block_hash in self.cache:
                # Cache hit! Move to end (LRU)
                self.cache.move_to_end(block_hash)
                cached_blocks.append(self.cache[block_hash])
            else:
                break  # First miss — remaining tokens need compute
        
        cached_tokens = len(cached_blocks) * self.block_size
        return cached_tokens, cached_blocks
    
    def store_prefix(self, prompt_tokens: list, gpu_block_ids: list):
        """Store computed KV blocks in cache"""
        for i, block_start in enumerate(range(0, len(prompt_tokens), self.block_size)):
            block_tokens = prompt_tokens[block_start:block_start + self.block_size]
            if len(block_tokens) < self.block_size:
                break
            
            block_hash = self._hash_block(block_tokens)
            if block_hash not in self.cache:
                # Evict if full
                if len(self.cache) >= self.max_cache_blocks:
                    self.cache.popitem(last=False)  # Remove oldest
                self.cache[block_hash] = gpu_block_ids[i]

# Performance impact:
# System prompt = 2000 tokens, LLaMA-70B
# Without cache: 2000 tokens prefill ≈ 200ms TTFT
# With cache: skip prefill for cached prefix → ~20ms TTFT (10x faster!)
# Memory cost: 2000 tokens × 2.6 MB/token = 5.2 GB per cached prefix

11.5 量化推論 (INT8/INT4/GPTQ/AWQ)

"""
Quantization for Inference:

目標：減少模型大小和記憶體頻寬需求 → 更快推論

┌──────────────┬─────────────────┬──────────────┬──────────────┐
│ Method       │ Bits/Param      │ 速度提升      │ 品質損失      │
├──────────────┼─────────────────┼──────────────┼──────────────┤
│ FP16/BF16    │ 16 (baseline)   │ 1x           │ 0            │
│ INT8 (W8A8)  │ 8               │ 1.5-2x       │ < 0.5%       │
│ INT4 (W4A16) │ 4 (weights only)│ 2-3x         │ 1-3%         │
│ GPTQ         │ 4 (calibrated)  │ 2-3x         │ < 1%         │
│ AWQ          │ 4 (activation)  │ 3-4x         │ < 1%         │
│ GGUF (llama.cpp)│ 2-6 (mixed) │ CPU viable!  │ Varies       │
└──────────────┴─────────────────┴──────────────┴──────────────┘

Key insight: Weights are easy to quantize (static), 
             Activations are hard (dynamic range, outliers)
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn

class Int8LinearWithOutliers(nn.Module):
    """LLM.int8() — Handle activation outliers with mixed precision
    
    Insight (Dettmers et al.): ~0.1% of hidden dimensions have 
    extreme activations (100x larger). Quantizing these → catastrophic.
    
    Solution: 
    - Detect outlier dimensions (magnitude > threshold)
    - Compute outlier dims in fp16 (small fraction)
    - Compute rest in int8 (99.9% of dims)
    - Sum results
    """
    
    def __init__(self, weight: torch.Tensor, outlier_threshold: float = 6.0):
        super().__init__()
        self.outlier_threshold = outlier_threshold
        
        # Find outlier columns in weight matrix
        # (columns that correspond to high-magnitude activation dims)
        self.outlier_dims = self._find_outliers(weight)
        
        # Separate weights
        self.weight_int8 = self._quantize_int8(
            weight[:, ~self.outlier_dims]
        )
        self.weight_fp16 = weight[:, self.outlier_dims].half()
        self.scale = weight[:, ~self.outlier_dims].abs().max(dim=0).values / 127
    
    def _find_outliers(self, weight):
        """Identify columns with outlier magnitudes"""
        col_magnitudes = weight.abs().max(dim=0).values
        threshold = col_magnitudes.mean() * self.outlier_threshold
        return col_magnitudes > threshold
    
    def _quantize_int8(self, tensor):
        scale = tensor.abs().max(dim=0).values / 127
        return (tensor / scale).round().to(torch.int8), scale
    
    def forward(self, x):
        # Split input by outlier/normal dimensions
        x_normal = x[:, :, ~self.outlier_dims]
        x_outlier = x[:, :, self.outlier_dims]
        
        # INT8 matmul for normal dims (fast, 99.9%)
        out_int8 = torch.matmul(x_normal.to(torch.int8), 
                                self.weight_int8[0].T)
        out_normal = out_int8.float() * self.weight_int8[1]
        
        # FP16 matmul for outlier dims (accurate, 0.1%)
        out_outlier = torch.matmul(x_outlier.half(), self.weight_fp16.T)
        
        return out_normal + out_outlier.float()


# AWQ (Activation-Aware Weight Quantization)
"""
AWQ key insight: 不是所有 weight channels 同等重要
  重要性 = 對應 activation channel 的平均大小
  
  對重要 channels: 先乘以 scaling factor s（放大），量化，推論時除以 s
  效果: 重要 channels 量化誤差按 1/s 縮小
  
  s 的選擇: grid search 使 quantization error * activation magnitude 最小
"""

11.6 完整 Serving 系統架構設計

"""
面試題：Design an LLM serving system for 10K concurrent users

Architecture:
                                                
┌──────────┐    ┌──────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────────────┐
│  Client  │───→│ Load Balancer│───→│  Request Router              │
└──────────┘    └──────────────┘    │  (prompt length routing)     │
                                    └────────┬────────┬────────────┘
                                             │        │
                              ┌──────────────┴─┐  ┌──┴──────────────┐
                              │ Prefill Workers │  │  Decode Workers  │
                              │ (compute-bound) │  │ (memory-bound)   │
                              │ GPU: 100% util  │  │ GPU: batch max   │
                              └────────┬────────┘  └────────┬────────┘
                                       │                     │
                                       └────────┬────────────┘
                                                │
                                    ┌───────────┴───────────┐
                                    │ KV Cache Store         │
                                    │ (GPU mem + CPU offload)│
                                    └───────────────────────┘

Design Decisions:

1. Disaggregated Prefill/Decode (Splitwise, DistServe):
   - Prefill: batch prompts, high GPU compute utilization
   - Decode: maximize concurrent requests (memory-bound)
   - Why split? Different hardware profiles optimal for each phase

2. Batching Strategy:
   - Continuous batching (don't wait for batch to complete)
   - Dynamic batch size based on available KV cache memory
   - Priority queue: shorter prompts first (faster TTFT)

3. Request Scheduling:
   - FCFS (fair) vs Shortest-Job-First (better avg latency)
   - Preemption: pause long generations for new short requests
   - Fairness: per-user rate limiting

4. Auto-scaling:
   - Scale on: GPU memory utilization + queue depth
   - Scale up: when P99 TTFT > SLO threshold
   - Scale down: when GPU util < 30% for 5 minutes
   
5. Multi-GPU Serving:
   - Tensor Parallelism: reduce per-GPU memory (same request across GPUs)
   - Pipeline Parallelism: overlap prefill/decode across stages
   - Data Parallelism: multiple replicas for throughput
"""

# Cost Analysis
def serving_cost_analysis(model_params_b: float, 
                          target_qps: float,
                          avg_prompt_tokens: int = 500,
                          avg_output_tokens: int = 200,
                          gpu_type: str = "A100_80GB") -> dict:
    """Estimate serving infrastructure cost"""
    
    gpu_configs = {
        "A100_80GB": {"mem_gb": 80, "cost_per_hour": 3.0, "decode_tps": 40},
        "H100_80GB": {"mem_gb": 80, "cost_per_hour": 5.0, "decode_tps": 80},
        "A10G_24GB": {"mem_gb": 24, "cost_per_hour": 1.0, "decode_tps": 15},
    }
    gpu = gpu_configs[gpu_type]
    
    # GPUs needed for model weights (TP)
    model_mem_gb = model_params_b * 2  # fp16
    tp_size = max(1, int(model_mem_gb / (gpu["mem_gb"] * 0.6)))  # 60% for model
    
    # Decode throughput per TP group
    # Each request takes ~avg_output_tokens / decode_tps seconds
    request_duration_sec = avg_output_tokens / gpu["decode_tps"]
    
    # Max concurrent requests per TP group (limited by KV cache memory)
    available_kv_mem = gpu["mem_gb"] * tp_size * 0.3  # 30% for KV
    kv_per_request_gb = (avg_prompt_tokens + avg_output_tokens) * 0.003  # ~3MB/token for 70B
    max_concurrent = int(available_kv_mem / kv_per_request_gb)
    
    # Throughput per TP group
    throughput_per_group = max_concurrent / request_duration_sec
    
    # Total TP groups needed
    num_groups = max(1, int(target_qps / throughput_per_group) + 1)
    total_gpus = num_groups * tp_size
    
    hourly_cost = total_gpus * gpu["cost_per_hour"]
    cost_per_1k_tokens = hourly_cost / (target_qps * 3600 * 
                         (avg_prompt_tokens + avg_output_tokens) / 1000)
    
    return {
        "tp_size": tp_size,
        "num_replicas": num_groups,
        "total_gpus": total_gpus,
        "max_concurrent_per_replica": max_concurrent,
        "hourly_cost_usd": hourly_cost,
        "cost_per_1k_tokens_usd": cost_per_1k_tokens,
    }

# Example: Serve LLaMA-70B at 100 QPS
cost = serving_cost_analysis(70, target_qps=100, gpu_type="H100_80GB")
print(f"Total GPUs: {cost['total_gpus']}")
print(f"Hourly cost: ${cost['hourly_cost_usd']:.0f}")
print(f"Cost per 1K tokens: ${cost['cost_per_1k_tokens_usd']:.4f}")

📖 術語表

術語英文白話解釋生活比喻
TTFTTime To First Token從發送 request 到收到第一個 token 的時間餐廳從點餐到上第一道菜的等待時間
TPSTokens Per Second生成 token 的速度（decode phase）打字機每秒打幾個字
KV CacheKey-Value Cache存儲已計算過的 attention K/V，避免重算記住之前說過的話，不用每次都重聽
PagedAttention—用分頁方式管理 KV cache，消除碎片OS 虛擬記憶體的分頁機制應用到 GPU
Continuous Batching—完成的 request 立即替換為新 request自助餐：有人吃完立即讓下一位坐下
Speculative Decoding投機解碼小模型猜測，大模型驗證，加速生成助手先寫草稿，主管一次性審核通過
Prefix Caching—緩存共享前綴的 KV，避免重複計算背熟固定開場白，不用每次重念
Quantization量化降低數值精度 (fp16→int4) 減少記憶體把高清照片壓縮成縮圖（小但能看）
AWQActivation-Aware Weight Quantization根據 activation 重要性選擇性保護精度VIP 座位保留高品質，經濟座用省空間
Disaggregated Serving分離式服務Prefill 和 Decode 用不同 GPU pool分工：A 部門負責受理、B 部門負責辦事

🧪 驗證考題

Q1: 為什麼 LLM decode phase 是 memory-bound 而不是 compute-bound？

答案

Decode 每步只生成 1 token：計算量 = 2×model_params FLOPs，但需要讀取整個模型權重（+ KV cache）。A100 compute = 312 TFLOPS (fp16)，但 HBM bandwidth = 2 TB/s。70B fp16 model = 140 GB → 讀一次需 70ms，但 140B FLOPs / 312 TFLOPS = 0.45ms。讀取時間 >> 計算時間 → memory-bound。Arithmetic intensity = FLOPs/bytes = 1（遠低於 GPU 的 balance point ~160）。

Q2: PagedAttention 如何幫助 beam search？

答案

Beam search 中多個 beams 共享前綴（相同的 KV cache）。PagedAttention 用 copy-on-write：多個 beams 的 page table 指向相同 blocks，只有當某個 beam diverge 時才 copy 那個 block。傳統系統必須為每個 beam 複製完整 KV cache（beam_width × full_sequence）→ 記憶體用量 O(beam_width × seq_len)。PagedAttention 下，共享前綴只存一份 → O(seq_len + beam_width × diverged_length)，記憶體節省 60-80%。

Q3: Continuous batching 相比 static batching 的吞吐量提升來自哪裡？

答案

Static batching：一個 batch 中最長的 request 決定所有人何時結束。短 request 生成完畢後 GPU 閒置等待（padding waste）。如果 batch 內 request 長度差異大（10 tokens vs 500 tokens），50% 以上 GPU cycles 浪費。Continuous batching：request 完成後立即移出、新 request 立即加入，GPU 永遠滿載。實測 vLLM 比 naive HuggingFace serving 吞吐量高 14-24x。

Q4: Speculative decoding 什麼情況下不適用？

答案

(1) Low acceptance rate: 如果 draft model 和 target 差異太大（不同 domain），大部分 token 被 reject，反而更慢（浪費 draft 時間）；(2) High batch utilization: 如果 target model 已經在 serve 大 batch（GPU 滿載），verification forward pass 的額外 compute 會 impact 其他 requests；(3) Very short outputs: overhead of running two models > benefit；(4) Sampling temperature high: 高溫度下 token 更隨機，draft model 更難猜中。最適合：greedy/低溫度 + 長輸出 + 有好的 draft model。

Q5: 如何設計一個支持多個 LoRA adapters 的 serving system？

答案

架構：一個 base model + N 個 LoRA adapters in GPU memory。
Batching：同一 batch 內可以有不同 adapter 的 requests（S-LoRA 論文）。
實作：Base model forward 共享，LoRA 部分用 custom CUDA kernel batched matmul（每個 request 乘自己的 BA 矩陣）。
記憶體：LoRA adapter ~160MB (r=64, 70B model)，80GB GPU 可放 ~400 個 adapters！
Scheduling：相同 adapter 的 requests batch 在一起效率更高（cache locality），但混合 batch 仍可行。
Hot/cold：LRU eviction 管理 GPU 中的 adapter set，cold adapters offload 到 CPU/SSD。

🎯 Follow-up Q&A

F1: 如何選擇 Tensor Parallel degree for serving vs training？

Training 用 TP 主要是記憶體（放不下 optimizer states），所以 TP=8 很常見。Serving 不需要 optimizer → TP 主要是為了降低延遲（單 request 更快）。但 TP 有 All-Reduce overhead，增加 TP 到一定程度後延遲反而上升。經驗法則：(1) 如果模型 fp16 fit in 1 GPU → TP=1（最高吞吐）；(2) 需要 2-4 GPUs 才能放下 → TP=2-4；(3) 延遲敏感場景才用更高 TP。吞吐量最大化：DP replicas > TP degree。

F2: Prefill 和 Decode 分離部署 (Splitwise) 的好處和挑戰？

好處：(1) Prefill workers 用 compute-optimized GPU（高 FLOPS），Decode workers 用 memory-optimized GPU（高 bandwidth）；(2) Prefill batch size 可以很大（GPU 利用率高），Decode batch size 由 KV memory 決定；(3) 避免長 prefill 阻塞短 decode。挑戰：(1) KV cache 傳輸：prefill 計算完的 KV 要傳給 decode worker（高頻寬需求）；(2) 負載均衡：prompt 長度分佈決定 prefill:decode 比例；(3) 增加系統複雜度和 P2P latency。適合超大規模 production deployment。

F3: FlashAttention 在 serving 中的作用？

FlashAttention 是 attention 計算的 IO-aware 優化：(1) 把 QKV 切成 tiles 在 SRAM 計算，減少 HBM 讀寫次數（從 O(N²) 降到 O(N²/SRAM_SIZE)）；(2) 不需要 materialize N×N attention matrix 到 HBM（省記憶體 O(N) vs O(N²)）。在 serving 中：(1) Prefill phase：長 prompt 的 attention 計算加速 2-4x；(2) Decode phase：Flash-Decoding 針對 decode 優化（沿 KV sequence 維度 parallel split）。FlashAttention-3 (H100) 利用 tensor cores + async pipeline 達到 hardware limit。

F4: 如何處理超長 context (100K+ tokens) 的 serving 挑戰？

(1) KV cache 記憶體爆炸: 100K tokens × 2.6 MB = 260 GB per request（比 model 還大！）→ 解法：KV compression (H2O: Heavy Hitter Oracle, 只保留重要 tokens 的 KV)、quantized KV cache (INT4 KV)。(2) Prefill 延遲: 100K tokens prefill 可能需要 10-30 秒 → 解法：chunked prefill（分批處理 prompt，允許 decode 穿插）。(3) Attention complexity: O(N²) → 解法：Ring Attention（跨 GPU 分散 seq dim）。(4) Throughput drop: 每個 request 佔用大量 GPU → 減少並發。

F5: vLLM vs TGI vs TensorRT-LLM 的比較？

vLLM: PagedAttention 先驅，最佳 memory efficiency，開源社區活躍，支援 multi-LoRA, speculative decoding。適合：通用 LLM serving。TGI (HuggingFace): 整合 HF ecosystem，容易部署，支援 watermarking, prompt caching。適合：快速上線。TensorRT-LLM (NVIDIA): 最高性能（custom CUDA kernels, in-flight batching），但配置複雜，NVIDIA 鎖定。適合：延遲敏感 production + NVIDIA GPU。SGLang: RadixAttention + compile-based optimization，best for structured generation。選擇：latency-first → TRT-LLM；throughput-first → vLLM；ease-of-use → TGI。

Chapter 12: RAG Pipeline — 檢索增強生成系統設計

🌱 從零開始理解

生活比喻：LLM 像是一個博學但記憶有限的教授——他知道很多通識知識，但不可能記住你公司所有內部文件。RAG 就是給教授配一個圖書館管理員：學生問問題時，管理員先去書架找到相關資料（Retrieval），然後教授看著這些資料回答（Generation）。

白話解釋：RAG = Retrieval + Augmented + Generation。不把所有知識塞進模型（太貴且無法即時更新），而是在推論時「即時查閱」外部知識庫。核心挑戰：(1) 怎麼把文件切成適合搜尋的片段 (Chunking)；(2) 怎麼找到最相關的片段 (Retrieval)；(3) 怎麼確保 LLM 用對了資料 (Grounding)。

為什麼工作上需要：幾乎所有 enterprise LLM 應用都用 RAG（ChatGPT + Bing, Google AI Overview, Copilot）。面試必考：「設計一個知識問答系統」本質上就是 RAG system design。

先備知識：向量空間 (embedding)、相似度搜尋 (cosine similarity)、基本資訊檢索概念。

12.1 RAG 系統全景架構

💡 核心概念

RAG 系統的核心 pipeline：

  1. Indexing (離線): 文件 → 切片 → Embedding → 存入 Vector DB
  2. Retrieval (線上): Query → Embedding → 相似度搜尋 → Top-K 文件片段
  3. Generation (線上): Query + Retrieved Chunks → LLM → Answer
"""
RAG System Architecture:

Offline Indexing Pipeline:
┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────────┐   ┌──────────────┐
│ Documents│──→│ Chunking │──→│ Embedding    │──→│ Vector Store │
│ (PDF,Web)│   │ (512 tok)│   │ (text→vec)   │   │ (HNSW/IVF)  │
└──────────┘   └──────────┘   └──────────────┘   └──────────────┘

Online Query Pipeline:
┌───────┐   ┌───────────┐   ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐   ┌────────┐
│ Query │──→│ Embedding │──→│ Retrieval│──→│ Reranker │──→│  LLM   │
│       │   │           │   │ (Top-50) │   │ (Top-5)  │   │Context │
└───────┘   └───────────┘   └──────────┘   └──────────┘   └────────┘

Advanced (2025):
  + Query rewriting (HyDE, multi-query)
  + Self-RAG (LLM decides if retrieval needed)
  + Agentic RAG (multi-hop reasoning)
  + Graph RAG (knowledge graph + vector)
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Optional
import numpy as np

@dataclass
class Document:
    """A source document"""
    id: str
    content: str
    metadata: dict = field(default_factory=dict)  # source, date, author, etc.

@dataclass
class Chunk:
    """A document chunk (unit of retrieval)"""
    id: str
    content: str
    embedding: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
    document_id: str = ""
    metadata: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    
@dataclass  
class RetrievalResult:
    """Retrieved chunk with relevance score"""
    chunk: Chunk
    score: float  # similarity or reranker score
    
class RAGPipeline:
    """End-to-end RAG system"""
    
    def __init__(self, embedder, vector_store, reranker, llm):
        self.embedder = embedder
        self.vector_store = vector_store
        self.reranker = reranker
        self.llm = llm
    
    def index_documents(self, documents: List[Document], 
                        chunk_size: int = 512, 
                        chunk_overlap: int = 50):
        """Offline: index documents into vector store"""
        for doc in documents:
            chunks = self._chunk_document(doc, chunk_size, chunk_overlap)
            for chunk in chunks:
                chunk.embedding = self.embedder.encode(chunk.content)
                self.vector_store.add(chunk)
    
    def query(self, question: str, top_k: int = 5, 
              use_hyde: bool = False) -> str:
        """Online: answer a question using RAG"""
        
        # Step 1: Optionally rewrite query (HyDE)
        if use_hyde:
            query_for_retrieval = self._hyde_rewrite(question)
        else:
            query_for_retrieval = question
        
        # Step 2: Embed query
        query_embedding = self.embedder.encode(query_for_retrieval)
        
        # Step 3: Retrieve top-K candidates (fast, approximate)
        candidates = self.vector_store.search(
            query_embedding, top_k=top_k * 10  # Over-retrieve
        )
        
        # Step 4: Rerank (accurate, cross-encoder)
        reranked = self.reranker.rerank(question, candidates)[:top_k]
        
        # Step 5: Generate answer with context
        context = "\n\n".join([r.chunk.content for r in reranked])
        prompt = self._build_prompt(question, context)
        answer = self.llm.generate(prompt)
        
        return answer
    
    def _hyde_rewrite(self, question: str) -> str:
        """HyDE: Hypothetical Document Embeddings
        
        Instead of embedding the question directly,
        ask LLM to generate a hypothetical answer,
        then embed THAT for better semantic matching.
        """
        prompt = f"Write a short passage that answers: {question}"
        hypothetical_doc = self.llm.generate(prompt, max_tokens=150)
        return hypothetical_doc
    
    def _chunk_document(self, doc: Document, 
                        chunk_size: int, overlap: int) -> List[Chunk]:
        """Split document into overlapping chunks"""
        # Implementation in next section
        pass
    
    def _build_prompt(self, question: str, context: str) -> str:
        return f"""Answer the question based on the provided context.
If the context doesn't contain enough information, say "I don't have enough information."

Context:
{context}

Question: {question}

Answer:"""

12.2 Chunking 策略 — 切片是 RAG 品質的基礎

"""
Chunking Strategy Comparison:

┌────────────────────┬──────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┐
│ Method             │ Chunk Size       │ 適用場景                       │
├────────────────────┼──────────────────┼───────────────────────────────┤
│ Fixed-size         │ 512 tokens       │ 通用，最簡單                    │
│ Sentence-based     │ 3-5 sentences    │ 句子邊界完整，適合 QA           │
│ Recursive          │ 512 with splits  │ 按段落→句子→字遞歸切割          │
│ Semantic           │ Variable         │ 按主題邊界切割，最精確           │
│ Document-aware     │ Section/heading  │ 保持文件結構 (Markdown headers) │
│ Parent-child       │ Small retrieve,  │ 檢索小塊，返回大塊上下文         │
│                    │ large context    │                               │
└────────────────────┴──────────────────┴───────────────────────────────┘

最佳實踐：
- Chunk size: 256-1024 tokens (太小缺上下文，太大太泛)
- Overlap: 10-20% (確保跨 chunk 邊界的資訊不丟失)
- 保留 metadata: source, page, section header
"""

from typing import List
import re

class RecursiveChunker:
    """Recursive character text splitter (LangChain style)
    
    Strategy: Try to split by largest separator first,
    fall back to smaller separators if chunk too large.
    
    Separators (priority): paragraph → sentence → word → character
    """
    
    def __init__(self, chunk_size: int = 512, chunk_overlap: int = 50,
                 separators: List[str] = None):
        self.chunk_size = chunk_size
        self.chunk_overlap = chunk_overlap
        self.separators = separators or [
            "\n\n",      # Paragraph break
            "\n",        # Line break
            ". ",        # Sentence end
            "? ",        # Question end
            "! ",        # Exclamation end
            "; ",        # Semicolon
            ", ",        # Comma
            " ",         # Word
            ""           # Character (last resort)
        ]
    
    def split(self, text: str) -> List[str]:
        """Split text into chunks respecting natural boundaries"""
        return self._recursive_split(text, self.separators)
    
    def _recursive_split(self, text: str, separators: List[str]) -> List[str]:
        if len(text) <= self.chunk_size:
            return [text.strip()] if text.strip() else []
        
        # Find the best separator that produces good chunks
        for sep in separators:
            if sep == "":
                # Last resort: hard split
                chunks = self._hard_split(text)
                return chunks
            
            if sep in text:
                parts = text.split(sep)
                chunks = []
                current_chunk = ""
                
                for part in parts:
                    candidate = current_chunk + sep + part if current_chunk else part
                    
                    if len(candidate) <= self.chunk_size:
                        current_chunk = candidate
                    else:
                        if current_chunk:
                            chunks.append(current_chunk.strip())
                        # If single part > chunk_size, recurse with smaller sep
                        if len(part) > self.chunk_size:
                            remaining_seps = separators[separators.index(sep)+1:]
                            sub_chunks = self._recursive_split(part, remaining_seps)
                            chunks.extend(sub_chunks)
                            current_chunk = ""
                        else:
                            current_chunk = part
                
                if current_chunk:
                    chunks.append(current_chunk.strip())
                
                # Add overlap
                return self._add_overlap(chunks)
        
        return [text]
    
    def _add_overlap(self, chunks: List[str]) -> List[str]:
        """Add overlap between consecutive chunks"""
        if self.chunk_overlap == 0:
            return chunks
        
        overlapped = []
        for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
            if i > 0:
                # Prepend end of previous chunk
                prev_end = chunks[i-1][-self.chunk_overlap:]
                chunk = prev_end + " " + chunk
            overlapped.append(chunk)
        return overlapped


class SemanticChunker:
    """Semantic chunking: split at topic boundaries
    
    Algorithm:
    1. Split into sentences
    2. Embed each sentence
    3. Compute cosine similarity between consecutive sentences
    4. Split where similarity drops below threshold (topic change)
    """
    
    def __init__(self, embedder, threshold: float = 0.5,
                 min_chunk_size: int = 100, max_chunk_size: int = 1000):
        self.embedder = embedder
        self.threshold = threshold
        self.min_chunk_size = min_chunk_size
        self.max_chunk_size = max_chunk_size
    
    def split(self, text: str) -> List[str]:
        sentences = self._split_sentences(text)
        if len(sentences) <= 1:
            return [text]
        
        # Embed all sentences
        embeddings = self.embedder.encode_batch(sentences)
        
        # Compute similarity between consecutive sentences
        similarities = []
        for i in range(len(embeddings) - 1):
            sim = np.dot(embeddings[i], embeddings[i+1]) / (
                np.linalg.norm(embeddings[i]) * np.linalg.norm(embeddings[i+1])
            )
            similarities.append(sim)
        
        # Find split points (where similarity drops)
        chunks = []
        current_chunk = [sentences[0]]
        
        for i, sim in enumerate(similarities):
            if sim < self.threshold and len(" ".join(current_chunk)) >= self.min_chunk_size:
                chunks.append(" ".join(current_chunk))
                current_chunk = [sentences[i+1]]
            else:
                current_chunk.append(sentences[i+1])
                # Force split if too large
                if len(" ".join(current_chunk)) > self.max_chunk_size:
                    chunks.append(" ".join(current_chunk))
                    current_chunk = []
        
        if current_chunk:
            chunks.append(" ".join(current_chunk))
        
        return chunks
    
    def _split_sentences(self, text: str) -> List[str]:
        return re.split(r'(?<=[.!?])\s+', text)

12.3 Embedding Models & Vector Databases

"""
Embedding Models (2025 MTEB Leaderboard):

┌──────────────────────────┬─────────┬──────────┬───────────────┐
│ Model                    │ Dim     │ Max Tok  │ MTEB Score    │
├──────────────────────────┼─────────┼──────────┼───────────────┤
│ text-embedding-3-large   │ 3072    │ 8191     │ 64.6          │
│ voyage-3                 │ 1024    │ 32000    │ 67.8          │
│ Cohere embed-v3          │ 1024    │ 512      │ 66.3          │
│ BGE-M3 (multilingual)    │ 1024    │ 8192     │ 65.1          │
│ GTE-Qwen2-7B            │ 3584    │ 32768    │ 70.2          │
│ NV-Embed-v2             │ 4096    │ 32768    │ 72.3          │
│ E5-Mistral-7B           │ 4096    │ 32768    │ 66.6          │
└──────────────────────────┴─────────┴──────────┴───────────────┘

Vector Database Comparison:

┌──────────────┬────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┐
│ DB           │ Index Type             │ Best For                    │
├──────────────┼────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────┤
│ FAISS        │ IVF, HNSW, PQ         │ Single-node, research       │
│ Pinecone     │ Proprietary (managed) │ Production SaaS, simple     │
│ Weaviate     │ HNSW + filtering      │ Hybrid search + filtering   │
│ Milvus       │ IVF, HNSW, DiskANN   │ Large scale, multi-modal    │
│ Qdrant       │ HNSW + payload filter │ Filtering-heavy workloads   │
│ ChromaDB     │ HNSW (small scale)    │ Prototyping, local dev      │
│ pgvector     │ IVF, HNSW             │ Existing PostgreSQL stack   │
└──────────────┴────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────┘
"""

class HNSWIndex:
    """Hierarchical Navigable Small World Graph (概念實作)
    
    HNSW = multi-layer skip-list + small world graph
    
    Key properties:
    - Build time: O(N * log(N))
    - Query time: O(log(N)) with high recall
    - Memory: O(N * M) where M = max connections per node
    - Tradeoffs: ef_construction (build quality), ef_search (query accuracy), M (memory)
    """
    
    def __init__(self, dim: int, max_elements: int,
                 M: int = 16, ef_construction: int = 200):
        """
        Args:
            dim: vector dimension
            max_elements: max capacity
            M: max connections per node (higher = better recall, more memory)
            ef_construction: build-time beam width (higher = better graph, slower build)
        """
        self.dim = dim
        self.M = M  # Bi-directional links per node per layer
        self.ef_construction = ef_construction
        self.max_level = int(np.log2(max_elements))
        
        # In production: use hnswlib or FAISS
        # self.index = hnswlib.Index(space='cosine', dim=dim)
    
    def search(self, query: np.ndarray, k: int = 10, 
               ef_search: int = 100) -> List[tuple]:
        """Approximate nearest neighbor search
        
        Args:
            ef_search: search beam width (higher = better recall, slower)
                      MUST be >= k
        
        Algorithm:
        1. Enter at top layer through entry point
        2. Greedy traverse each layer: move to nearest neighbor
        3. At layer 0: beam search with ef_search candidates
        4. Return top-k from candidates
        
        Recall vs speed tradeoff:
          ef_search=50:  recall@10 ~95%, latency ~1ms
          ef_search=200: recall@10 ~99%, latency ~3ms
          ef_search=500: recall@10 ~99.9%, latency ~8ms
        """
        pass  # Conceptual — use hnswlib in practice


class IVFIndex:
    """Inverted File Index (IVF) — cluster-based ANN
    
    Algorithm:
    1. Build: K-means cluster all vectors into N_list centroids
    2. Search: find N_probe nearest centroids, 
               only search vectors in those clusters
    
    Tradeoffs:
    - N_list: more clusters = faster search, more memory for centroids
    - N_probe: more probes = better recall, slower
    
    Typical: N_list = sqrt(N_vectors), N_probe = N_list * 0.05
    
    IVF + PQ (Product Quantization):
    - Compress vectors: 768-dim float32 → 64 bytes (12x compression)
    - Enables billion-scale search on single machine
    """
    
    def __init__(self, dim: int, n_list: int = 1024, 
                 n_probe: int = 32, use_pq: bool = False,
                 pq_m: int = 64):
        self.dim = dim
        self.n_list = n_list
        self.n_probe = n_probe
        
        # Build with FAISS:
        # if use_pq:
        #     quantizer = faiss.IndexFlatL2(dim)
        #     self.index = faiss.IndexIVFPQ(quantizer, dim, n_list, pq_m, 8)
        # else:
        #     quantizer = faiss.IndexFlatL2(dim)
        #     self.index = faiss.IndexIVFFlat(quantizer, dim, n_list)


# Hybrid Search: Vector + Keyword (BM25)
"""
純 vector search 的問題：
  - 稀有詞彙/專有名詞匹配差 (embedding 可能 miss exact matches)
  - 無法精確匹配 ID、數字、特定 pattern

解法：Hybrid = Vector similarity + BM25 keyword score

Score = α * vector_score + (1-α) * bm25_score

Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF):
  score(d) = Σ 1/(k + rank_i(d))  for each retrieval method
  k = 60 (typical constant)
  
  好處：不需要 normalize scores，直接合併排名
"""

def reciprocal_rank_fusion(rankings: List[List[str]], k: int = 60) -> List[tuple]:
    """Combine multiple ranking lists using RRF
    
    Args:
        rankings: list of ranked document id lists (each from different retriever)
        k: RRF constant (higher = less emphasis on top ranks)
    
    Returns:
        Fused ranking with scores
    """
    doc_scores = {}
    
    for ranking in rankings:
        for rank, doc_id in enumerate(ranking):
            if doc_id not in doc_scores:
                doc_scores[doc_id] = 0
            doc_scores[doc_id] += 1.0 / (k + rank + 1)
    
    # Sort by fused score
    fused = sorted(doc_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
    return fused

12.4 Reranking — 精確重排序

"""
Two-stage retrieval:
  Stage 1 (Bi-encoder): Fast approximate search → Top-100
  Stage 2 (Cross-encoder reranker): Accurate scoring → Top-5

Why two stages?
  - Bi-encoder: query and doc encoded independently → dot product (fast!)
    But: can't capture fine-grained query-doc interaction
    
  - Cross-encoder: [query, doc] together → full attention between them
    But: O(N) forward passes (can't pre-compute doc embeddings)
    
  Solution: use bi-encoder for recall, cross-encoder for precision
"""

class CrossEncoderReranker:
    """Cross-encoder reranker for RAG
    
    Models: Cohere Rerank, bge-reranker-v2, ms-marco-MiniLM
    
    Input: [CLS] query [SEP] document [SEP]
    Output: relevance score (0-1)
    """
    
    def __init__(self, model_name: str = "BAAI/bge-reranker-v2-m3"):
        # self.model = CrossEncoder(model_name)
        pass
    
    def rerank(self, query: str, candidates: List[RetrievalResult],
               top_k: int = 5) -> List[RetrievalResult]:
        """Rerank candidates using cross-encoder
        
        Typical latency: 50-100ms for 100 candidates on GPU
        """
        pairs = [(query, c.chunk.content) for c in candidates]
        
        # Cross-encoder scores all pairs
        # scores = self.model.predict(pairs)
        scores = [0.0] * len(pairs)  # placeholder
        
        # Sort by cross-encoder score
        scored_candidates = list(zip(candidates, scores))
        scored_candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
        
        results = []
        for candidate, score in scored_candidates[:top_k]:
            results.append(RetrievalResult(
                chunk=candidate.chunk, 
                score=score
            ))
        
        return results


class ColBERTReranker:
    """ColBERT: late interaction reranker
    
    Innovation: token-level matching instead of single vector
    
    Query: embed each token → [q1, q2, ..., qN]
    Doc: embed each token → [d1, d2, ..., dM]
    
    Score = Σᵢ max_j cos(qᵢ, dⱼ)  (MaxSim per query token)
    
    Advantage over cross-encoder:
    - Doc embeddings can be pre-computed (offline)
    - Only query tokens need online computation
    - 10-100x faster than cross-encoder with similar quality
    """
    pass

12.5 Advanced RAG Techniques

"""
Advanced RAG Techniques (2024-2025):

1. HyDE (Hypothetical Document Embeddings):
   Query → LLM generates hypothetical answer → Embed THAT for retrieval
   Why: Hypothetical doc is in same "space" as real docs (better match)

2. Multi-Query RAG:
   Original query → LLM generates N different phrasings
   Retrieve for each phrasing → Merge results (RRF)
   Why: Captures different aspects/interpretations of the query

3. Self-RAG (Self-Reflective RAG):
   LLM decides: (a) Do I need retrieval? (b) Is retrieved doc relevant?
   (c) Is my answer grounded in the doc? (d) Is my answer useful?
   Uses special tokens: [Retrieve], [IsRel], [IsSup], [IsUse]

4. Corrective RAG (CRAG):
   After retrieval, evaluate quality. If low confidence:
   → Trigger web search as fallback
   → Decompose query into sub-queries

5. Graph RAG:
   Build knowledge graph from documents
   Combine graph traversal + vector similarity
   Better for multi-hop reasoning ("Who is the CEO of X's parent company?")

6. Agentic RAG:
   LLM as agent: decides what to retrieve, when, how many hops
   Uses tool-calling: search(query), filter(criteria), summarize(docs)
"""

class SelfRAG:
    """Self-RAG: LLM decides when and how to use retrieval
    
    Special tokens trained into the model:
    - [Retrieve]: yes/no — should I retrieve?
    - [IsRel]: relevant/irrelevant — is this doc relevant to query?
    - [IsSup]: fully/partially/no support — does doc support answer?
    - [IsUse]: 1-5 scale — how useful is the overall response?
    """
    
    def __init__(self, llm, retriever):
        self.llm = llm
        self.retriever = retriever
    
    def generate(self, query: str) -> str:
        """Self-reflective generation with adaptive retrieval"""
        
        # Step 1: Ask model if retrieval is needed
        needs_retrieval = self._check_retrieval_needed(query)
        
        if not needs_retrieval:
            # Model is confident — generate directly
            return self.llm.generate(query)
        
        # Step 2: Retrieve
        docs = self.retriever.search(query, top_k=5)
        
        # Step 3: Filter relevant docs (model judges)
        relevant_docs = []
        for doc in docs:
            if self._is_relevant(query, doc):
                relevant_docs.append(doc)
        
        if not relevant_docs:
            # No relevant docs found — generate without context
            return self.llm.generate(query + "\n[No relevant context found]")
        
        # Step 4: Generate with context
        context = "\n".join([d.content for d in relevant_docs])
        response = self.llm.generate(
            f"Context: {context}\nQuestion: {query}\nAnswer:"
        )
        
        # Step 5: Check if response is grounded
        is_supported = self._check_grounding(response, relevant_docs)
        if not is_supported:
            # Regenerate or add caveat
            response += "\n[Note: This answer may not be fully supported by retrieved documents]"
        
        return response
    
    def _check_retrieval_needed(self, query: str) -> bool:
        """Model predicts [Retrieve] token probability"""
        # In practice: check logit of [Retrieve=Yes] token
        return True  # Simplified
    
    def _is_relevant(self, query: str, doc) -> bool:
        """Model predicts [IsRel] for query-doc pair"""
        return True  # Simplified
    
    def _check_grounding(self, response: str, docs: list) -> bool:
        """Model predicts [IsSup] — is response supported by docs?"""
        return True  # Simplified


class AgenticRAG:
    """Multi-hop RAG with tool-calling agent
    
    For complex questions requiring multiple retrieval steps:
    "What was the revenue of the company that acquired Twitter?"
    → Step 1: "Who acquired Twitter?" → Retrieve → "Elon Musk / X Corp"
    → Step 2: "What is X Corp's revenue?" → Retrieve → "$..." 
    """
    
    def __init__(self, llm, tools: dict):
        self.llm = llm
        self.tools = tools  # {"search": vector_search, "web": web_search, ...}
    
    def solve(self, query: str, max_steps: int = 5) -> str:
        """Agent loop: think → act → observe → repeat"""
        history = [{"role": "user", "content": query}]
        
        for step in range(max_steps):
            # Think: what tool to use?
            response = self.llm.generate_with_tools(
                history, available_tools=list(self.tools.keys())
            )
            
            if response.is_final_answer:
                return response.content
            
            # Act: call the tool
            tool_name = response.tool_call.name
            tool_args = response.tool_call.arguments
            observation = self.tools[tool_name](**tool_args)
            
            # Observe: add result to history
            history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": response.reasoning})
            history.append({"role": "tool", "content": str(observation)})
        
        return "Could not find answer within step limit."

12.6 RAG 評估與優化

"""
RAG Evaluation Framework:

┌─────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Metric              │ What it measures                          │
├─────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Retrieval Recall@K  │ 正確文件是否在 Top-K 中?                  │
│ Retrieval MRR       │ 正確文件排在第幾?                         │
│ Context Relevance   │ 檢索到的 context 與 query 相關嗎?         │
│ Faithfulness        │ Answer 是否忠於 context (不幻覺)?         │
│ Answer Relevance    │ Answer 是否回答了 question?               │
│ Answer Correctness  │ Answer 是否事實正確?                      │
└─────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────┘

RAGAS Framework metrics:
- Faithfulness = # claims supported by context / total claims
- Answer Relevance = semantic similarity(answer, question)  
- Context Precision = relevant chunks in top-k / k
- Context Recall = # relevant facts retrieved / total relevant facts
"""

class RAGEvaluator:
    """Evaluate RAG pipeline quality"""
    
    def __init__(self, judge_llm):
        self.judge = judge_llm
    
    def evaluate_faithfulness(self, answer: str, context: str) -> float:
        """Is the answer grounded in the provided context?
        
        Score 0-1: fraction of answer claims supported by context
        """
        # Extract claims from answer
        claims_prompt = f"List all factual claims in this answer:\n{answer}"
        claims = self.judge.generate(claims_prompt)
        
        # Check each claim against context
        supported = 0
        total = len(claims)
        for claim in claims:
            check_prompt = f"""Is this claim supported by the context?
            Claim: {claim}
            Context: {context}
            Answer: Yes or No"""
            result = self.judge.generate(check_prompt)
            if "yes" in result.lower():
                supported += 1
        
        return supported / max(total, 1)
    
    def evaluate_retrieval_recall(self, retrieved_ids: List[str],
                                   ground_truth_ids: List[str]) -> float:
        """Recall@K: fraction of relevant docs retrieved"""
        retrieved_set = set(retrieved_ids)
        relevant_set = set(ground_truth_ids)
        
        if not relevant_set:
            return 1.0
        
        return len(retrieved_set & relevant_set) / len(relevant_set)


# Common RAG Failure Modes & Fixes:
"""
┌──────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Problem                  │ Solution                              │
├──────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Retrieved wrong docs     │ Better chunking, hybrid search, HyDE │
│ Right docs but wrong ans │ Better prompt, reranking, more context│
│ Hallucination            │ Citation, faithfulness check, self-RAG│
│ Outdated information     │ Incremental indexing, TTL on chunks   │
│ Multi-hop fails          │ Agentic RAG, query decomposition     │
│ Terminology mismatch     │ Synonym expansion, domain fine-tuning │
│ Too much context noise   │ Aggressive reranking, smaller top-k  │
│ No relevant docs exist   │ Confidence scoring, "I don't know"   │
└──────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────┘
"""

📖 術語表

術語英文白話解釋生活比喻
RAGRetrieval-Augmented Generation先查資料再回答問題的 AI 系統開卷考試：可以翻書再答題
Embedding—把文字轉成向量數字表示把每本書轉成 GPS 座標放在地圖上
Vector DB向量資料庫專門存儲和搜尋向量的資料庫按 GPS 距離查找最近的書的圖書館
HNSWHierarchical Navigable Small World多層圖結構的近似最近鄰搜尋高速公路→省道→鄉道的分層導航
IVFInverted File Index先找最近的群集再精確搜尋先找到對的書架區域，再找具體的書
Chunking文件切片把長文件切成適合搜尋的小段落把整本書撕成一頁一頁方便索引
Reranker重排序器精確評估 query-doc 相關性重排結果初選 100 人→精選面試 5 人
Cross-encoder—把 query+doc 一起輸入模型打分把兩份文件並排比較（精確但慢）
Bi-encoder—query 和 doc 各自獨立編碼再比距離各自拍照後比照片像不像（快但粗）
HyDEHypothetical Document Embeddings先讓 LLM 猜答案，再用答案去搜尋先想像正確答案長什麼樣，再去找類似的
Self-RAG—LLM 自己決定是否需要查資料學生自己判斷要不要翻書（不是每題都翻）
Grounding接地/忠實度確保回答基於檢索到的資料確保答案有書本根據，不是瞎編的

🧪 驗證考題

Q1: 為什麼 RAG 需要 chunking？直接把整篇文件當作一個 vector 不行嗎？

答案

(1) Embedding 精度：把整篇 10 頁文件壓縮成一個 1024 維向量會丟失太多細節——所有段落的語意被平均化，具體資訊被稀釋。(2) Context window：LLM 有 context 限制（4K-128K），如果 retrieve 整篇文件可能超出。(3) Noise：文件中只有 1-2 段與 query 相關，送整篇會引入大量無關內容干擾 LLM。(4) 多文件需求：答案可能跨多個文件的不同段落，需要 fine-grained retrieval。最佳 chunk size：256-1024 tokens，視 embedding model 能力和 LLM context 長度而定。

Q2: HNSW 和 IVF 各自的 trade-off 是什麼？什麼場景用哪個？

答案

HNSW：高 recall（>99%）、低延遲（<5ms），但記憶體高（整個 graph + 原始 vectors 都在 RAM）。適合：dataset < 10M vectors，latency-critical 場景。IVF(+PQ)：記憶體低（PQ 壓縮 10-30x），支持 billion-scale，但 recall 稍低（95-98%），延遲稍高。適合：超大 dataset、記憶體有限、可接受稍低 recall。混合：IVF-HNSW = IVF 做 cluster 層，每個 cluster 內用 HNSW → 兼顧 scale 和 accuracy。

Q3: HyDE 為什麼比直接 embed query 效果好？什麼情況下反而更差？

答案

為什麼好：Query 通常很短且用「問題語氣」(e.g., "What is X?")，而文件用「陳述語氣」(e.g., "X is...")。兩者在 embedding space 中距離較遠。HyDE 讓 LLM 生成假設答案（陳述語氣），跟文件在同一個語意空間→更好的 cosine similarity。什麼時候差：(1) LLM 的假設答案完全錯誤（把搜尋帶到錯誤方向）；(2) 事實性 lookup query ("What's Apple's stock price?") 不需要語意匹配；(3) 增加一次 LLM call 的延遲（TTFT 多 200-500ms）。

Q4: Reranker 和 embedding similarity 的核心差異是什麼？

答案

Bi-encoder (embedding)：query 和 doc 各自獨立編碼成 vectors，用 dot product 比較。好處是 doc embeddings 可以 precompute + cache（indexing 一次，query O(1) 比較）。壞處是 query 和 doc 之間沒有直接 token-level interaction。Cross-encoder (reranker)：[CLS] query [SEP] doc [SEP] 一起通過 model，所有 query-doc token pairs 做 full attention。好處是捕捉精確的語意關係（word overlap, negation, context）。壞處是 O(N) forward passes（不能 precompute）。所以 reranker 只用在已經 retrieve 的 top-K 結果上（K=50-100）。

Q5: 設計一個企業知識庫 RAG 系統，支持 50K 內部文件、100 QPS。

答案

Indexing：50K docs × avg 5 chunks × 1024-dim = 250K vectors → 單節點 HNSW 輕鬆搞定（~1 GB RAM）。Chunking: recursive 512 tokens + 50 overlap。Embedding: BGE-M3（multilingual）。
Online：Query → embed (10ms) → HNSW search top-50 (2ms) → Cross-encoder rerank top-5 (50ms) → LLM generate (500ms) → Total P95 < 800ms。
Scale：100 QPS × 600ms per request = 60 concurrent → 需要 ~4 LLM instances (15 QPS each)。Vector search 和 reranker 在 1 GPU 上可以 handle all traffic。
Update：Incremental indexing（新文件即時加入），stale detection（>6 months 自動 flag）。
特殊考慮：ACL filtering（只返回 user 有權限看的文件），citation（標註來源段落+頁碼）。

🎯 Follow-up Q&A

F1: 如何處理 RAG 中的 hallucination（幻覺）問題？

多層防禦：(1) Prompt engineering: "Only answer based on provided context. If unsure, say I don't know." (2) Citation enforcement: 要求 LLM 標註每句話的來源 chunk，無法標註的 = 幻覺。(3) Self-RAG / CRAG: 訓練模型 output [IsSup] token 自我檢查。(4) Post-hoc verification: 生成後用 NLI model 檢查 answer 是否被 context entail。(5) Confidence scoring: 如果 retrieval score < threshold，回覆 "I'm not sure" 而非猜測。(6) Structured output: 讓模型 output JSON with answer + confidence + sources 欄位。

F2: Multi-modal RAG (圖片+表格) 如何設計？

(1) 表格：轉成 markdown text 後正常 chunk/embed；或用 ColPali (multi-modal embedding) 把表格截圖直接 embed。(2) 圖片：CLIP/SigLIP embed 圖片為 vector；或用 VLM (GPT-4V) 生成圖片描述再 embed text。(3) PDFs：Layout-aware extraction (Table transformer, PDF parser) 保留結構資訊。(4) 混合 retrieval：text query → 同時搜 text vectors 和 image vectors，合併結果送 multimodal LLM。(5) 新趨勢 (2025)：ColPali/ColQwen 直接 embed PDF page images，不需要 OCR/parsing。

F3: Graph RAG vs Vector RAG，何時用哪個？

Vector RAG：適合 "what" questions（事實查找）—— "What is X?" 直接語意匹配找到答案段落。Graph RAG：適合 "how/why" 和 multi-hop questions —— "How are X and Y related?" 需要沿著 entity relationships traverse。具體場景：企業知識庫（多數是 factoid questions）→ Vector；生物醫學文獻（drug-gene-disease 關係）→ Graph；法律案例分析（案例間引用關係）→ Graph + Vector hybrid。Microsoft Graph RAG (2024)：用 LLM 從文件中抽取 entity-relationship，build knowledge graph，query 時 graph traversal + community summarization。

F4: 如何優化 RAG 的延遲？目標 P99 < 500ms。

(1) Embedding cache: 高頻 queries cache embedding + retrieval results (Redis)。(2) Streaming: LLM 生成部分即返回（TTFT < 200ms），不等完整答案。(3) Parallel retrieval: vector search + BM25 + reranker 用 async/parallel 執行而非 sequential。(4) Pre-computed answers: 高頻問題直接 cache 完整答案（0ms retrieval）。(5) Smaller reranker: 用 MiniLM 級別 reranker (30ms vs 100ms for large models)。(6) Skip reranker: 如果 vector top-1 score > high threshold，直接用（skip rerank）。(7) GPU quantization: INT8 embedding model + INT4 reranker。Breakdown target: embed 10ms + search 3ms + rerank 30ms + LLM TTFT 200ms = 243ms P50。

F5: RAG vs Long-context LLM (128K+)，各自的優劣？

RAG 優勢：(1) 可搜尋百萬文件（scale 不受 context 限制）；(2) 更新即時（新文件秒級可搜）；(3) 成本低（只送 5 chunks 到 LLM vs 送 128K tokens）；(4) Citation 明確（知道答案來自哪個文件）。Long-context 優勢：(1) 不需要 chunking/embedding pipeline（簡單）；(2) 全文理解（不會 miss cross-chunk info）；(3) 不會因為 retrieval error 丟失答案。最佳實踐 (2025)：混合——RAG retrieve top-20 chunks（~10K tokens），放入 long-context LLM（128K），讓 LLM 在更豐富的 context 中生成。這比只送 top-5 好很多（retrieval recall 問題被緩解）。

F6: 如何做 RAG 的 A/B testing 和 continuous improvement？

(1) Offline eval: 建立 golden dataset（200+ query/answer/source 三元組），定期跑 RAGAS metrics。(2) Online metrics: user thumbs up/down rate, follow-up question rate (越少越好), citation click rate。(3) A/B test dimensions: chunk size、embedding model、reranker、top-k、prompt template 各 A/B 獨立測試。(4) Feedback loop: user 糾正的答案 → 加入 ground truth dataset → retune embedding model (contrastive learning on hard negatives)。(5) Monitoring: retrieval score distribution drift, answer length anomalies, latency P99。(6) Auto-improvement: LLM-as-judge 自動標註 faithfulness，低分 samples 進入 human review queue。

AI/ML System Design Interview Book · Google L5 · Traditional Chinese

Chapter 13: CI/CD for ML（機器學習的持續整合與持續部署）

🌱 從零開始理解

生活比喻

想像你經營一家餐廳。傳統軟體的 CI/CD 就像確認廚房設備正常運作、食材新鮮、菜單正確印刷。但 ML 的 CI/CD 更像是：你不僅要確認廚房運作，還要確認食材品質（資料驗證）、新菜色口味測試（模型驗證）、試吃反饋（影子部署），最後才正式上菜單（生產部署）。

白話解釋

ML CI/CD = 自動化流程，確保每次模型更新都經過：資料品質檢查 → 模型訓練 → 模型評估 → 安全部署 → 監控回饋。與傳統 CI/CD 的差異在於：ML 有「資料」和「模型」兩個額外的變動維度。

為什麼工作上需要

  • Google 每天部署數千個模型更新，沒有自動化 pipeline 不可能維護
  • 資料品質問題是 ML 系統最常見的故障源（Google 論文指出 60%+ 的 ML 故障來自資料）
  • 手動部署模型容易出錯且無法回滾

先備知識

  • 基本 CI/CD 概念（GitHub Actions / Jenkins）
  • ML 訓練流程（train/eval/serve）
  • Docker 容器基礎
  • 版本控制（Git）

📚 核心內容

13.1 ML CI/CD vs 傳統 CI/CD

維度傳統 CI/CDML CI/CD
版本控制對象程式碼程式碼 + 資料 + 模型 + 超參數
測試單元/整合測試+ 資料驗證 + 模型品質測試
建置產出二進位檔/容器模型 artifact + serving 容器
部署驗證健康檢查+ A/B test + shadow mode + canary
回滾上一版程式碼上一版模型 + 可能需要資料回滾

13.2 資料驗證（Data Validation）

為什麼重要：「Garbage in, garbage out」。如果訓練資料有問題，模型一定有問題。Google 的 TFX 團隊發現，大多數 ML pipeline 故障可追溯到資料問題。

Great Expectations 資料驗證框架

import great_expectations as gx

# 建立資料驗證 suite
context = gx.get_context()

# 定義期望（expectations）
validator = context.sources.pandas_default.read_csv("training_data.csv")

# Schema 驗證：確認欄位存在且型別正確
validator.expect_column_to_exist("user_id")
validator.expect_column_to_exist("click_through_rate")
validator.expect_column_values_to_be_between(
    "click_through_rate", min_value=0.0, max_value=1.0
)

# 統計驗證：確認分佈沒有偏移
validator.expect_column_mean_to_be_between(
    "click_through_rate", min_value=0.02, max_value=0.08
)
validator.expect_column_proportion_of_unique_values_to_be_between(
    "user_id", min_value=0.8, max_value=1.0
)

# Null 值檢查
validator.expect_column_values_to_not_be_null("user_id")
validator.expect_column_values_to_not_be_null("timestamp")

# 資料量檢查（防止上游 pipeline 靜默失敗）
validator.expect_table_row_count_to_be_between(
    min_value=100000, max_value=10000000
)

# 執行驗證
results = validator.validate()
if not results.success:
    raise DataValidationError(f"Data validation failed: {results}")

TensorFlow Data Validation (TFDV)

import tensorflow_data_validation as tfdv

# 從訓練資料生成 schema（基線）
train_stats = tfdv.generate_statistics_from_csv("train.csv")
schema = tfdv.infer_schema(train_stats)

# 對新資料做驗證
new_stats = tfdv.generate_statistics_from_csv("new_batch.csv")
anomalies = tfdv.validate_statistics(
    statistics=new_stats, 
    schema=schema
)

# 檢查異常
if anomalies.anomaly_info:
    for feature, anomaly in anomalies.anomaly_info.items():
        print(f"⚠️ Feature '{feature}': {anomaly.description}")
        # 例如：feature 'age' 出現負值
        # 例如：feature 'country' 出現新類別 'XX'
    raise DataAnomalyError("Schema violations detected")

13.3 DVC（Data Version Control）

核心概念：Git 管理程式碼版本，DVC 管理資料和模型版本。DVC 在 Git repo 中只存 metadata（.dvc 檔案），實際大檔案存在遠端 storage（S3/GCS）。

# DVC Pipeline 定義 (dvc.yaml)
"""
stages:
  prepare:
    cmd: python src/prepare.py
    deps:
      - src/prepare.py
      - data/raw/
    outs:
      - data/prepared/
    params:
      - prepare.split_ratio
      
  train:
    cmd: python src/train.py
    deps:
      - src/train.py
      - data/prepared/
    outs:
      - models/model.pkl
    params:
      - train.learning_rate
      - train.n_estimators
    metrics:
      - metrics/train_metrics.json:
          cache: false
          
  evaluate:
    cmd: python src/evaluate.py
    deps:
      - src/evaluate.py
      - models/model.pkl
      - data/prepared/test.csv
    metrics:
      - metrics/eval_metrics.json:
          cache: false
    plots:
      - plots/confusion_matrix.csv
"""

# Python 中程式化使用 DVC
import subprocess
import json

def run_dvc_pipeline():
    """執行完整 DVC pipeline 並檢查指標"""
    # 重現 pipeline
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["dvc", "repro"], capture_output=True, text=True
    )
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise PipelineError(f"DVC pipeline failed: {result.stderr}")
    
    # 比較指標與上一版
    diff_result = subprocess.run(
        ["dvc", "metrics", "diff"], capture_output=True, text=True
    )
    metrics_diff = json.loads(diff_result.stdout)
    
    # 自動品質門檻
    current_metrics = json.load(open("metrics/eval_metrics.json"))
    if current_metrics["auc_roc"] < 0.85:
        raise QualityGateError(
            f"AUC-ROC {current_metrics['auc_roc']:.4f} < threshold 0.85"
        )
    
    return current_metrics

13.4 MLflow 實驗追蹤與模型 Registry

import mlflow
import mlflow.sklearn
from sklearn.ensemble import GradientBoostingClassifier
from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score, precision_score, recall_score

# 設定 MLflow tracking server
mlflow.set_tracking_uri("http://mlflow-server:5000")
mlflow.set_experiment("recommendation_model_v2")

def train_and_log_model(X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, params):
    """訓練模型並記錄到 MLflow"""
    with mlflow.start_run(run_name=f"gbm_lr{params['learning_rate']}") as run:
        # 記錄參數
        mlflow.log_params(params)
        
        # 記錄資料版本（DVC commit hash）
        import subprocess
        dvc_hash = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "log", "-1", "--format=%H", "--", "data/prepared.dvc"]
        ).decode().strip()
        mlflow.log_param("data_version", dvc_hash)
        
        # 訓練
        model = GradientBoostingClassifier(**params)
        model.fit(X_train, y_train)
        
        # 評估
        y_pred = model.predict(X_test)
        y_prob = model.predict_proba(X_test)[:, 1]
        
        metrics = {
            "auc_roc": roc_auc_score(y_test, y_prob),
            "precision": precision_score(y_test, y_pred),
            "recall": recall_score(y_test, y_pred),
            "f1": 2 * precision_score(y_test, y_pred) * recall_score(y_test, y_pred) 
                  / (precision_score(y_test, y_pred) + recall_score(y_test, y_pred)),
        }
        mlflow.log_metrics(metrics)
        
        # 記錄模型 artifact
        mlflow.sklearn.log_model(
            model, 
            "model",
            registered_model_name="recommendation_model"
        )
        
        # 自動升級到 Staging（如果通過品質門檻）
        if metrics["auc_roc"] > 0.90:
            client = mlflow.tracking.MlflowClient()
            latest_version = client.get_latest_versions(
                "recommendation_model", stages=["None"]
            )[0]
            client.transition_model_version_stage(
                name="recommendation_model",
                version=latest_version.version,
                stage="Staging"
            )
            print(f"✅ Model v{latest_version.version} promoted to Staging")
        
        return run.info.run_id, metrics

13.5 Canary Deployment（金絲雀部署）

概念：先把新模型部署到一小部分流量（1-5%），觀察指標正常後逐步擴大，出問題立即回滾。

import numpy as np
from scipy import stats
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Optional
import time

@dataclass
class CanaryConfig:
    initial_traffic_pct: float = 0.01  # 1% 起步
    max_traffic_pct: float = 1.0
    step_size: float = 0.05  # 每步增加 5%
    observation_window_sec: int = 3600  # 每步觀察 1 小時
    rollback_threshold: float = 0.05  # 指標退化 > 5% 就回滾
    min_samples: int = 1000  # 最少觀察樣本數

@dataclass  
class CanaryMetrics:
    latency_p99_ms: float
    error_rate: float
    prediction_quality: float  # e.g., CTR, conversion
    sample_count: int

class CanaryDeployer:
    def __init__(self, config: CanaryConfig):
        self.config = config
        self.current_traffic_pct = 0.0
        self.baseline_metrics: Optional[CanaryMetrics] = None
    
    def start_canary(self, model_version: str):
        """啟動金絲雀部署"""
        self.current_traffic_pct = self.config.initial_traffic_pct
        self._update_traffic_split(model_version, self.current_traffic_pct)
        print(f"🐤 Canary started: {model_version} at {self.current_traffic_pct*100:.1f}%")
        
    def evaluate_and_promote(self, canary_metrics: CanaryMetrics) -> str:
        """評估金絲雀指標，決定下一步"""
        if canary_metrics.sample_count < self.config.min_samples:
            return "WAIT"  # 樣本不足，繼續等
        
        if self.baseline_metrics is None:
            raise ValueError("Baseline metrics not set")
        
        # 檢查延遲退化
        latency_degradation = (
            (canary_metrics.latency_p99_ms - self.baseline_metrics.latency_p99_ms) 
            / self.baseline_metrics.latency_p99_ms
        )
        if latency_degradation > self.config.rollback_threshold:
            return "ROLLBACK"
        
        # 檢查錯誤率
        if canary_metrics.error_rate > self.baseline_metrics.error_rate * 1.5:
            return "ROLLBACK"
        
        # 檢查預測品質（統計顯著性檢定）
        quality_change = (
            (canary_metrics.prediction_quality - self.baseline_metrics.prediction_quality)
            / self.baseline_metrics.prediction_quality
        )
        if quality_change < -self.config.rollback_threshold:
            return "ROLLBACK"
        
        # 通過所有檢查 → 增加流量
        self.current_traffic_pct = min(
            self.current_traffic_pct + self.config.step_size,
            self.config.max_traffic_pct
        )
        
        if self.current_traffic_pct >= self.config.max_traffic_pct:
            return "PROMOTE"  # 全量部署
        
        return "ADVANCE"  # 繼續增加流量
    
    def _update_traffic_split(self, model_version: str, pct: float):
        """更新流量分配（實際中透過 service mesh / load balancer）"""
        print(f"  Traffic split: canary={pct*100:.1f}%, baseline={100-pct*100:.1f}%")

13.6 Shadow Deployment（影子部署）

概念：新模型接收真實流量並產生預測，但結果不會被使用。同時記錄新舊模型的預測結果，離線比較。適用於高風險場景（金融、醫療）。

import asyncio
from typing import Any, Dict
import logging

class ShadowDeployment:
    """影子部署：新模型同步推論但不影響使用者"""
    
    def __init__(self, primary_model, shadow_model, logger):
        self.primary = primary_model
        self.shadow = shadow_model
        self.logger = logger
        self.comparison_results = []
    
    async def predict(self, features: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        """主模型回應使用者，影子模型靜默執行"""
        # 主模型（使用者看到的結果）
        primary_task = asyncio.create_task(
            self._predict_primary(features)
        )
        # 影子模型（靜默執行，不影響使用者）
        shadow_task = asyncio.create_task(
            self._predict_shadow(features)
        )
        
        # 等待主模型結果（使用者延遲不受影子模型影響）
        primary_result = await primary_task
        
        # 非同步等待影子模型（不阻塞回應）
        asyncio.create_task(
            self._compare_results(features, primary_result, shadow_task)
        )
        
        return primary_result
    
    async def _predict_primary(self, features):
        return self.primary.predict(features)
    
    async def _predict_shadow(self, features):
        try:
            return self.shadow.predict(features)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"Shadow model error: {e}")
            return None
    
    async def _compare_results(self, features, primary_result, shadow_task):
        """離線比較兩個模型的結果"""
        try:
            shadow_result = await asyncio.wait_for(shadow_task, timeout=5.0)
            if shadow_result is not None:
                comparison = {
                    "features_hash": hash(str(features)),
                    "primary_prediction": primary_result,
                    "shadow_prediction": shadow_result,
                    "agreement": primary_result == shadow_result,
                }
                self.comparison_results.append(comparison)
                
                # 定期輸出比較報告
                if len(self.comparison_results) % 1000 == 0:
                    agreement_rate = sum(
                        1 for c in self.comparison_results[-1000:] if c["agreement"]
                    ) / 1000
                    self.logger.info(
                        f"Shadow agreement rate (last 1K): {agreement_rate:.3f}"
                    )
        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
            self.logger.warning("Shadow model timed out")

13.7 完整 ML CI/CD Pipeline（GitHub Actions 範例）

# 以 Python 表示 CI/CD pipeline 邏輯
# 實際部署用 GitHub Actions YAML / Argo Workflows

class MLCICDPipeline:
    """完整 ML CI/CD Pipeline 流程"""
    
    STAGES = [
        "data_validation",
        "code_quality", 
        "unit_tests",
        "training",
        "model_evaluation",
        "integration_tests",
        "shadow_deployment",
        "canary_deployment",
        "full_rollout"
    ]
    
    def run_pipeline(self, trigger: str):
        """
        觸發條件：
        - code change → 完整 pipeline
        - data change → 從 data_validation 開始
        - schedule    → 從 training 開始（定期重訓練）
        """
        results = {}
        
        # Stage 1: 資料驗證
        results["data_validation"] = self.validate_data()
        if not results["data_validation"]["passed"]:
            self.alert_data_team(results["data_validation"])
            return "FAILED_DATA_VALIDATION"
