│  │  ├── Monitoring & observability                          │    │
│  │  └── ML platform tooling (MLflow, Kubeflow, etc.)       │    │
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │
│                                                                   │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

TCO Calculator 實現

"""
ML Total Cost of Ownership (TCO) Calculator
幫助面試中快速估算和比較不同方案的成本
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from enum import Enum
import math

class PricingTier(Enum):
    ON_DEMAND = "on_demand"
    SPOT = "spot"
    RESERVED_1YR = "reserved_1yr"
    RESERVED_3YR = "reserved_3yr"
    SAVINGS_PLAN = "savings_plan"

@dataclass
class GPUInstance:
    name: str
    gpu_model: str
    num_gpus: int
    gpu_memory_gb: int
    on_demand_hourly: float
    spot_hourly: float  # 平均 spot 價格
    reserved_1yr_hourly: float
    reserved_3yr_hourly: float
    fp16_tflops: float
    memory_bandwidth_gbps: float

# 2024-2025 常見 GPU 實例定價（us-east-1, Linux）
GPU_CATALOG = {
    "p4d.24xlarge": GPUInstance(
        name="p4d.24xlarge", gpu_model="A100-40GB", num_gpus=8,
        gpu_memory_gb=320, on_demand_hourly=32.77, spot_hourly=12.0,
        reserved_1yr_hourly=20.47, reserved_3yr_hourly=13.99,
        fp16_tflops=2496, memory_bandwidth_gbps=16000
    ),
    "p5.48xlarge": GPUInstance(
        name="p5.48xlarge", gpu_model="H100-80GB", num_gpus=8,
        gpu_memory_gb=640, on_demand_hourly=98.32, spot_hourly=35.0,
        reserved_1yr_hourly=61.40, reserved_3yr_hourly=43.10,
        fp16_tflops=7916, memory_bandwidth_gbps=26000
    ),
    "g5.xlarge": GPUInstance(
        name="g5.xlarge", gpu_model="A10G", num_gpus=1,
        gpu_memory_gb=24, on_demand_hourly=1.006, spot_hourly=0.35,
        reserved_1yr_hourly=0.63, reserved_3yr_hourly=0.44,
        fp16_tflops=125, memory_bandwidth_gbps=600
    ),
    "g6.xlarge": GPUInstance(
        name="g6.xlarge", gpu_model="L4", num_gpus=1,
        gpu_memory_gb=24, on_demand_hourly=0.805, spot_hourly=0.28,
        reserved_1yr_hourly=0.50, reserved_3yr_hourly=0.35,
        fp16_tflops=121, memory_bandwidth_gbps=300
    ),
    "inf2.xlarge": GPUInstance(
        name="inf2.xlarge", gpu_model="Inferentia2", num_gpus=1,
        gpu_memory_gb=32, on_demand_hourly=0.758, spot_hourly=0.27,
        reserved_1yr_hourly=0.47, reserved_3yr_hourly=0.33,
        fp16_tflops=190, memory_bandwidth_gbps=400  # 推論專用
    ),
}


class MLCostCalculator:
    """
    面試用 TCO 快速估算工具
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.catalog = GPU_CATALOG
    
    # ==========================================
    # 訓練成本估算
    # ==========================================
    
    def estimate_training_cost(self, 
                              model_params_billions: float,
                              training_tokens_trillions: float,
                              gpu_type: str = "p5.48xlarge",
                              mfu: float = 0.40,
                              pricing: PricingTier = PricingTier.SPOT,
                              target_days: Optional[int] = None) -> dict:
        """
        估算 LLM 預訓練成本
        
        Chinchilla Scaling Law:
        Training FLOP ≈ 6 × params × tokens
        
        參數：
        - model_params_billions: 模型參數量（B）
        - training_tokens_trillions: 訓練 token 數（T）
        - mfu: Model FLOP Utilization（H100 目前最佳 ~45-55%）
        - target_days: 目標訓練天數（如果指定，計算所需 GPU 數）
        """
        instance = self.catalog[gpu_type]
        
        # 總計算量
        total_flops = 6 * model_params_billions * 1e9 * training_tokens_trillions * 1e12
        
        # 單 GPU 每秒有效 FLOP
        effective_flops_per_gpu = instance.fp16_tflops * 1e12 * mfu / instance.num_gpus
        
        # 總 GPU-seconds 需求
        total_gpu_seconds = total_flops / effective_flops_per_gpu
        total_gpu_hours = total_gpu_seconds / 3600
        
        # 如果指定天數，計算需要多少 instance
        if target_days:
            total_instance_hours = total_gpu_hours / instance.num_gpus
            instances_needed = math.ceil(
                total_instance_hours / (target_days * 24)
            )
            total_gpus = instances_needed * instance.num_gpus
        else:
            instances_needed = None
            total_gpus = None
        
        # 成本（根據定價層級）
        hourly_rate = {
            PricingTier.ON_DEMAND: instance.on_demand_hourly,
            PricingTier.SPOT: instance.spot_hourly,
            PricingTier.RESERVED_1YR: instance.reserved_1yr_hourly,
            PricingTier.RESERVED_3YR: instance.reserved_3yr_hourly,
        }[pricing]
        
        instance_hours = total_gpu_hours / instance.num_gpus
        total_cost = instance_hours * hourly_rate
        
        # 加上額外成本 (storage, networking, failed runs)
        overhead_multiplier = 1.3  # 30% overhead for failures, storage, etc.
        
        return {
            "model_size": f"{model_params_billions}B",
            "training_tokens": f"{training_tokens_trillions}T",
            "total_flops": f"{total_flops:.2e}",
            "gpu_type": gpu_type,
            "mfu": f"{mfu*100:.0f}%",
            "pricing_tier": pricing.value,
            "total_gpu_hours": f"{total_gpu_hours:,.0f}",
            "instance_hours": f"{instance_hours:,.0f}",
            "compute_cost": f"${total_cost:,.0f}",
            "total_cost_with_overhead": f"${total_cost * overhead_multiplier:,.0f}",
            "instances_needed": instances_needed,
            "total_gpus": total_gpus,
            "target_days": target_days,
            "cost_per_billion_tokens": f"${total_cost / (training_tokens_trillions * 1000):,.0f}",
        }
    
    # ==========================================
    # 推論成本估算
    # ==========================================
    
    def estimate_inference_cost(self,
                               model_params_billions: float,
                               requests_per_day: int,
                               avg_input_tokens: int = 500,
                               avg_output_tokens: int = 200,
                               target_latency_ms: int = 2000,
                               gpu_type: str = "g5.xlarge") -> dict:
        """
        估算 LLM 推論服務月成本
        
        關鍵考量：
        1. 每個 request 的計算量 = prefill + decode
        2. Batch size 影響 throughput（越大越高效但 latency 增加）
        3. KV cache 記憶體是瓶頸（決定最大 concurrent requests）
        """
        instance = self.catalog[gpu_type]
        
        # KV Cache 計算
        # 每 token KV cache = 2 * num_layers * hidden_dim * 2 bytes (FP16)
        # 粗估：~1 MB per 1000 tokens for 7B model
        kv_cache_per_request_mb = (
            (avg_input_tokens + avg_output_tokens) * 
            model_params_billions * 0.15  # 經驗係數
        )
        
        # 每 GPU 可同時服務的 request 數（受 GPU memory 限制）
        model_memory_gb = model_params_billions * 2  # FP16
        available_for_kv = (instance.gpu_memory_gb - model_memory_gb) * 1024  # MB
        max_concurrent = int(available_for_kv / kv_cache_per_request_mb)
        
        # Throughput 估算 (tokens/second per GPU)
        # Decode throughput ≈ memory_bandwidth / (2 * model_params * bytes_per_param)
        # 因為 decode 是 memory-bound
        decode_tokens_per_sec = (
            instance.memory_bandwidth_gbps * 1e9 / 
            (2 * model_params_billions * 1e9 * 2)  # 2 bytes FP16
        )
        
        # 考慮 batching: throughput ∝ batch_size (up to compute bound)
        effective_batch_size = min(max_concurrent, 32)  # practical limit
        batch_throughput = decode_tokens_per_sec * min(effective_batch_size, 8)
        
        # 每 request 處理時間
        time_per_request_ms = (avg_output_tokens / batch_throughput) * 1000
        
        # 需要的 GPU 數量
        requests_per_second = requests_per_day / 86400
        gpus_needed_throughput = math.ceil(
            requests_per_second * (time_per_request_ms / 1000)
        )
        gpus_needed_latency = math.ceil(
            requests_per_second / (1000 / target_latency_ms)
        )
        gpus_needed = max(gpus_needed_throughput, gpus_needed_latency, 1)
        
        # 月成本
        hours_per_month = 730
        monthly_cost = gpus_needed * instance.on_demand_hourly * hours_per_month
        cost_per_1k_requests = monthly_cost / (requests_per_day * 30) * 1000
        
        return {
            "model_size": f"{model_params_billions}B",
            "requests_per_day": f"{requests_per_day:,}",
            "gpu_type": gpu_type,
            "gpus_needed": gpus_needed,
            "max_concurrent_per_gpu": max_concurrent,
            "decode_throughput_tps": f"{batch_throughput:.0f}",
            "monthly_compute_cost": f"${monthly_cost:,.0f}",
            "cost_per_1k_requests": f"${cost_per_1k_requests:.2f}",
            "latency_estimate_ms": f"{time_per_request_ms:.0f}",
        }
    
    # ==========================================
    # Spot Instance 策略
    # ==========================================
    
    def spot_savings_analysis(self, 
                            instance_type: str,
                            total_hours: float,
                            interruption_rate: float = 0.05,
                            checkpoint_overhead_pct: float = 0.05) -> dict:
        """
        Spot vs On-Demand 成本分析
        
        考慮因素：
        1. Spot 折扣 (通常 60-70% off)
        2. 中斷後的重計算成本
        3. Checkpoint I/O overhead
        4. 多出的管理複雜度
        """
        instance = self.catalog[instance_type]
        
        # On-Demand baseline
        on_demand_cost = total_hours * instance.on_demand_hourly
        
        # Spot 計算（含中斷損失）
        # 每次中斷損失 = checkpoint_interval / 2 (平均損失半個 interval 的工作)
        checkpoint_interval_hours = 0.167  # 10 分鐘
        avg_loss_per_interruption = checkpoint_interval_hours / 2
        
        # 預期中斷次數
        expected_interruptions = total_hours * interruption_rate
        lost_hours = expected_interruptions * avg_loss_per_interruption
        
        # Effective hours (含重做 + checkpoint overhead)
        effective_hours = total_hours * (1 + checkpoint_overhead_pct) + lost_hours
        spot_cost = effective_hours * instance.spot_hourly
        
        # 混合策略：80% Spot + 20% On-Demand (critical path)
        hybrid_cost = (
            0.8 * effective_hours * instance.spot_hourly +
            0.2 * total_hours * instance.on_demand_hourly
        )
        
        savings_spot = (on_demand_cost - spot_cost) / on_demand_cost * 100
        savings_hybrid = (on_demand_cost - hybrid_cost) / on_demand_cost * 100
        
        return {
            "instance_type": instance_type,
            "total_hours": total_hours,
            "on_demand_cost": f"${on_demand_cost:,.0f}",
            "spot_cost": f"${spot_cost:,.0f}",
            "hybrid_cost": f"${hybrid_cost:,.0f}",
            "spot_savings": f"{savings_spot:.1f}%",
            "hybrid_savings": f"{savings_hybrid:.1f}%",
            "expected_interruptions": f"{expected_interruptions:.1f}",
            "lost_compute_hours": f"{lost_hours:.1f}",
            "recommendation": (
                "Pure Spot" if interruption_rate < 0.03 else
                "Hybrid (80/20)" if interruption_rate < 0.10 else
                "Reserved + On-Demand (Spot too risky)"
            ),
        }


# ==========================================
# 推論成本最佳化技術
# ==========================================

class InferenceCostOptimizer:
    """
    推論服務成本最佳化策略集合
    目標：在滿足 SLA 的前提下最小化成本
    """
    
    @staticmethod
    def quantization_savings(model_params_b: float, 
                           original_precision: str = "FP16",
                           target_precision: str = "INT4") -> dict:
        """
        量化對推論成本的影響
        
        INT4 vs FP16:
        - 模型大小: 4x smaller → 可用更小 GPU
        - Throughput: 2-4x higher (memory bandwidth bound)
        - 品質損失: <1% on most benchmarks (with good calibration)
        """
        precision_bytes = {"FP32": 4, "FP16": 2, "INT8": 1, "INT4": 0.5}
        
        original_size_gb = model_params_b * precision_bytes[original_precision]
        target_size_gb = model_params_b * precision_bytes[target_precision]
        
        # Throughput improvement (memory-bound → proportional to compression)
        compression_ratio = precision_bytes[original_precision] / precision_bytes[target_precision]
        throughput_improvement = min(compression_ratio, 3.5)  # 實際上限
        
        # 可能使用更小的 GPU
        if target_size_gb < 24:
            recommended_gpu = "g5.xlarge (A10G 24GB)"
            gpu_cost = 1.006
        elif target_size_gb < 48:
            recommended_gpu = "g5.2xlarge (A10G 24GB) or g6.2xlarge (L4)"
            gpu_cost = 1.212
        else:
            recommended_gpu = "p4d.24xlarge or larger"
            gpu_cost = 32.77
        
        return {
            "original_size_gb": f"{original_size_gb:.1f} GB",
            "quantized_size_gb": f"{target_size_gb:.1f} GB",
            "compression_ratio": f"{compression_ratio}x",
            "throughput_improvement": f"{throughput_improvement:.1f}x",
            "cost_reduction": f"{(1 - 1/throughput_improvement)*100:.0f}%",
            "recommended_gpu": recommended_gpu,
            "quality_impact": "< 1% degradation (with GPTQ/AWQ calibration)",
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def distillation_roi(teacher_cost_monthly: float,
                        student_size_ratio: float = 0.1,
                        distillation_compute_cost: float = 50000,
                        quality_retention: float = 0.95) -> dict:
        """
        模型蒸餾 ROI 分析
        
        Trade-off: 一次性蒸餾計算成本 vs 持續的推論節省
        """
        student_cost_monthly = teacher_cost_monthly * student_size_ratio / 0.5
        # student 雖然小但 throughput 不是完美線性
        
        monthly_savings = teacher_cost_monthly - student_cost_monthly
        payback_months = distillation_compute_cost / monthly_savings
        
        return {
            "teacher_monthly_cost": f"${teacher_cost_monthly:,.0f}",
            "student_monthly_cost": f"${student_cost_monthly:,.0f}",
            "monthly_savings": f"${monthly_savings:,.0f}",
            "distillation_cost": f"${distillation_compute_cost:,.0f}",
            "payback_period_months": f"{payback_months:.1f}",
            "quality_retention": f"{quality_retention*100:.0f}%",
            "annual_net_savings": f"${monthly_savings * 12 - distillation_compute_cost:,.0f}",
            "recommendation": "Proceed" if payback_months < 3 else "Evaluate alternatives",
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def autoscaling_strategy(peak_rps: float, 
                           avg_rps: float,
                           min_latency_ms: float = 200) -> dict:
        """
        自動擴縮策略設計
        
        目標：不過度預置（浪費），也不低配（SLA violation）
        """
        peak_to_avg_ratio = peak_rps / avg_rps
        
        # 基礎容量：處理 avg + buffer
        base_capacity_rps = avg_rps * 1.3  # 30% buffer
        
        # 峰值容量
        peak_capacity_rps = peak_rps * 1.2  # 20% headroom
        
        # 策略選擇
        if peak_to_avg_ratio < 2:
            strategy = "Predictive + Reactive"
            description = "流量穩定，用預測型擴縮 + reactive 兜底"
            waste_pct = 10
        elif peak_to_avg_ratio < 5:
            strategy = "Scheduled + Reactive"  
            description = "有明顯尖離峰，用排程擴縮 + reactive"
            waste_pct = 20
        else:
            strategy = "Aggressive Reactive + Queue Buffer"
            description = "流量波動大，用 queue 平滑 + aggressive scale-up"
            waste_pct = 30
        
        # Cost comparison: always-on peak vs autoscaling
        always_on_cost_ratio = 1.0  # baseline
        autoscaling_cost_ratio = (avg_rps / peak_rps) + (waste_pct / 100)
        
        return {
            "peak_rps": peak_rps,
            "avg_rps": avg_rps,
            "peak_to_avg_ratio": f"{peak_to_avg_ratio:.1f}x",
            "strategy": strategy,
            "description": description,
            "base_instances": math.ceil(base_capacity_rps / 100),  # 假設 100 rps/instance
            "max_instances": math.ceil(peak_capacity_rps / 100),
            "cost_vs_always_on": f"{autoscaling_cost_ratio*100:.0f}% of always-on",
            "savings": f"{(1-autoscaling_cost_ratio)*100:.0f}%",
            "scale_up_time": "60-120s (new instance cold start)",
            "recommendation": "Use warm pool for <30s scale-up if SLA is strict",
        }


# ==========================================
# Storage 成本最佳化
# ==========================================

class StorageCostOptimizer:
    """儲存成本最佳化"""
    
    # S3 定價 (us-east-1, per GB/month)
    S3_TIERS = {
        "Standard": 0.023,
        "Intelligent-Tiering": 0.023,  # 自動分層
        "Standard-IA": 0.0125,         # 不常存取
        "Glacier Instant": 0.004,
        "Glacier Flexible": 0.0036,
        "Glacier Deep": 0.00099,
    }
    
    def optimize_ml_storage(self, 
                           training_data_tb: float,
                           checkpoints_tb: float,
                           experiment_logs_tb: float,
                           retention_months: int = 6) -> dict:
        """
        ML Pipeline 儲存成本最佳化策略
        """
        # Training data: 經常讀取 → Standard
        training_cost = training_data_tb * 1024 * self.S3_TIERS["Standard"]
        
        # Checkpoints: 最近 3 個保留 Standard，其餘 Glacier
        recent_ckpt_tb = min(checkpoints_tb, 0.5)  # 最近幾個
        old_ckpt_tb = max(0, checkpoints_tb - recent_ckpt_tb)
        ckpt_cost = (
            recent_ckpt_tb * 1024 * self.S3_TIERS["Standard"] +
            old_ckpt_tb * 1024 * self.S3_TIERS["Glacier Instant"]
        )
        
        # Experiment logs: 用 Intelligent-Tiering（自動分層）
        logs_cost = experiment_logs_tb * 1024 * self.S3_TIERS["Intelligent-Tiering"]
        
        total_monthly = training_cost + ckpt_cost + logs_cost
        
        # vs 全部放 Standard 的對比
        naive_cost = (training_data_tb + checkpoints_tb + experiment_logs_tb) * 1024 * self.S3_TIERS["Standard"]
        
        return {
            "training_data": f"{training_data_tb} TB → S3 Standard: ${training_cost:,.0f}/mo",
            "checkpoints": f"{checkpoints_tb} TB → Mixed: ${ckpt_cost:,.0f}/mo",
            "experiment_logs": f"{experiment_logs_tb} TB → Intelligent-Tiering: ${logs_cost:,.0f}/mo",
            "optimized_total": f"${total_monthly:,.0f}/mo",
            "naive_total": f"${naive_cost:,.0f}/mo",
            "savings": f"{(1 - total_monthly/naive_cost)*100:.0f}%",
            "lifecycle_rules": [
                "Checkpoints older than 7 days → Glacier Instant",
                "Checkpoints older than 90 days → Glacier Deep Archive",
                "Experiment logs older than 30 days → Standard-IA",
                "Failed run artifacts → delete after 7 days",
            ],
        }


# ==========================================
# 面試用快速估算公式
# ==========================================

class QuickEstimates:
    """面試中常用的快速估算"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def llm_training_cost_rule_of_thumb(params_b: float, 
                                        tokens_t: float) -> str:
        """
        LLM 訓練成本速算（2024 價格，H100 Spot）
        
        經驗公式：Cost ≈ $1-2 per 1B params × 1T tokens (H100 Spot)
                  Cost ≈ $3-6 per 1B params × 1T tokens (H100 On-Demand)
        
        例：
        - Llama 3 70B, 15T tokens → $70 × 15 × $1.5 = ~$1.5M (Spot)
        - GPT-4 (est. 1.8T params, 13T tokens) → $1.8T × 13 × $1.5 = ~$35M
        """
        spot_estimate = params_b * tokens_t * 1.5
        on_demand_estimate = params_b * tokens_t * 4.5
        
        return (
            f"Model: {params_b}B params, {tokens_t}T tokens\n"
            f"Spot estimate: ${spot_estimate:,.0f}K\n"
            f"On-Demand estimate: ${on_demand_estimate:,.0f}K\n"
            f"(Rule: $1-2 per 1B × 1T on H100 Spot)"
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def inference_cost_per_token() -> dict:
        """
        推論成本 per token 參考值 (2024-2025)
        """
        return {
            "GPT-4o (OpenAI API)": {"input": "$2.50/1M", "output": "$10.00/1M"},
            "GPT-4o-mini": {"input": "$0.15/1M", "output": "$0.60/1M"},
            "Claude 3.5 Sonnet": {"input": "$3.00/1M", "output": "$15.00/1M"},
            "Self-hosted 70B (H100)": {"input": "$0.30/1M", "output": "$0.60/1M"},
            "Self-hosted 70B (A10G INT4)": {"input": "$0.10/1M", "output": "$0.20/1M"},
            "Self-hosted 7B (L4 INT4)": {"input": "$0.02/1M", "output": "$0.04/1M"},
            "Breakeven vs API": "~100K requests/day for 70B model",
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def gpu_utilization_impact(current_util: float = 0.30,
                              target_util: float = 0.70,
                              monthly_gpu_spend: float = 500000) -> dict:
        """
        GPU 利用率提升的直接省錢效果
        """
        # 相同工作量，需要的 GPU 反比於利用率
        reduction_factor = current_util / target_util
        new_spend = monthly_gpu_spend * reduction_factor
        savings = monthly_gpu_spend - new_spend
        
        return {
            "current_utilization": f"{current_util*100:.0f}%",
            "target_utilization": f"{target_util*100:.0f}%",
            "current_monthly_spend": f"${monthly_gpu_spend:,.0f}",
            "new_monthly_spend": f"${new_spend:,.0f}",
            "monthly_savings": f"${savings:,.0f}",
            "annual_savings": f"${savings * 12:,.0f}",
            "how": [
                "Gang scheduling (reduce fragmentation)",
                "Bin-packing (fill GPU memory fully)",
                "Right-sizing (match instance to workload)",
                "Time-slicing (share GPUs for small jobs)",
                "Queue priority (reduce idle wait time)",
            ],
        }

🧪 驗證考題

Q1: 你的團隊每月 GPU 帳單 $2M，老闆要求砍 40%。你的行動計畫？

答案：系統性的 4 步驟：

  1. Audit (Week 1)：分析 cost breakdown — Training vs Inference vs Idle。查 GPU utilization（通常發現 30-40% idle）。找到 top 5 最貴的 job/service。
  2. Quick Wins (Week 2-4)：(a) Spot migration for training jobs → 立省 60-70% compute; (b) 量化推論模型 FP16→INT4 → throughput 3x → GPU 數量砍 2/3; (c) 停掉 abandoned experiments 和 zombie processes; (d) Autoscaling 推論服務（很多團隊 always-on at peak）。
  3. Medium-term (Month 2-3)：(a) 模型蒸餾（70B → 7B for 80% use cases）; (b) 提高 cluster utilization（multi-tenancy, gang scheduling）; (c) Storage lifecycle policy（checkpoint 分層）; (d) Reserved/Savings Plan for baseline load。
  4. Long-term (Quarter 2+)：(a) 自研推論引擎最佳化; (b) 考慮自建 vs 雲端 TCO; (c) 模型架構最佳化（MoE, speculative decoding）。

實際數字：Spot (60% training × 65% savings = 39% savings on training) + Quantization (70% inference × 50% savings = 35% on inference) + Autoscaling (20% waste reduction) → 總計可達 40-50% 節省。

Q2: Self-hosted LLM vs API (OpenAI/Anthropic)，什麼時候該選哪個？

答案：決策框架：

維度Self-hosted 更好API 更好
流量>100K requests/day<10K requests/day
延遲需求需要 <100ms (on-premise GPU)可接受 200-500ms
資料隱私不能送外部 (醫療/金融/軍事)可接受 third-party
客製化需要 fine-tune 或 custom tokenizer通用任務用 prompt 即可
團隊能力有 ML Infra 團隊 (≥3 人)沒有 GPU/Infra 經驗
模型品質70B 品質夠用需要 GPT-4/Claude 3.5 等級

盈虧平衡點：Self-hosted 70B on 4×A10G (INT4) 月成本 ~$3K。OpenAI GPT-4o-mini at 100K req/day (500+200 tokens) = $0.75/1K × 100K × 30 = $2.25K/month。所以 <100K/day 用 API 更划算，>100K/day 自架更省。但注意：自架需要 on-call、GPU driver 更新、model 版本管理等隱性人力成本。

Q3: Spot Instance 中斷率 15%，你的訓練還能省錢嗎？

答案：需要計算 effective cost。假設：On-Demand $32/hr，Spot $12/hr (62% off)，checkpoint 每 10 分鐘，中斷損失平均 5 分鐘工作量。1000 小時任務：On-Demand cost = $32,000。Spot：中斷次數 = 1000 × 0.15 = 150 次。損失時間 = 150 × 5/60 = 12.5 小時。Checkpoint overhead = 5% = 50 小時。Effective hours = 1000 + 50 + 12.5 = 1062.5 hr。Spot cost = 1062.5 × $12 = $12,750。仍省 60%！

臨界點：Spot 不划算的條件 = Spot_price × (1 + overhead + interruption_loss) > On-Demand_price。$12 × (1 + 0.05 + 0.15×5/600) ≈ $12.75 << $32。即使中斷率 30% 仍然划算。只有當 Spot price > On-Demand × 0.7 或 checkpoint 成本極高（如 >1TB model, slow storage）時才不適合。

Q4: 如何設計一個 GPU 共享平台讓多個團隊的 utilization 都提升？

答案：五個機制：(1) Fair-share scheduling：保證每團隊最低配額，空閒資源按權重分享（類似 YARN Fair Scheduler）。(2) GPU Bin-packing：把小任務（推論、notebook）填滿大 GPU 的剩餘空間（MIG/MPS）。(3) Priority preemption：低優先任務（research exploration）放 preemptible tier，高優先任務（production training）可搶佔。(4) Time-sharing：開發環境用 time-sliced GPU（不需要獨佔整張）。(5) Usage chargeback：按實際 GPU-hours 計費給各團隊（有帳必省）。典型效果：cluster utilization 從 30% 提升到 70%+ = 等效省一半 GPU。

Q5: 比較 vLLM continuous batching vs naive batching 的成本差異。

答案：

  • Naive static batching：等湊滿 batch_size 個 request → 所有 request 必須等最長的那個完成 → 短 request 浪費 GPU cycles 等長 request。Throughput: ~200 tokens/s (7B, A10G)。
  • vLLM continuous batching：request 隨到隨加入，完成的立刻離開 → GPU 永遠滿載。搭配 PagedAttention 減少 KV cache 碎片化。Throughput: ~800-1200 tokens/s (7B, A10G)。
  • 成本影響：4-6x throughput → 需要的 GPU 數量降為 1/4-1/6。月費從 $12K 降到 $2-3K（同樣流量）。
  • Speculative Decoding（加碼）：用小 model 猜 5 tokens，大 model 一次驗證。命中率 70% → throughput 再提升 2-3x。

🎯 Follow-up Q&A

F1: Reserved Instance vs Savings Plan 的差別？ML workload 推薦哪個？

RI (Reserved Instance)：鎖定特定 instance type + region + OS，最高 72% 折扣（3 年全預付）。不靈活：換 GPU 型號要重新購買。Savings Plan：承諾每小時花費金額（如 $50/hr），可用於任何 instance type。靈活：A100 不夠時可換 H100。ML 推薦：(1) 推論服務（穩定負載）→ Compute Savings Plan（最靈活）。(2) 固定訓練叢集（如預留 A100 cluster）→ RI（折扣最大）。(3) 混合策略：50% 基礎量用 Savings Plan + 剩餘用 Spot。

F2: 如何說服 ML 研究員配合成本最佳化（他們通常不在乎錢）？

三個策略：(1) Budget 透明化：每週寄 cost report 給每個研究員，標註他們的 top 5 最貴 experiments。「你上週跑了 3 個 abandoned job 花了 $5K」比「請省錢」有效。(2) 工具化：提供自動 spot migration、checkpoint、preemptible queue — 研究員不需要改 code 就能省錢。(3) Incentive 對齊：固定預算下，省錢 = 更多實驗 = 更多論文。「同樣 $100K 預算，用 Spot 可以跑 3x 實驗數量」。注意：永遠不要為了省錢犧牲研究速度（如強制排隊 2 天），否則研究員會繞過系統或離職。

F3: Multi-cloud 策略能省錢嗎？風險是什麼？

理論上可以：GCP TPU 預訓練可能比 AWS GPU 便宜 20-30%；Azure 有時有更好的 Spot 價格。實務上的 cost：(1) 資料搬遷：跨雲 egress 費 $0.08-0.12/GB（100 TB training data = $8K-12K 單次）。(2) 工程複雜度：需要抽象化 infra layer（Terraform multi-provider, 跨雲 k8s）。(3) 除錯困難：兩個雲的 GPU driver/NCCL 版本不同。(4) 供應商 lock-in：GCP TPU 需要 JAX/XLA，不能直接搬 PyTorch 到 TPU。建議：除非規模 >$5M/month 且有專門 Platform 團隊，否則 single-cloud + Spot diversity 更實際。

F4: 推論服務的 cold start 問題如何解決？對成本的影響？

Cold start = 新 instance 從啟動到能服務 request 的時間。LLM 推論 cold start 特別長：下載模型 (70B = 140GB from S3 @ 5 GB/s = 28s) + load to GPU memory (5-10s) + warmup (3-5s) = 40-50s total。對成本影響：Autoscaling 不敢 scale-to-zero → 維持最少 N 個 warm instance → 浪費。解法：(1) Warm pool：預啟動 instance 但不加入 serving（省模型下載時間）。(2) 模型快取：instance local NVMe cache model weights（只需 GPU load 5s）。(3) Serverless GPU with snapshot restore（如 AWS Lambda SnapStart 概念應用在 GPU）。(4) 預測式擴縮：根據歷史流量提前 2 分鐘 scale up。

F5: On-premise GPU cluster vs Cloud，什麼規模 breakeven？

粗估 TCO 比較（3 年）：Cloud H100 On-Demand: $98/hr × 8760 × 3 = ~$2.6M per 8-GPU node。On-premise H100 DGX: 硬體 $300K + 3 年電費 $100K + 冷卻/機房 $50K + 人力 $150K (0.1 FTE × $500K × 3) + 網路 $50K = ~$650K per 8-GPU node。Breakeven: On-prem 比 Cloud 便宜 4x！但前提：(1) 3 年期 utilization > 60%；(2) 有 datacenter 空間和電力；(3) 有 infra 團隊維運。實務上 Cloud RI/Spot 可以把差距縮小到 2x。Meta/Google 選擇自建因為規模夠大（10,000+ GPU）且有 infra 團隊。小公司 (<100 GPU) 用 Cloud 更合理（不需要自己管硬體）。

Part VI: 面試實戰

🎯 Part VI: AI/ML System Design 面試實戰 (Ch21-25)

目標讀者：Google L5-L6 面試準備者
框架：每題使用 4 步驟法 — Requirements → High-Level Design → Deep Dive → Evaluation
風格：繁體中文 + 英文術語保留 + ASCII 架構圖 + 程式碼範例

📖 Chapter 21: Design Real-time Recommendation System

題目：設計一個支援 1 億用戶、延遲 <50ms 的即時推薦系統

🔹 Step 1: Requirements Clarification

Functional Requirements

  • 即時推薦：用戶打開 App 時 <50ms 返回 Top-K 推薦結果
  • 多場景支援：首頁 Feed、商品詳情頁「猜你喜歡」、搜尋結果排序
  • Cold Start：新用戶/新商品上線 1 小時內可被推薦
  • 即時反饋：用戶行為（點擊/購買）5 分鐘內影響推薦結果

Non-Functional Requirements

  • QPS: 100M DAU × 10 requests/day = ~12K QPS (peak 50K QPS)
  • Latency: p99 < 50ms (end-to-end)
  • Availability: 99.99%
  • 推薦品質：CTR 提升 >15% vs baseline

Scale Estimation

維度數值備註
Users100M DAU500M 註冊用戶
Items10M active items每日新增 100K
Features~500 dimensions/user包含 embedding
Candidate Pool10M → 1000 → 50三階段漏斗
Model Size~2GB (Two-Tower)每台機器需載入

🔹 Step 2: High-Level Design

三階段漏斗（Funnel）設計原則

階段候選數模型複雜度延遲預算核心目標
Retrieval10M → 1000低（embedding dot product）<10ms高 Recall
Ranking1000 → 200高（deep model）<20ms高 Precision
Re-ranking200 → 50中（rules + light model）<5ms多樣性 + 業務邏輯

🔹 Step 3: Deep Dive

3.1 Two-Tower Model（雙塔模型）

💡 核心思想：將 user 和 item 分別編碼為固定維度向量，線上推薦時只需計算 dot product / cosine similarity，實現 O(1) 的 scoring 複雜度。 
import tensorflow as tf

class TwoTowerModel(tf.keras.Model):
    """
    雙塔推薦模型：
    - User Tower: 編碼用戶歷史行為 + profile 為 128-dim embedding
    - Item Tower: 編碼商品屬性 + content 為 128-dim embedding
    - 線上只需 User Tower 即時推論 + ANN 查詢 Item embeddings
    """
    def __init__(self, user_feature_dim=256, item_feature_dim=128, 
                 embedding_dim=128):
        super().__init__()
        # User Tower: 處理 sparse + dense features
        self.user_tower = tf.keras.Sequential([
            tf.keras.layers.Dense(512, activation='relu'),
            tf.keras.layers.BatchNormalization(),
            tf.keras.layers.Dense(256, activation='relu'),
            tf.keras.layers.Dropout(0.2),
            tf.keras.layers.Dense(embedding_dim),  # 輸出 128-dim
            tf.keras.layers.Lambda(
                lambda x: tf.nn.l2_normalize(x, axis=1)  # L2 normalize
            )
        ])
        # Item Tower: 相同結構，獨立參數
        self.item_tower = tf.keras.Sequential([
            tf.keras.layers.Dense(256, activation='relu'),
            tf.keras.layers.BatchNormalization(),
            tf.keras.layers.Dense(128, activation='relu'),
            tf.keras.layers.Dense(embedding_dim),
            tf.keras.layers.Lambda(
                lambda x: tf.nn.l2_normalize(x, axis=1)
            )
        ])
        # Temperature parameter for softmax
        self.temperature = tf.Variable(0.05, trainable=True)
    
    def call(self, user_features, item_features):
        user_emb = self.user_tower(user_features)   # [B, 128]
        item_emb = self.item_tower(item_features)   # [B, 128]
        # Cosine similarity (already L2 normalized)
        similarity = tf.reduce_sum(
            user_emb * item_emb, axis=1
        ) / self.temperature
        return similarity
    
    def get_user_embedding(self, user_features):
        """線上推論：只執行 User Tower"""
        return self.user_tower(user_features)
    
    def get_item_embedding(self, item_features):
        """離線建索引：批次計算所有 Item embeddings"""
        return self.item_tower(item_features)


# === Training with In-Batch Negatives ===
def train_step(model, user_batch, pos_item_batch, optimizer):
    """
    In-Batch Negatives: 同一 batch 內其他 item 作為負樣本
    優點：無需額外 negative sampling，計算效率高
    batch_size=1024 → 每個正樣本有 1023 個負樣本
    """
    with tf.GradientTape() as tape:
        user_embs = model.get_user_embedding(user_batch)     # [B, 128]
        item_embs = model.get_item_embedding(pos_item_batch) # [B, 128]
        
        # 計算 all-pairs similarity matrix
        logits = tf.matmul(
            user_embs, item_embs, transpose_b=True
        ) / model.temperature  # [B, B]
        
        # 對角線是正樣本
        labels = tf.range(tf.shape(logits)[0])
        loss = tf.nn.sparse_softmax_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            labels=labels, logits=logits
        )
        loss = tf.reduce_mean(loss)
    
    gradients = tape.gradient(loss, model.trainable_variables)
    optimizer.apply_gradients(zip(gradients, model.trainable_variables))
    return loss

3.2 ANN (Approximate Nearest Neighbor) 索引

import faiss
import numpy as np

class ANNIndex:
    """
    FAISS IVF-PQ Index for 10M items:
    - IVF (Inverted File): 將向量空間分成 nlist 個 cluster
    - PQ (Product Quantization): 將 128-dim 壓縮為 M 個子空間
    
    記憶體估算：
    - Raw: 10M × 128 × 4 bytes = 5.12 GB
    - IVF-PQ: 10M × 32 bytes ≈ 320 MB (16x 壓縮)
    
    延遲：<5ms for top-100 on single CPU core
    """
    def __init__(self, dim=128, nlist=4096, m=32, nbits=8):
        # IVF (coarse quantizer) + PQ (fine quantizer)
        quantizer = faiss.IndexFlatIP(dim)  # Inner product
        self.index = faiss.IndexIVFPQ(
            quantizer, dim, nlist, m, nbits,
            faiss.METRIC_INNER_PRODUCT
        )
        self.index.nprobe = 64  # 搜尋 64 個 cluster（trade-off: recall vs latency）
    
    def build(self, item_embeddings: np.ndarray):
        """
        離線建索引（每小時更新）
        item_embeddings: [10M, 128] float32
        Training time: ~10 min on 32-core CPU
        """
        # 需要先 train（學習 cluster centroids + PQ codebook）
        faiss.normalize_L2(item_embeddings)  # L2 normalize for cosine sim
        self.index.train(item_embeddings[:1_000_000])  # 用 1M 樣本訓練
        self.index.add(item_embeddings)  # 加入全部 10M
        print(f"Index size: {self.index.ntotal} vectors")
    
    def search(self, query_embedding: np.ndarray, top_k=1000):
        """
        線上查詢：<5ms
        query_embedding: [1, 128] 或 [batch, 128]
        """
        faiss.normalize_L2(query_embedding)
        distances, indices = self.index.search(query_embedding, top_k)
        return indices[0], distances[0]  # item_ids, scores


# === ScaNN (Google 內部方案) vs FAISS 比較 ===
# ScaNN: Asymmetric hashing + anisotropic quantization
# 優勢：在相同 recall@100 下，延遲比 FAISS 低 2-3x
# Google 內部推薦系統首選

3.3 Feature Store 設計

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ Feature Store │ ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ │ │ │ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │ Feature Registry (Metadata) │ │ │ │ • Feature definitions (name, type, owner) │ │ │ │ • Lineage tracking │ │ │ │ • Version management │ │ │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ ┌─────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌────────────────┐ │ │ │ Offline │ │ Near-Real │ │ Online │ │ │ │ Store │ │ Time │ │ Store │ │ │ ├─────────────┤ ├──────────────┤ ├────────────────┤ │ │ │ Hive/BQ │ │ Flink + Kafka│ │ Redis Cluster │ │ │ │ Daily batch │ │ 5-min window │ │ <1ms lookup │ │ │ │ Historical │ │ Sliding agg │ │ Latest values │ │ │ │ Training │ │ • click_rate │ │ • user_emb │ │ │ │ data only │ │ • view_count │ │ • item_emb │ │ │ │ │ │ • trend_score│ │ • context_feat │ │ │ └─────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ Feature freshness SLA: │ │ • User profile: updated daily (batch) │ │ • Behavioral: updated per 5 min (streaming) │ │ • Context (time): computed at request time (online) │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ 
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
import redis
import time

@dataclass
class FeatureSpec:
    name: str
    dtype: str          # float32, int64, embedding
    freshness: str      # realtime, near-realtime, batch
    default_value: any  # Cold start 預設值
    ttl_seconds: int    # Redis TTL

class OnlineFeatureStore:
    """
    Redis-based Online Feature Store
    設計重點：
    1. Batch GET: 一次取多個 feature，減少 RTT
    2. Feature encoding: 使用 MessagePack 壓縮
    3. Fallback: Redis miss 時返回 default value (Cold Start)
    """
    def __init__(self, redis_cluster_nodes: List[str]):
        self.redis = redis.RedisCluster(
            startup_nodes=[
                {"host": node, "port": 6379} 
                for node in redis_cluster_nodes
            ],
            decode_responses=False
        )
        self.feature_specs: Dict[str, FeatureSpec] = {}
    
    def get_user_features(self, user_id: str, 
                          feature_names: List[str]) -> Dict:
        """
        批次取得用戶特徵 (Pipeline 模式減少 RTT)
        延遲目標: <2ms for 20 features
        """
        pipe = self.redis.pipeline()
        keys = [f"user:{user_id}:{fname}" for fname in feature_names]
        for key in keys:
            pipe.get(key)
        
        results = pipe.execute()
        features = {}
        for fname, value in zip(feature_names, results):
            if value is not None:
                features[fname] = self._decode(value)
            else:
                # Cold start fallback
                spec = self.feature_specs[fname]
                features[fname] = spec.default_value
        return features
    
    def update_realtime_feature(self, user_id: str, 
                                feature_name: str, value):
        """即時更新（來自 Flink streaming pipeline）"""
        key = f"user:{user_id}:{feature_name}"
        ttl = self.feature_specs[feature_name].ttl_seconds
        self.redis.setex(key, ttl, self._encode(value))
    
    def _encode(self, value) -> bytes:
        import msgpack
        return msgpack.packb(value)
    
    def _decode(self, data: bytes):
        import msgpack
        return msgpack.unpackb(data)

3.4 Cold Start 策略

場景策略實作方式生效時間
新用戶Population-based使用全局熱門 + 用戶 demographics即時
新用戶 (有行為)Explorationε-greedy / Thompson Sampling5-10 次互動後
新商品Content-basedItem Tower 編碼 metadata → ANN index<1 小時
新商品 (有曝光)Online LearningIncremental model update5 分鐘
class ColdStartStrategy:
    """
    多策略 Cold Start Handler
    原則：盡快收集信號 → 盡快切換到個人化推薦
    """
    def get_recommendations(self, user_id: str, context: dict) -> List[str]:
        user_history_count = self.get_interaction_count(user_id)
        
        if user_history_count == 0:
            # 全新用戶：基於 demographics + global trending
            return self._population_based(context)
        elif user_history_count < 10:
            # 少量行為：exploration + exploitation
            explore_ratio = max(0.3, 1.0 - user_history_count * 0.1)
            return self._epsilon_greedy(user_id, explore_ratio)
        else:
            # 足夠行為：完全個人化
            return self._personalized(user_id, context)
    
    def _population_based(self, context: dict) -> List[str]:
        """根據時段、地區、設備類型推薦全局熱門"""
        segment = f"{context['country']}:{context['device']}:{context['hour_bucket']}"
        return self.trending_cache.get(segment, self.global_trending)
    
    def _epsilon_greedy(self, user_id: str, epsilon: float) -> List[str]:
        """探索-利用平衡"""
        import random
        if random.random() < epsilon:
            # Exploration: 從多樣化候選池中隨機選
            return self._diverse_sample(n=50)
        else:
            # Exploitation: 用已有數據做推薦
            return self._personalized(user_id, {})

3.5 Online Learning Pipeline

class OnlineLearningPipeline:
    """
    即時模型更新 Pipeline:
    1. User action → Kafka topic
    2. Flink job → 生成 training sample
    3. Parameter Server → incremental gradient update
    4. Shadow deployment → A/B test validation
    
    關鍵決策：
    - 只更新 embedding layers（不動 DNN backbone）
    - 使用 exponential decay 降低舊樣本權重
    - 每 5 分鐘 checkpoint + validation
    """
    def process_user_action(self, event: dict):
        # event = {user_id, item_id, action, timestamp}
        
        # 1. 生成 training sample
        sample = {
            "user_features": self.feature_store.get_user_features(
                event["user_id"], self.training_features
            ),
            "item_features": self.feature_store.get_item_features(
                event["item_id"]
            ),
            "label": 1.0 if event["action"] in ["click", "purchase"] else 0.0,
            "weight": self._time_decay_weight(event["timestamp"])
        }
        
        # 2. Incremental update (only embedding layers)
        self.online_trainer.update(sample)
        
        # 3. 每 5 分鐘 validate + push
        if self.should_checkpoint():
            metrics = self.validate_on_holdout()
            if metrics["auc"] > self.baseline_auc * 0.99:  # 不低於 baseline 1%
                self.push_to_serving()
            else:
                self.rollback()
                self.alert("Online model degradation detected")
    
    def _time_decay_weight(self, event_ts: float) -> float:
        """指數衰減：越新的樣本權重越大"""
        age_hours = (time.time() - event_ts) / 3600
        half_life = 24  # 24 小時半衰期
        return 2 ** (-age_hours / half_life)

🔹 Step 4: Evaluation & Metrics

維度指標目標監控方式
品質CTR / CVR+15% vs baselineOnline A/B test
延遲p99 latency<50msPer-stage breakdown
覆蓋率Item coverage>80% items exposed/weekDaily report
多樣性Intra-list diversity>0.6 (cosine distance)Per-request
公平性Position biasCorrection via IPWOffline eval

⚖️ Key Trade-offs

  • Recall vs Latency: ANN nprobe↑ → recall↑ but latency↑. 用 64 nprobe 可達 95% recall@1000 + <5ms
  • Freshness vs Stability: Online learning 太激進 → 不穩定。解法：exponential decay + validation gate
  • Exploration vs Exploitation: 過度 exploit → filter bubble。解法：Thompson Sampling + diversity re-ranking
  • Model Complexity vs Latency: 更大 model → 更準但更慢。解法：distillation + quantization for serving

🔹 Follow-up Questions

Q1: 如何處理 Position Bias（位置偏差）？

問題：用戶傾向點擊前面的結果，導致模型「以為」前面的 item 更好，形成正向反饋循環。

解法：

  1. IPW (Inverse Propensity Weighting)：根據位置給予不同權重。位置 1 的點擊權重低（因為曝光優勢），位置 10 的點擊權重高。
  2. Position feature：訓練時加入 position 作為 feature，serving 時設為固定值（如 position=1），消除位置影響。
  3. Randomization：定期隨機打亂一小部分流量（1-5%），收集無偏數據用於 offline evaluation。
# IPW correction
def compute_ipw_weight(position: int, position_ctr: Dict[int, float]) -> float:
    """
    位置 k 的 propensity = P(click | position=k, item is relevant)
    用全局 CTR by position 近似
    """
    propensity = position_ctr.get(position, 0.01)
    # Clip to avoid extreme weights
    return min(1.0 / propensity, 10.0)
Q2: Two-Tower vs Cross-Network 如何選擇？

Two-Tower（YouTube DNN, Google）：

  • ✅ 可以離線計算 item embedding → ANN 快速檢索
  • ✅ Serving 時只需 user tower forward pass + ANN lookup
  • ❌ User-Item 交互信息只有 dot product，表達力有限

Cross-Network（DCN, DeepFM）：

  • ✅ 顯式建模 feature interactions，精度更高
  • ❌ 需要 user+item feature 一起過模型，無法離線計算 item embedding
  • ❌ 只能用在 Ranking 階段（候選數少時）

最佳實踐：Retrieval 用 Two-Tower（效率），Ranking 用 Cross-Network（精度）。

Q3: 如何做到 5 分鐘內反映用戶最新行為？

答：分層更新策略：

  1. Feature level (最快, 秒級)：用戶剛點擊的 item embedding 直接加入 user 的 short-term interest sequence
  2. Near-realtime (5 分鐘)：Flink sliding window 聚合行為特徵（如最近 5 min click category distribution）
  3. Model level (小時級)：Online learning 更新 embedding layer
class RealtimeUserState:
    def on_user_action(self, user_id: str, item_id: str, action: str):
        # Level 1: 直接更新 Redis 中的 recent_items list
        key = f"user:{user_id}:recent_clicks"
        self.redis.lpush(key, item_id)
        self.redis.ltrim(key, 0, 49)  # 只保留最近 50 個
        self.redis.expire(key, 3600)  # 1 hour TTL
        
        # Level 2: 發送到 Kafka → Flink 聚合
        self.kafka_producer.send("user_actions", {
            "user_id": user_id,
            "item_id": item_id,
            "action": action,
            "timestamp": time.time()
        })
Q4: ANN 索引如何做到不停機更新？

答：Double-buffer（雙緩衝）策略：

  1. 維護兩套索引：Active Index (serving) + Shadow Index (building)
  2. 每小時離線 build 新 Shadow Index（含最新 item embeddings）
  3. Build 完成後 atomic swap：Shadow → Active
  4. 舊 Active 延遲 5 分鐘後釋放（drain inflight requests）
class ANNIndexManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.active_index = None
        self.shadow_index = None
    
    def rebuild_hourly(self, new_embeddings):
        # 1. Build new index in background
        self.shadow_index = ANNIndex(dim=128)
        self.shadow_index.build(new_embeddings)
        
        # 2. Validate quality (recall check)
        recall = self.validate_recall(self.shadow_index)
        if recall < 0.90:
            raise ValueError(f"New index recall too low: {recall}")
        
        # 3. Atomic swap
        old_active = self.active_index
        self.active_index = self.shadow_index
        self.shadow_index = None
        
        # 4. Delayed cleanup
        time.sleep(300)  # Wait for inflight requests
        del old_active
Q5: 如何評估推薦系統的長期效果？

問題：短期 CTR 提升可能靠 clickbait，長期反而傷害用戶留存。

答：Multi-horizon evaluation framework：

時間範圍指標評估方式
即時 (per-request)CTR, engagement timeOnline metrics
短期 (7 days)DAU retention, session lengthA/B test cohort
中期 (30 days)Revenue/user, subscriptionLong-running experiment
長期 (90 days)User satisfaction (NPS), diversitySurvey + offline analysis

Google 的做法：使用 "long-term user value" 作為最終 reward signal，而非單純 CTR。Short-term CTR 作為 proxy metric 進行快速迭代，但每季度校準一次與 long-term value 的相關性。

Q6: 如何處理 item 的 popularity bias？

問題：熱門商品佔據大部分曝光 → 新/冷門商品得不到機會 → 馬太效應。

解法組合：

  1. In-batch negative sampling correction：熱門 item 被當作 negative 的概率更高，加入 log(popularity) 修正
  2. Re-ranking diversity：MMR (Maximal Marginal Relevance) 保證推薦列表類別多樣性
  3. Exploration quota：每次推薦保留 10-20% 位置給探索性 items
  4. Inverse propensity scoring：訓練時對熱門 item 降權
def mmr_rerank(candidates, scores, embeddings, lambda_param=0.5, top_k=50):
    """
    MMR Re-ranking: 平衡 relevance 和 diversity
    lambda=1.0: 純 relevance
    lambda=0.0: 純 diversity
    """
    selected = []
    remaining = list(range(len(candidates)))
    
    while len(selected) < top_k and remaining:
        best_score = -float('inf')
        best_idx = -1
        
        for idx in remaining:
            relevance = scores[idx]
            # Max similarity to already selected items
            if selected:
                sim_to_selected = max(
                    cosine_similarity(embeddings[idx], embeddings[s])
                    for s in selected
                )
            else:
                sim_to_selected = 0
            
            mmr_score = lambda_param * relevance - (1 - lambda_param) * sim_to_selected
            if mmr_score > best_score:
                best_score = mmr_score
                best_idx = idx
        
        selected.append(best_idx)
        remaining.remove(best_idx)
    
    return [candidates[i] for i in selected]
Q7: 系統如何水平擴展到 50K QPS？

答：分層擴展策略：

  • API Gateway：Stateless，直接水平加機器。使用 consistent hashing 做 user-level routing（同用戶打同台 → cache 友好）
  • Retrieval (ANN)：Index sharding by item_id hash。每 shard 獨立 serve，query fan-out 到所有 shard → merge top-K
  • Ranking Model：GPU serving (TensorRT)，batch inference。多 replica，每台 GPU 可處理 ~5K QPS (batch=128, latency=20ms)
  • Feature Store (Redis)：Redis Cluster with 100+ shards，每 shard ~100K QPS

容量規劃：

  • 50K QPS / 5K per GPU = 10 GPU machines for ranking
  • 50K QPS × 20 features / 100K per Redis shard = 10 Redis shards (minimum)
  • ANN: 4 shards × 3 replicas = 12 machines

📖 Chapter 22: Design LLM Serving Platform

題目：設計一個能服務 GPT-4 級別模型、支援 1000 並發用戶的 LLM 推論平台

🔹 Step 1: Requirements Clarification

Functional Requirements

  • 支援 GPT-4 級別模型（175B+ parameters）的即時推論
  • Streaming token generation（逐 token 返回）
  • 支援 multi-turn conversation（context window 128K tokens）
  • 支援 batch inference（離線任務）
  • Multi-model serving（不同 model 版本/大小共存）

Non-Functional Requirements

  • Throughput: 1000 concurrent users, ~10K tokens/sec aggregate
  • Latency: TTFT (Time to First Token) < 500ms, TBT (Time Between Tokens) < 50ms
  • GPU Utilization: > 70%
  • Cost efficiency: < $0.01 / 1K input tokens, < $0.03 / 1K output tokens
  • Availability: 99.9%

Scale Estimation

維度數值說明
Model Size175B params = 350GB (FP16)8x A100 80GB = 640GB VRAM
KV Cache/user~2GB per 128K context主要 memory bottleneck
1000 concurrent~2TB KV Cache total需要 memory management
Tokens/sec/GPU~150 tokens (batch=1)Batching → 2000+ tokens/GPU
GPU 需求10K tokens/sec ÷ 2K/GPU = 5 nodes每 node 8x A100

🔹 Step 3: Deep Dive

3.1 PagedAttention — KV Cache Memory Management

💡 核心問題：傳統 LLM serving 需要為每個 sequence 預分配 max_seq_len 的連續 KV cache memory。128K context → 2GB/sequence → 1000 並發 = 2TB。但大多數 sequence 不會用滿 128K → 巨大浪費。

PagedAttention 解法：類似 OS 虛擬記憶體，將 KV cache 分成固定大小的 block（如 16 tokens/block），按需分配 physical block。序列增長時才分配新 block → 記憶體利用率從 20-40% 提升到 >95%。 
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
import numpy as np

class KVBlock:
    """
    一個 KV Cache Block 存放 block_size 個 token 的 K,V tensors
    大小: 2 (K+V) × num_layers × num_heads × head_dim × block_size × dtype_size
    Example (175B model, block_size=16):
      2 × 96 layers × 96 heads × 128 dim × 16 tokens × 2 bytes = 72MB per block
    """
    BLOCK_SIZE = 16  # tokens per block
    
    def __init__(self, block_id: int):
        self.block_id = block_id
        self.ref_count = 0  # Copy-on-Write reference counting
        self.num_filled = 0  # 實際使用的 token 數

class PagedKVCacheManager:
    """
    虛擬記憶體風格的 KV Cache 管理器
    - Block Table: 每個 sequence 維護 logical → physical block mapping
    - Free Pool: 空閒 physical blocks
    - CoW: 共享前綴時使用 Copy-on-Write
    """
    def __init__(self, num_gpu_blocks: int, block_size: int = 16):
        self.block_size = block_size
        self.free_blocks: List[int] = list(range(num_gpu_blocks))
        # sequence_id → list of physical block ids
        self.block_tables: Dict[int, List[int]] = {}
        # block_id → KVBlock metadata
        self.blocks: Dict[int, KVBlock] = {
            i: KVBlock(i) for i in range(num_gpu_blocks)
        }
    
    def allocate(self, seq_id: int, num_tokens: int) -> bool:
        """為新 sequence 分配 blocks"""
        num_blocks_needed = (num_tokens + self.block_size - 1) // self.block_size
        
        if len(self.free_blocks) < num_blocks_needed:
            return False  # 需要 preemption
        
        allocated = []
        for _ in range(num_blocks_needed):
            block_id = self.free_blocks.pop()
            self.blocks[block_id].ref_count = 1
            allocated.append(block_id)
        
        self.block_tables[seq_id] = allocated
        return True
    
    def append_token(self, seq_id: int) -> Optional[int]:
        """生成新 token 時可能需要新 block"""
        blocks = self.block_tables[seq_id]
        last_block = self.blocks[blocks[-1]]
        
        if last_block.num_filled < self.block_size:
            # 當前 block 還有空間
            last_block.num_filled += 1
            return None
        else:
            # 需要新 block
            if not self.free_blocks:
                return -1  # 需要 preemption
            new_block_id = self.free_blocks.pop()
            self.blocks[new_block_id].ref_count = 1
            self.blocks[new_block_id].num_filled = 1
            blocks.append(new_block_id)
            return new_block_id
    
    def free_sequence(self, seq_id: int):
        """釋放完成序列的所有 blocks"""
        for block_id in self.block_tables.pop(seq_id, []):
            block = self.blocks[block_id]
            block.ref_count -= 1
            if block.ref_count == 0:
                block.num_filled = 0
                self.free_blocks.append(block_id)
    
    def share_prefix(self, src_seq_id: int, dst_seq_id: int, 
                     num_shared_tokens: int):
        """
        Prefix Caching: 共享系統 prompt 的 KV blocks
        使用 Copy-on-Write — 只有當需要修改時才複製
        """
        num_shared_blocks = num_shared_tokens // self.block_size
        src_blocks = self.block_tables[src_seq_id]
        
        # 共享 blocks (增加 ref_count)
        shared = src_blocks[:num_shared_blocks]
        for block_id in shared:
            self.blocks[block_id].ref_count += 1
        
        self.block_tables[dst_seq_id] = shared.copy()
    
    def get_utilization(self) -> float:
        """記憶體利用率"""
        total = len(self.blocks)
        used = total - len(self.free_blocks)
        return used / total

3.2 Continuous Batching（連續批處理）

💡 為什麼需要 Continuous Batching：
傳統 static batching：等 batch 中最長序列完成才能處理下一批 → GPU idle 嚴重。
Continuous batching：序列完成就立刻替換新請求進 batch → GPU 永遠滿載。
效果：throughput 提升 2-5x。 
from enum import Enum
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import heapq
import time

class RequestState(Enum):
    WAITING = "waiting"      # 在隊列中等待
    PREFILL = "prefill"      # 正在做 prefill (首次)
    DECODING = "decoding"    # 逐 token 生成中
    PREEMPTED = "preempted"  # 被搶佔，等待恢復
    FINISHED = "finished"    # 完成

@dataclass
class InferenceRequest:
    request_id: str
    prompt_tokens: List[int]
    max_new_tokens: int
    priority: int = 0  # 越高越優先
    arrival_time: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    state: RequestState = RequestState.WAITING
    generated_tokens: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    @property
    def num_total_tokens(self):
        return len(self.prompt_tokens) + len(self.generated_tokens)

class ContinuousBatchScheduler:
    """
    vLLM-style Continuous Batching Scheduler
    
    核心邏輯：
    1. 每個 iteration 檢查是否有空間加入新請求
    2. 已完成的請求立即移出 batch
    3. Memory 不夠時 preempt 低優先級請求
    """
    def __init__(self, max_batch_size: int = 256, 
                 max_num_blocks: int = 10000):
        self.waiting_queue: List[InferenceRequest] = []  # priority queue
        self.running_batch: List[InferenceRequest] = []
        self.preempted_queue: List[InferenceRequest] = []
        self.kv_manager = PagedKVCacheManager(max_num_blocks)
        self.max_batch_size = max_batch_size
    
    def add_request(self, request: InferenceRequest):
        """新請求加入 waiting queue"""
        heapq.heappush(
            self.waiting_queue, 
            (-request.priority, request.arrival_time, request)
        )
    
    def schedule_step(self) -> List[InferenceRequest]:
        """
        每個 forward pass 前調用
        決定哪些 requests 參與這次 batch
        """
        # 1. 移出已完成的 requests
        self.running_batch = [
            r for r in self.running_batch 
            if r.state != RequestState.FINISHED
        ]
        
        # 2. 嘗試恢復被搶佔的 requests (FCFS)
        while self.preempted_queue and self._can_add_to_batch():
            req = self.preempted_queue.pop(0)
            if self.kv_manager.allocate(
                req.request_id, req.num_total_tokens
            ):
                req.state = RequestState.DECODING
                self.running_batch.append(req)
            else:
                self.preempted_queue.insert(0, req)
                break
        
        # 3. 嘗試加入新請求
        while self.waiting_queue and self._can_add_to_batch():
            _, _, req = heapq.heappop(self.waiting_queue)
            if self.kv_manager.allocate(
                req.request_id, len(req.prompt_tokens)
            ):
                req.state = RequestState.PREFILL
                self.running_batch.append(req)
            else:
                # Memory 不夠 → 嘗試 preempt
                if not self._preempt_lowest_priority():
                    # 無法騰出空間，放回隊列
                    heapq.heappush(
                        self.waiting_queue,
                        (-req.priority, req.arrival_time, req)
                    )
                    break
        
        return self.running_batch
    
    def _can_add_to_batch(self) -> bool:
        return len(self.running_batch) < self.max_batch_size
    
    def _preempt_lowest_priority(self) -> bool:
        """搶佔最低優先級的 running request"""
        if not self.running_batch:
            return False
        # 找到優先級最低的
        victim = min(self.running_batch, key=lambda r: r.priority)
        victim.state = RequestState.PREEMPTED
        self.running_batch.remove(victim)
        self.kv_manager.free_sequence(victim.request_id)
        self.preempted_queue.append(victim)
        return True

3.3 Prefill/Decode Disaggregation（計算分離）

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ Prefill/Decode Disaggregation │ ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ │ │ │ ┌───────────────────────┐ ┌───────────────────────┐ │ │ │ Prefill Workers │ │ Decode Workers │ │ │ │ (Compute-bound) │ │ (Memory-bound) │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ • Process full prompt│ │ • Generate 1 token │ │ │ │ • High FLOPS usage │ │ • Low FLOPS, high BW │ │ │ │ • Batch=1-4 (large) │ │ • Batch=64-256 │ │ │ │ • GPU: H100 SXM │ │ • GPU: H100/A100 │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ Output: KV Cache │ │ Input: KV Cache │ │ │ └───────────┬───────────┘ └───────────┬───────────┘ │ │ │ │ │ │ └──────────┐ ┌─────────────┘ │ │ ▼ ▼ │ │ ┌──────────────────────┐ │ │ │ KV Cache Transfer │ │ │ │ (NVLink / RDMA) │ │ │ │ ~50μs per transfer │ │ │ └──────────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ Why disaggregate? │ │ • Prefill: compute-bound (FLOPs ∝ seq_len²) │ │ • Decode: memory-bandwidth-bound (read entire KV cache) │ │ • Different optimal batch sizes → resource waste if mixed │ │ • Separate → each pool optimized independently │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

3.4 Speculative Decoding（推測解碼）

class SpeculativeDecoder:
    """
    推測解碼：用小模型快速 draft K 個 tokens，大模型一次 verify
    
    原理：
    - Draft model (7B): 快速生成 K=5 個候選 tokens
    - Target model (175B): 一次 forward pass 同時 verify 所有 K tokens
    - 接受率 α ≈ 70-90% → 實際加速 2-3x
    
    數學保證：輸出分佈與純 target model 完全一致（rejection sampling）
    """
    def __init__(self, target_model, draft_model, num_speculative_tokens=5):
        self.target = target_model    # 175B (大模型)
        self.draft = draft_model      # 7B (小模型)
        self.K = num_speculative_tokens
    
    def generate_step(self, input_ids, kv_cache):
        """
        一次 speculative decoding step:
        1. Draft model 生成 K 個 token (fast)
        2. Target model 一次 verify K+1 個位置 (parallel)
        3. Rejection sampling 決定接受幾個
        """
        # Step 1: Draft K tokens (auto-regressive, but fast)
        draft_tokens = []
        draft_probs = []
        draft_kv = kv_cache.copy()
        
        for _ in range(self.K):
            logits = self.draft.forward(input_ids, draft_kv)
            prob = softmax(logits[-1])
            token = sample(prob)
            draft_tokens.append(token)
            draft_probs.append(prob)
            input_ids = concat(input_ids, [token])
        
        # Step 2: Target model verify all K positions in ONE forward pass
        # 這是加速的關鍵：K 次 draft forward → 1 次 target forward
        all_positions = concat(input_ids[:-self.K], draft_tokens)
        target_logits = self.target.forward(all_positions, kv_cache)
        target_probs = [softmax(l) for l in target_logits[-self.K-1:]]
        
        # Step 3: Rejection sampling
        accepted_tokens = []
        for i in range(self.K):
            draft_p = draft_probs[i][draft_tokens[i]]
            target_p = target_probs[i][draft_tokens[i]]
            
            # Accept with probability min(1, target_p / draft_p)
            if np.random.random() < min(1.0, target_p / draft_p):
                accepted_tokens.append(draft_tokens[i])
            else:
                # Reject: sample from adjusted distribution
                adjusted = np.maximum(
                    target_probs[i] - draft_probs[i], 0
                )
                adjusted /= adjusted.sum()
                new_token = sample(adjusted)
                accepted_tokens.append(new_token)
                break  # Stop accepting after first rejection
        
        # 如果全部接受，bonus: 從 target_probs[K] 再取一個 token
        if len(accepted_tokens) == self.K:
            bonus_token = sample(target_probs[self.K])
            accepted_tokens.append(bonus_token)
        
        return accepted_tokens  # 返回 1 ~ K+1 個 tokens

3.5 Prefix Caching（前綴快取）

class PrefixCache:
    """
    觀察：多數對話共享相同的 system prompt (e.g., 2000 tokens)
    
    如果 1000 並發用戶都有相同 system prompt：
    - Without cache: 1000 × 2000 tokens × KV size = 巨大浪費
    - With cache: 1 份 KV + 1000 個 CoW references = 節省 999x
    
    實作：Radix Tree 存儲 prefix → KV block mapping
    """
    def __init__(self):
        # hash(token_sequence) → list of KV block IDs
        self.cache: Dict[int, List[int]] = {}
        self.hit_count = 0
        self.miss_count = 0
    
    def lookup(self, prompt_tokens: List[int]) -> Optional[int]:
        """
        找到最長匹配前綴
        返回: 可以跳過的 token 數量（已有 cached KV）
        """
        best_match_len = 0
        
        # 從最長到最短嘗試匹配
        for length in range(len(prompt_tokens), 0, -16):  # step=block_size
            prefix_hash = hash(tuple(prompt_tokens[:length]))
            if prefix_hash in self.cache:
                best_match_len = length
                self.hit_count += 1
                break
        
        if best_match_len == 0:
            self.miss_count += 1
        
        return best_match_len
    
    def insert(self, prompt_tokens: List[int], kv_blocks: List[int]):
        """Prefill 完成後存入 cache"""
        prefix_hash = hash(tuple(prompt_tokens))
        self.cache[prefix_hash] = kv_blocks
    
    @property
    def hit_rate(self) -> float:
        total = self.hit_count + self.miss_count
        return self.hit_count / total if total > 0 else 0.0

🔹 Step 4: Evaluation & Metrics

指標定義目標優化手段
TTFTTime to First Token<500msPrefix cache, chunked prefill
TBTTime Between Tokens<50msContinuous batching, speculative
ThroughputTokens/sec/GPU>2000Batching, quantization
GPU UtilSM occupancy>70%Prefill/Decode split
Memory UtilKV cache efficiency>90%PagedAttention
Cost$/1M tokens<$10INT4 quantization, spot GPUs

⚖️ Key Trade-offs

  • Batch Size vs Latency：Larger batch → higher throughput but higher per-request latency. Solution: SLA-aware scheduler with preemption.
  • Quantization vs Quality：INT4 saves 4x memory but may degrade quality on reasoning tasks. Solution: selective quantization (attention in FP16, FFN in INT4).
  • Speculative Decoding overhead：Draft model uses GPU cycles. Only worthwhile when acceptance rate >60% and target model is memory-bound.
  • Prefix cache memory：More cache → higher hit rate but less space for new sequences. Solution: LRU eviction with frequency-weighted priority.

🔹 Follow-up Questions

Q1: 如何在有限 GPU memory 中支援 128K context？

答：多層次記憶體管理：

  1. PagedAttention：按需分配 KV blocks，避免預留全部 128K
  2. KV Cache offloading：不常存取的 KV blocks swap 到 CPU memory 或 NVMe SSD
  3. Windowed attention：對於超長 context，使用 sliding window（如 Mistral）+ 關鍵位置 full attention
  4. KV Cache compression：GQA (Grouped Query Attention) 已將 KV heads 從 96 減少到 8，減少 12x
# Tiered memory management
class TieredKVCache:
    def __init__(self):
        self.gpu_cache = {}   # Hot: 最近 4K tokens
        self.cpu_cache = {}   # Warm: 4K-32K tokens  
        self.disk_cache = {}  # Cold: 32K-128K tokens
    
    def access(self, seq_id, position):
        """LRU-like promotion on access"""
        if position in self.gpu_cache.get(seq_id, {}):
            return self.gpu_cache[seq_id][position]  # Hit
        elif position in self.cpu_cache.get(seq_id, {}):
            # Promote CPU → GPU (async prefetch)
            self._promote_to_gpu(seq_id, position)
            return self.cpu_cache[seq_id][position]
Q2: 如何實現 Tensor Parallelism 跨 8 GPU？

答：Tensor Parallelism (TP) 將每一層的 weight matrix 切分到多個 GPU：

  • Column Parallel：FFN 的第一層 W1 按 column 切分，每 GPU 計算部分 neurons
  • Row Parallel：FFN 的第二層 W2 按 row 切分，AllReduce 合併結果
  • Attention：不同 heads 分佈在不同 GPU 上

通訊開銷：每層 2 次 AllReduce（attention output + FFN output）。NVLink bandwidth = 900 GB/s (H100) → overhead <5%。

175B on 8x A100：350GB / 8 = 43.75GB per GPU（剩餘空間給 KV cache）。

Q3: 如何做 Graceful degradation 當流量暴增？

答：三層防線：

  1. Admission Control：超過 capacity 時 reject 新請求（返回 503 + retry-after header）
  2. Quality Degradation：自動切換到較小模型（175B → 70B → 13B），犧牲品質保可用性
  3. Dynamic Batching：增大 batch size + 允許更高 latency（通知客戶端）

Priority System：

  • P0 (paid API): 保證 SLA，永不 preempt
  • P1 (free tier): 可被 preempt，best-effort
  • P2 (batch jobs): 低優先級，空閒時才處理
Q4: 如何做到 model rollout 不中斷服務？

答：Blue-Green Deployment + Traffic Shifting：

  1. 新模型部署到 Shadow cluster，先跑 offline evaluation
  2. 通過後切 5% → 25% → 50% → 100% traffic（canary）
  3. 每階段監控：latency, quality (human eval), cost
  4. 異常時 instant rollback（切回舊模型 cluster）

KV cache 不兼容問題：新模型 architecture 變更時，active sessions 的 KV cache 無法遷移 → graceful drain（等 active sessions 完成再切流量）。

Q5: INT4 量化對模型品質的影響如何量化？

答：

精度Model SizePerplexity (WikiText)MMLUThroughput
FP16350GB3.56 (baseline)86.4%1x
INT8 (GPTQ)175GB3.58 (+0.6%)86.1%1.8x
INT4 (AWQ)87GB3.65 (+2.5%)85.2%2.5x
INT4 (GGML)87GB3.72 (+4.5%)84.5%2.3x

最佳實踐：AWQ (Activation-aware Weight Quantization) — 根據 activation magnitude 保護重要 weights 不被量化，品質損失最小。

Selective Quantization：Attention QKV projections 用 FP16（敏感），FFN 用 INT4（robust），混合後 quality ≈ INT8 但 speed ≈ INT4。

Q6: 如何監控 LLM serving 的健康狀態？

答：三維度監控：

  1. Infrastructure metrics：GPU utilization, memory usage, network bandwidth, queue depth
  2. Service metrics：TTFT/TBT p50/p99, throughput, error rate, timeout rate
  3. Quality metrics：Average output length (突然變短 = 異常), repetition rate, refusal rate

Alert triggers：

  • GPU util < 30% for 5 min → possible deadlock or scheduling bug
  • KV cache utilization > 95% → preemption storm imminent
  • TTFT p99 > 2s → prefill queue backed up
  • Output token length mean drop >30% → possible model corruption

📖 Chapter 23: Design Feature Platform

題目：設計一個支援 1000+ features、線上延遲 <10ms 的統一 Feature Platform

🔹 Step 1: Requirements Clarification

Functional Requirements

  • Feature Registry：統一註冊、發現、管理所有 features（metadata + lineage）
  • Offline Feature Store：批次計算 features for training（point-in-time correct）
  • Online Feature Store：線上 serving <10ms 取得 features
  • Near-Realtime Features：streaming window aggregations（5min/1hr/24hr windows）
  • Feature Monitoring：自動偵測 training-serving skew + data drift
  • Feature Sharing：跨團隊共享 features，避免重複計算

Non-Functional Requirements

  • Scale: 1000+ feature definitions, 100M+ entities (users/items)
  • Online Latency: p99 < 10ms for batch get (20 features)
  • Freshness: batch features (daily), streaming features (5 min), online features (realtime)
  • Consistency: training 和 serving 使用相同 feature 定義（消除 skew）
  • Cost: 比每個團隊自建節省 50%+ 運算/儲存成本

Scale Estimation

維度數值說明
Feature definitions1000+跨 50+ 團隊
Entities100M users + 10M items每個有 ~100 features
Online reads500K QPS50K model QPS × 10 features/request
Storage (online)~100GB100M × 100 feat × 8 bytes avg
Storage (offline)~50TB歷史 2 年 daily snapshots
Streaming events1M events/secUser action stream
