🔹 Step 3: Deep Dive

3.1 Feature Definition DSL（特徵定義語言）

💡 核心設計原則：Write once, serve everywhere。同一份 feature 定義自動生成 batch pipeline (training) + streaming pipeline (near-realtime) + online serving code。消除 training-serving skew 的根源。 
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Optional, Callable
from enum import Enum

class Freshness(Enum):
    BATCH = "batch"           # 每日更新
    STREAMING = "streaming"   # 5 分鐘窗口
    REALTIME = "realtime"     # 請求時計算

class DataType(Enum):
    FLOAT = "float32"
    INT = "int64"
    STRING = "string"
    EMBEDDING = "float32[]"
    BOOL = "bool"

@dataclass
class FeatureDefinition:
    """
    統一的 Feature 定義
    一份定義 → 自動生成 batch + streaming + online pipelines
    """
    name: str                          # e.g., "user_click_rate_7d"
    entity: str                        # e.g., "user", "item", "user-item"
    dtype: DataType
    freshness: Freshness
    owner: str                         # team alias
    description: str
    
    # Data source
    source_table: Optional[str] = None  # batch: BigQuery/Hive table
    source_stream: Optional[str] = None # streaming: Kafka topic
    
    # Transformation logic (unified)
    transform_sql: Optional[str] = None  # SQL-based (most common)
    transform_fn: Optional[Callable] = None  # Python UDF (complex logic)
    
    # Serving config
    default_value: any = 0.0           # Fallback for missing values
    ttl_seconds: int = 86400           # Online store TTL
    
    # Monitoring
    expected_range: tuple = (0.0, 1.0) # Alert if outside range
    freshness_sla_minutes: int = 60    # Alert if stale
    
    # Metadata
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    version: int = 1


# === Example Feature Definitions ===

user_click_rate_7d = FeatureDefinition(
    name="user_click_rate_7d",
    entity="user",
    dtype=DataType.FLOAT,
    freshness=Freshness.BATCH,
    owner="recommendation-team",
    description="用戶過去 7 天的平均點擊率",
    source_table="warehouse.user_events",
    transform_sql="""
        SELECT 
            user_id,
            COUNT(CASE WHEN action = 'click' THEN 1 END) * 1.0 / 
            NULLIF(COUNT(CASE WHEN action = 'impression' THEN 1 END), 0)
            AS user_click_rate_7d
        FROM warehouse.user_events
        WHERE event_date BETWEEN DATE_SUB('{date}', 7) AND '{date}'
        GROUP BY user_id
    """,
    default_value=0.05,  # Global average as fallback
    expected_range=(0.0, 0.5),
    tags=["behavioral", "ctr"]
)

user_realtime_click_count_5m = FeatureDefinition(
    name="user_realtime_click_count_5m",
    entity="user",
    dtype=DataType.INT,
    freshness=Freshness.STREAMING,
    owner="recommendation-team",
    description="用戶最近 5 分鐘點擊次數（Flink 滑動窗口）",
    source_stream="kafka://user-actions",
    transform_sql="""
        SELECT 
            user_id,
            COUNT(*) AS user_realtime_click_count_5m
        FROM user_actions
        WHERE action = 'click'
        GROUP BY 
            user_id,
            TUMBLE(event_time, INTERVAL '5' MINUTE)
    """,
    default_value=0,
    ttl_seconds=600,  # 10 min TTL (streaming feature)
    expected_range=(0, 100),
    freshness_sla_minutes=5
)

3.2 Point-in-Time Correctness（時間點正確性）

💡 最常見的 data leakage 錯誤：用 training sample 時間點之後的數據計算 feature → 洩漏未來信息 → 模型 offline 指標虛高但 online 無效。

解法：Feature Platform 自動實現 temporal join — 對於每個 training sample，只 lookup 該時間點之前（或之前）的 feature 值。 
import pandas as pd
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

class PointInTimeJoiner:
    """
    Point-in-Time Correct Feature Retrieval for Training
    
    問題情境：
    - Training label: user_123 在 2024-01-15 10:00 點擊了 item_A
    - Feature: user_click_rate_7d
    - 正確: 使用 2024-01-14 (或更早) 的 snapshot
    - 錯誤: 使用 2024-01-15 或之後的 snapshot (data leakage!)
    """
    def join(self, labels_df: pd.DataFrame, 
             feature_table: str, 
             feature_name: str,
             entity_col: str = "user_id",
             timestamp_col: str = "event_timestamp") -> pd.DataFrame:
        """
        對每個 label row，找到 timestamp 之前最新的 feature 值
        
        SQL 等效 (BigQuery):
        SELECT l.*, f.feature_value
        FROM labels l
        LEFT JOIN feature_snapshots f
        ON l.user_id = f.user_id
        AND f.snapshot_date = (
            SELECT MAX(snapshot_date) 
            FROM feature_snapshots 
            WHERE user_id = l.user_id 
            AND snapshot_date < DATE(l.event_timestamp)
        )
        """
        # 實作：asof merge (pandas) 或 temporal join (Spark)
        feature_df = self._load_feature_snapshots(feature_table)
        
        # Sort both by timestamp
        labels_sorted = labels_df.sort_values(timestamp_col)
        feature_sorted = feature_df.sort_values("snapshot_timestamp")
        
        # Asof merge: for each label, find latest feature BEFORE label timestamp
        result = pd.merge_asof(
            labels_sorted,
            feature_sorted,
            left_on=timestamp_col,
            right_on="snapshot_timestamp",
            by=entity_col,
            direction="backward",  # 只看過去的值
            tolerance=pd.Timedelta("7 days")  # 最多往回看 7 天
        )
        
        # 填充缺失值 (entity 太新，沒有歷史 feature)
        result[feature_name] = result[feature_name].fillna(
            self.feature_registry.get_default(feature_name)
        )
        
        return result
    
    def validate_no_leakage(self, result_df: pd.DataFrame,
                            timestamp_col: str, 
                            feature_snapshot_col: str) -> bool:
        """驗證沒有未來數據洩漏"""
        violations = result_df[
            result_df[feature_snapshot_col] >= result_df[timestamp_col]
        ]
        if len(violations) > 0:
            raise DataLeakageError(
                f"Found {len(violations)} rows with future feature values!"
            )
        return True

3.3 Training-Serving Skew Detection

import numpy as np
from scipy import stats

class FeatureSkewDetector:
    """
    偵測 Training 和 Serving 之間的 feature 分佈差異
    
    常見 skew 原因：
    1. Bug: training 用 log(x+1)，serving 忘了 +1
    2. Data source change: 上游表 schema 變更
    3. Time drift: training 用舊數據，serving 是最新
    4. Population shift: 新用戶比例增加
    """
    def __init__(self, alert_threshold_psi: float = 0.2,
                 alert_threshold_ks: float = 0.05):
        self.psi_threshold = alert_threshold_psi
        self.ks_threshold = alert_threshold_ks
    
    def compute_psi(self, training_dist: np.ndarray, 
                    serving_dist: np.ndarray, 
                    n_bins: int = 10) -> float:
        """
        Population Stability Index (PSI)
        PSI < 0.1: 無顯著差異
        0.1 < PSI < 0.2: 需關注
        PSI > 0.2: 顯著偏移，需調查
        
        公式: PSI = Σ (P_serving - P_training) × ln(P_serving / P_training)
        """
        # Bin edges from training distribution
        _, bin_edges = np.histogram(training_dist, bins=n_bins)
        
        # Count proportions in each bin
        train_counts, _ = np.histogram(training_dist, bins=bin_edges)
        serve_counts, _ = np.histogram(serving_dist, bins=bin_edges)
        
        # Avoid zero division
        train_pct = (train_counts + 1) / (len(training_dist) + n_bins)
        serve_pct = (serve_counts + 1) / (len(serving_dist) + n_bins)
        
        psi = np.sum(
            (serve_pct - train_pct) * np.log(serve_pct / train_pct)
        )
        return psi
    
    def detect_skew(self, feature_name: str,
                    training_values: np.ndarray,
                    serving_values: np.ndarray) -> dict:
        """全面 skew 檢測報告"""
        psi = self.compute_psi(training_values, serving_values)
        ks_stat, ks_pvalue = stats.ks_2samp(training_values, serving_values)
        
        result = {
            "feature": feature_name,
            "psi": psi,
            "ks_statistic": ks_stat,
            "ks_pvalue": ks_pvalue,
            "training_mean": np.mean(training_values),
            "serving_mean": np.mean(serving_values),
            "mean_shift": abs(np.mean(serving_values) - np.mean(training_values)),
            "alert": psi > self.psi_threshold or ks_pvalue < self.ks_threshold
        }
        
        if result["alert"]:
            result["severity"] = "HIGH" if psi > 0.5 else "MEDIUM"
            result["recommendation"] = (
                "Investigate data source changes. "
                "Consider retraining model with recent data."
            )
        
        return result
    
    def run_daily_check(self, all_features: List[str]):
        """每日自動 skew 檢查（production monitoring）"""
        alerts = []
        for fname in all_features:
            train_vals = self.load_training_distribution(fname)
            serve_vals = self.load_serving_distribution(fname, last_24h=True)
            result = self.detect_skew(fname, train_vals, serve_vals)
            if result["alert"]:
                alerts.append(result)
        
        if alerts:
            self.send_alert(alerts)
        return alerts

3.4 Online Store 設計（<10ms batch get）

class OnlineFeatureService:
    """
    線上 Feature Serving - 目標 p99 < 10ms for 20 features
    
    設計決策：
    1. Redis Cluster (主) + DynamoDB (備) dual-write
    2. Entity-level batching: 一次 MGET 取所有 features for an entity
    3. Feature group pre-materialization: 常用 feature 組合預打包
    4. Circuit breaker: 單個 feature 失敗不影響整體 serving
    """
    def __init__(self, redis_cluster, dynamo_client):
        self.redis = redis_cluster
        self.dynamo = dynamo_client
        self.feature_registry = FeatureRegistry()
        self.circuit_breakers = {}
    
    def get_features(self, entity_type: str, entity_id: str,
                     feature_names: List[str]) -> Dict[str, any]:
        """
        批次取得多個 features (single entity)
        
        Key schema: {entity_type}:{entity_id}:{feature_group}
        Value: MessagePack encoded dict of feature values
        
        Performance:
        - Redis MGET: 1 RTT for all features (vs N RTTs)
        - Pre-packed feature groups: 1 key per group
        - Typical: 2-3 keys per request → < 5ms
        """
        results = {}
        missing = []
        
        # Group features by their feature_group for efficient lookup
        groups = self._group_by_pack(feature_names)
        
        # Batch read from Redis (single pipeline)
        pipe = self.redis.pipeline()
        group_keys = []
        for group_name, features in groups.items():
            key = f"{entity_type}:{entity_id}:{group_name}"
            pipe.get(key)
            group_keys.append((key, features))
        
        redis_results = pipe.execute()
        
        for (key, features), value in zip(group_keys, redis_results):
            if value is not None:
                decoded = msgpack.unpackb(value)
                for f in features:
                    results[f] = decoded.get(f)
            else:
                missing.extend(features)
        
        # Fallback: DynamoDB for cache misses
        if missing:
            dynamo_results = self._dynamo_fallback(
                entity_type, entity_id, missing
            )
            results.update(dynamo_results)
        
        # Fill defaults for still-missing features
        for f in feature_names:
            if f not in results or results[f] is None:
                results[f] = self.feature_registry.get_default(f)
        
        return results
    
    def get_features_batch(self, entity_type: str, 
                           entity_ids: List[str],
                           feature_names: List[str]) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
        """
        Multi-entity batch (e.g., score 1000 candidate items)
        使用 Redis pipeline 並行取所有 entities
        """
        pipe = self.redis.pipeline()
        for eid in entity_ids:
            for group in self._get_groups(feature_names):
                pipe.get(f"{entity_type}:{eid}:{group}")
        
        all_results = pipe.execute()
        # ... parse and return

🔹 Step 4: Evaluation & Metrics

指標目標監控方式
Online Latency (p99)<10msPer-request timing
Feature FreshnessSLA per featureLast-updated timestamp
Training-Serving Skew (PSI)<0.1Daily batch comparison
Feature Coverage>99% (non-null rate)Serving-time logging
Cost per featureTracked per ownerChargeback dashboard

⚖️ Key Trade-offs

  • Freshness vs Cost：Real-time features 需要 streaming infra (Flink + Kafka) → expensive。大多數 features 用 batch 就夠。
  • Consistency vs Latency：強一致性（每次都查最新）vs 最終一致性（允許短暫 stale）。推薦系統可接受分鐘級 stale。
  • Generality vs Performance：通用 DSL 方便開發，但某些 feature 需要手寫優化 pipeline。解法：支援 escape hatch (custom UDF)。
  • Shared vs Isolated：共享 feature 節省成本，但一個團隊改定義可能影響其他團隊。解法：版本化 + 明確 ownership + breaking change review。

🔹 Follow-up Questions

Q1: 如何防止 training-serving skew？

答：多層防線：

  1. Unified Definition：同一份 transform logic 生成 batch 和 streaming pipeline → 消除邏輯差異
  2. Feature logging：Serving 時 log 實際使用的 feature 值 → 用這些 logged features 做 training（最強保證）
  3. Automated testing：每次 feature 定義變更 → 自動比較新舊版在 holdout set 上的分佈差異
  4. Runtime monitoring：PSI/KS-test 持續監控，drift 超閾值自動告警

Google 的做法 (Feast → Vertex Feature Store)：推薦「feature logging + replay training」模式 — online serving 時 log 所有 feature 值到 BigQuery → 用 logged features + labels 做 training → 完全消除 skew。

Q2: Streaming feature 和 Batch feature 如何統一管理？

答：Lambda Architecture → Kappa Architecture 演進：

  • Lambda（傳統）：Batch 和 Streaming 各自計算，serving 時 merge。問題：兩套邏輯難保一致。
  • Kappa（推薦）：所有計算基於 event stream。Batch 是 stream 的特例（replay historical events）。一份 Flink SQL → backfill 和 realtime 用同一邏輯。

Hybrid approach (最實用)：

  • Simple aggregations (count, sum, avg): Flink SQL 統一處理（Kappa style）
  • Complex features (ML-derived embeddings, graph features): Batch only (Spark)
  • Serving 時 merge: batch_value + streaming_delta = final_value
Q3: 如何處理 late-arriving data？

問題：事件可能延遲到達（網路問題、client 離線後上線），導致 streaming window 計算不準確。

解法：

  1. Watermark-based processing (Flink)：設定 allowed lateness（如 10 min），遲到但在 window 內的事件仍可更新結果
  2. Batch correction：每日 batch job 使用完整數據重算 → 覆蓋 streaming 的近似值（最終一致）
  3. Side output：極度遲到的事件（>1 hour）進入 dead letter queue，不影響 realtime feature 但記錄 audit
Q4: Feature Store 如何支援 model 的 A/B test？

答：Feature versioning + experiment-aware serving：

  • 每個 feature 可以有多個版本同時 live（v1, v2）
  • Model A 用 feature_x_v1，Model B 用 feature_x_v2
  • Feature serving API 接受 feature_version_map 參數
  • Feature logging 記錄每個 request 實際使用的版本 → 訓練時可追溯
# Experiment-aware feature retrieval
def get_features_for_experiment(user_id, experiment_config):
    version_map = experiment_config.get_feature_versions()
    # {"user_click_rate_7d": "v2", "user_embedding": "v1"}
    
    features = {}
    for fname, version in version_map.items():
        key = f"user:{user_id}:{fname}:{version}"
        features[fname] = redis.get(key)
    return features
Q5: 跨團隊 feature sharing 的治理如何做？

答：Feature Marketplace + Governance Framework：

  1. Ownership：每個 feature 必須有明確 owner team，負責 SLA 和 correctness
  2. SLA contract：freshness (daily/5min)、availability (99.9%)、accuracy (NULL rate <1%)
  3. Deprecation policy：至少 30 天通知 + migration guide，不可 silently remove
  4. Access control：RBAC — 只有 approved 團隊可以使用某些 PII features
  5. Cost attribution：按 usage 比例 chargeback，鼓勵刪除不用的 features
Q6: 如何處理 feature 計算失敗？

答：Multi-layer fallback：

  1. Primary：Redis (最新值)
  2. Fallback 1：DynamoDB (前一天的值)
  3. Fallback 2：Static default value (feature 定義中指定)
  4. Circuit breaker：某個 feature source 連續失敗 → 自動 fallback + alert owner

關鍵設計：Feature 缺失不能 block 整個 serving request。使用 partial results + degrade gracefully。

📖 Chapter 24: Design ML Training Platform

題目：設計支援 1000+ data scientists、GPU 利用率 >70% 的 ML Training Platform

🔹 Step 1: Requirements Clarification

Functional Requirements

  • Job submission：用戶提交 training job（code + config + data path）
  • Multi-framework：支援 PyTorch, JAX, TensorFlow
  • Distributed training：支援 data parallel, tensor parallel, pipeline parallel
  • Experiment tracking：自動記錄 hyperparameters, metrics, artifacts
  • Resource management：GPU/CPU/Memory 配額管理
  • Auto-recovery：training job 中斷自動恢復（checkpoint resume）

Non-Functional Requirements

  • Users: 1000+ data scientists, 100+ teams
  • GPU Cluster: 1000+ GPUs (mix A100/H100)
  • GPU Utilization: >70% cluster-wide average
  • Job scheduling latency: <5 min from submit to running (for available quota)
  • Spot instance support: 3x cost savings with automatic preemption handling
  • Fault tolerance: auto-resume from last checkpoint within 10 min

🔹 Step 3: Deep Dive

3.1 Gang Scheduling（組調度）

💡 為什麼需要 Gang Scheduling：分散式訓練需要所有 workers 同時啟動（NCCL AllReduce 需要所有 rank 就緒）。如果 Job 需要 32 GPUs 但只有 28 空閒 → 不能部分啟動 → 必須等齊所有 32 GPUs 才能一起啟動。這就是 "gang" scheduling。 
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Set
from enum import Enum
import time

class JobState(Enum):
    PENDING = "pending"
    SCHEDULED = "scheduled"
    RUNNING = "running"
    PREEMPTED = "preempted"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"

@dataclass
class TrainingJob:
    job_id: str
    user: str
    team: str
    num_gpus: int              # 需要的 GPU 數量
    num_nodes: int             # 需要的 node 數量 (num_gpus / 8)
    priority: int              # 0-100, 越高越優先
    preemptible: bool          # 是否可被搶佔 (spot jobs = True)
    max_duration_hours: float  # 最大運行時間
    gpu_type: str = "any"      # "A100", "H100", or "any"
    topology_requirement: str = "none"  # "same_switch", "same_rack", "none"
    submit_time: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    state: JobState = JobState.PENDING
    assigned_nodes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    checkpoint_path: Optional[str] = None

class GangScheduler:
    """
    Gang Scheduler: 保證分散式 training job 的所有 workers 同時獲得資源
    
    算法：
    1. 按 priority + wait_time 排序 pending jobs
    2. 對每個 job 嘗試找到 num_nodes 個符合條件的空閒 nodes
    3. 找到 → 全部分配（atomic）；找不到 → 嘗試 preemption 或等待
    4. Backfill: 用小 job 填充大 job 之間的碎片
    """
    def __init__(self, cluster_nodes: Dict[str, 'NodeInfo']):
        self.nodes = cluster_nodes
        self.pending_queue: List[TrainingJob] = []
        self.running_jobs: Dict[str, TrainingJob] = {}
        self.team_quotas: Dict[str, int] = {}  # team → max GPUs
    
    def schedule(self):
        """主調度循環（每 10 秒執行）"""
        # Sort by effective priority
        self.pending_queue.sort(
            key=lambda j: self._effective_priority(j), reverse=True
        )
        
        for job in list(self.pending_queue):
            # Check team quota
            if not self._check_quota(job):
                continue
            
            # Try to find suitable nodes (gang allocation)
            suitable_nodes = self._find_nodes(job)
            
            if suitable_nodes:
                # Atomic allocation
                self._allocate(job, suitable_nodes)
            elif job.priority > 50 and not job.preemptible:
                # High priority: try preemption
                self._try_preempt_for(job)
            # else: wait in queue
        
        # Backfill: try small jobs that fit in gaps
        self._backfill()
    
    def _find_nodes(self, job: TrainingJob) -> Optional[List[str]]:
        """
        找到 num_nodes 個空閒節點
        考慮：GPU 類型、network topology、故障隔離
        """
        available = [
            nid for nid, info in self.nodes.items()
            if info.is_available()
            and (job.gpu_type == "any" or info.gpu_type == job.gpu_type)
        ]
        
        if len(available) < job.num_nodes:
            return None
        
        # Topology-aware selection
        if job.topology_requirement == "same_switch":
            # 優先選同一 network switch 下的 nodes (低延遲通訊)
            switch_groups = self._group_by_switch(available)
            for switch, nodes in switch_groups.items():
                if len(nodes) >= job.num_nodes:
                    return nodes[:job.num_nodes]
            return None  # 無法滿足 topology 要求
        
        # Default: best-fit bin-packing
        return sorted(available, 
                     key=lambda n: self.nodes[n].free_gpus, 
                     reverse=True)[:job.num_nodes]
    
    def _effective_priority(self, job: TrainingJob) -> float:
        """
        Fair-share priority: 
        base_priority + wait_time_bonus - over_quota_penalty
        """
        wait_hours = (time.time() - job.submit_time) / 3600
        wait_bonus = min(wait_hours * 5, 30)  # Max +30 after 6 hours
        
        team_usage = self._get_team_usage(job.team)
        team_quota = self.team_quotas.get(job.team, 100)
        over_quota_penalty = max(0, (team_usage - team_quota) * 2)
        
        return job.priority + wait_bonus - over_quota_penalty
    
    def _try_preempt_for(self, high_prio_job: TrainingJob):
        """
        搶佔低優先級 jobs 為高優先級 job 騰出空間
        只搶佔 preemptible=True 的 jobs (spot instances)
        """
        preemptible_jobs = [
            j for j in self.running_jobs.values()
            if j.preemptible and j.priority < high_prio_job.priority
        ]
        
        # Sort by lowest priority first
        preemptible_jobs.sort(key=lambda j: j.priority)
        
        freed_nodes = []
        for victim in preemptible_jobs:
            # Signal checkpoint + graceful shutdown
            self._preempt_job(victim)
            freed_nodes.extend(victim.assigned_nodes)
            
            if len(freed_nodes) >= high_prio_job.num_nodes:
                self._allocate(high_prio_job, 
                             freed_nodes[:high_prio_job.num_nodes])
                return True
        
        return False
    
    def _backfill(self):
        """
        Backfill: 用預計不會影響高優先級 job 的小 job 填碎片
        條件：小 job 的預計結束時間 < 高優先級 job 的預計資源可用時間
        """
        for job in self.pending_queue:
            if job.num_gpus <= 8:  # Only backfill small jobs
                available = self._find_nodes(job)
                if available:
                    # Check: won't block any higher-priority waiting jobs
                    job_end_time = time.time() + job.max_duration_hours * 3600
                    if not self._would_block_higher_priority(job, job_end_time):
                        self._allocate(job, available)

3.2 Elastic Training（彈性訓練）

class ElasticTrainingController:
    """
    Elastic Training: 訓練過程中動態增減 workers
    
    場景：
    1. 用 16 GPUs 開始訓練
    2. 有 16 GPUs 空閒 → 動態擴展到 32 GPUs (加速 ~1.8x)
    3. 高優先級 job 來了 → 收縮回 16 GPUs (不停止，繼續訓練)
    
    支援框架：PyTorch Elastic (torchelastic), Horovod Elastic
    """
    def __init__(self, min_workers: int, max_workers: int, 
                 current_workers: int):
        self.min_workers = min_workers
        self.max_workers = max_workers
        self.current_workers = current_workers
    
    def scale_decision(self, cluster_state: dict) -> int:
        """
        決定是否 scale up/down
        返回目標 worker 數
        """
        free_gpus = cluster_state["free_gpus"]
        pending_high_prio = cluster_state["pending_high_priority_gpus"]
        
        # Scale up: 有空閒 GPU 且沒有高優先級 job 等待
        if free_gpus >= 8 and pending_high_prio == 0:
            new_target = min(
                self.current_workers + (free_gpus // 8) * 8,
                self.max_workers
            )
            return new_target
        
        # Scale down: 高優先級 job 需要 GPU
        elif pending_high_prio > 0:
            gpus_to_release = min(
                pending_high_prio,
                self.current_workers - self.min_workers
            )
            return self.current_workers - gpus_to_release
        
        return self.current_workers
    
    def handle_scale_event(self, old_world_size: int, 
                           new_world_size: int,
                           model, optimizer):
        """
        Scale 事件發生時：
        1. Save checkpoint
        2. Re-initialize distributed group
        3. Adjust learning rate (linear scaling rule)
        4. Resume from checkpoint
        """
        # Linear scaling rule: lr ∝ world_size
        lr_scale = new_world_size / old_world_size
        for param_group in optimizer.param_groups:
            param_group['lr'] *= lr_scale
        
        # Gradient accumulation 也需要調整
        # effective_batch = per_gpu_batch × world_size × grad_accum
        # 保持 effective_batch 不變 → 調整 grad_accum
        new_grad_accum = max(1, int(
            self.effective_batch_size / 
            (self.per_gpu_batch * new_world_size)
        ))
        
        print(f"Scaled: {old_world_size} → {new_world_size} workers, "
              f"LR: {lr_scale:.2f}x, GradAccum: {new_grad_accum}")
        
        return new_grad_accum

3.3 Spot Instance Management（成本優化）

class SpotInstanceManager:
    """
    Spot GPU Instances: 比 on-demand 便宜 60-70%
    
    挑戰：
    - 隨時可能被 cloud provider 回收（2 分鐘警告）
    - 必須能 graceful checkpoint + resume
    
    策略：
    1. Frequent checkpointing (每 15-30 min)
    2. 多 AZ 分散風險
    3. Fallback to on-demand for critical training jobs
    4. Checkpoint-aware scheduling (接近 checkpoint 時不 scale down)
    """
    def __init__(self, checkpoint_interval_min: int = 15):
        self.checkpoint_interval = checkpoint_interval_min * 60
        self.last_checkpoint_time = time.time()
    
    def handle_spot_interruption(self, job: TrainingJob, 
                                  warning_seconds: int = 120):
        """
        收到 spot termination 通知：
        1. 立即 trigger emergency checkpoint
        2. 完成後 graceful shutdown
        3. 自動重新排隊（resume from checkpoint）
        """
        print(f"⚠️ Spot interruption for job {job.job_id}, "
              f"{warning_seconds}s to save")
        
        # Emergency checkpoint (async, must complete in 120s)
        checkpoint_path = self._emergency_checkpoint(job)
        
        if checkpoint_path:
            # 重新加入 scheduler queue with resume info
            job.state = JobState.PREEMPTED
            job.checkpoint_path = checkpoint_path
            # Scheduler will re-schedule on available nodes
            self.scheduler.requeue(job, priority_boost=10)
        else:
            # Checkpoint 失敗 → 從上一個 checkpoint 重啟
            job.checkpoint_path = job.last_successful_checkpoint
            self.scheduler.requeue(job, priority_boost=10)
    
    def optimal_checkpoint_strategy(self, 
                                     job_duration_hours: float,
                                     spot_interruption_rate: float = 0.05):
        """
        最優 checkpoint 頻率：
        - Too frequent: checkpoint overhead (5-10% training time)
        - Too rare: interruption → lose more progress
        
        數學最優: interval = sqrt(2 × checkpoint_cost × expected_compute_between_interruptions)
        """
        # 平均每 20 小時被中斷一次 (5% hourly rate)
        mean_time_between_interruptions = 1.0 / spot_interruption_rate  # 20 hours
        checkpoint_cost_hours = 0.1  # ~6 min to save 50GB checkpoint
        
        # Optimal interval (Young's formula)
        optimal_interval = (2 * checkpoint_cost_hours * 
                          mean_time_between_interruptions) ** 0.5
        
        return min(optimal_interval, 0.5)  # Cap at 30 min

3.4 GPU Utilization Optimization

class GPUUtilizationOptimizer:
    """
    目標：cluster-wide GPU utilization > 70%
    
    常見低利用率原因：
    1. 碎片化：4-GPU job 無法放入只有 2+2 空閒的節點
    2. I/O bottleneck：data loading 跟不上 GPU 計算
    3. Communication overhead：AllReduce 等待慢 worker
    4. Bubble (pipeline parallel)：micro-batch 之間的空閒
    
    解決方案：
    """
    def diagnose_low_utilization(self, job_metrics: dict) -> dict:
        """分析 GPU 利用率低的根因"""
        gpu_util = job_metrics["gpu_utilization"]  # 0-100%
        gpu_memory_util = job_metrics["gpu_memory_utilization"]
        io_wait_pct = job_metrics["io_wait_percentage"]
        comm_pct = job_metrics["communication_percentage"]
        
        diagnosis = {"gpu_util": gpu_util, "causes": []}
        
        if io_wait_pct > 20:
            diagnosis["causes"].append({
                "cause": "I/O Bottleneck",
                "evidence": f"IO wait {io_wait_pct}%",
                "fix": [
                    "Increase num_workers in DataLoader (try 4-8)",
                    "Enable prefetch_factor=2",
                    "Use faster storage (NVMe > network filesystem)",
                    "Pre-process data into optimized format (WebDataset, TFRecord)"
                ]
            })
        
        if comm_pct > 30:
            diagnosis["causes"].append({
                "cause": "Communication Overhead",
                "evidence": f"Comm {comm_pct}% of step time",
                "fix": [
                    "Increase batch size (more compute per comm)",
                    "Use gradient compression",
                    "Check network topology (same switch preferred)",
                    "Overlap compute and communication (DDP bucketing)"
                ]
            })
        
        if gpu_memory_util < 50:
            diagnosis["causes"].append({
                "cause": "Under-utilizing GPU Memory",
                "evidence": f"GPU memory only {gpu_memory_util}% used",
                "fix": [
                    "Increase batch size",
                    "Enable gradient checkpointing for larger model",
                    "Use mixed precision (FP16/BF16) to fit larger batch"
                ]
            })
        
        if gpu_util < 50 and not diagnosis["causes"]:
            diagnosis["causes"].append({
                "cause": "CPU Bottleneck (data preprocessing)",
                "evidence": "Low GPU util without IO/comm issues",
                "fix": [
                    "Move preprocessing to GPU (DALI, torchvision on GPU)",
                    "Pre-compute features offline",
                    "Profile with torch.profiler to find CPU hotspot"
                ]
            })
        
        return diagnosis
    
    def multi_tenant_packing(self, jobs: List[TrainingJob], 
                             nodes: List['NodeInfo']) -> Dict:
        """
        GPU Time-sharing for small jobs:
        - 多個 1-GPU jobs 共享同一 node
        - MIG (Multi-Instance GPU) 切分 A100 為多個小 GPU
        - MPS (Multi-Process Service) 允許多 process 共享 GPU
        """
        allocations = {}
        
        for job in sorted(jobs, key=lambda j: j.num_gpus, reverse=True):
            if job.num_gpus >= 8:
                # 大 job: 獨佔整個 node
                node = self._find_free_node(nodes)
                if node:
                    allocations[job.job_id] = {"node": node, "exclusive": True}
            elif job.num_gpus == 1:
                # 小 job: 共享 node (MIG slice)
                node = self._find_node_with_free_mig_slice(nodes)
                if node:
                    allocations[job.job_id] = {
                        "node": node, "exclusive": False, "mig_slice": "3g.40gb"
                    }
        
        return allocations

🔹 Step 4: Evaluation & Metrics

指標目標計算方式
Cluster GPU Utilization>70%Σ(GPU active time) / Σ(GPU total time)
Job Wait Time (p50)<5 minsubmit_time → start_time
Job Completion Rate>98%successful / total submitted
Spot Savings>50% cost reduction(on-demand cost - actual cost) / on-demand cost
Checkpoint Overhead<5% of training timecheckpoint_time / total_training_time
Fairness (Jain's Index)>0.9across teams by quota

⚖️ Key Trade-offs

  • Utilization vs Latency：高利用率（pack densely）→ 新 job 等待時間長。解法：預留 10-15% capacity for burst。
  • Spot vs Reliability：Spot 便宜 70% 但不穩定。解法：critical jobs on-demand, experimental on spot。
  • Gang Schedule vs Fragmentation：嚴格 gang → 碎片多。解法：backfill + elastic training。
  • Checkpoint Frequency vs Overhead：頻繁 checkpoint 安全但 overhead。解法：async checkpoint (不 block training)。

🔹 Follow-up Questions

Q1: 如何處理 straggler（慢 worker）問題？

問題：Data Parallel 中最慢的 worker 決定整體速度（AllReduce barrier）。一個壞 GPU 可能讓 32 GPUs 都在等待。

解法：

  1. Health monitoring：持續監控每個 GPU 的 step time。如果某 GPU 持續慢 >20% → 標記為 unhealthy
  2. Dynamic replacement：自動將 unhealthy worker 替換到新 node（elastic training 能力）
  3. Gradient timeout：AllReduce 設定 timeout，超時的 worker 的 gradient 被 skip（近似 gradient）
  4. Asynchronous SGD：不等待所有 workers（loss of strict synchronization but higher throughput）
Q2: 如何實現 async checkpoint 不影響 training 速度？

答：

  1. Background snapshotting：Copy model state to CPU memory (fast, ~1s for 50GB)
  2. Async write：Background thread 將 CPU snapshot 寫入 S3/GCS（不 block GPU training）
  3. Delta checkpoint：只存變化的部分（初始 full + 後續 incremental）→ 每次只存 ~5GB
  4. Copy-on-Write：使用 fork() 技巧 — fork 子進程繼承 model state snapshot，子進程負責寫入
import torch
import threading

class AsyncCheckpointer:
    def __init__(self, save_path: str):
        self.save_path = save_path
        self._bg_thread = None
    
    def save_async(self, model, optimizer, step: int):
        """Non-blocking checkpoint save"""
        # 1. Quick CPU snapshot (blocks training ~1-2s)
        cpu_state = {
            "model": {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()},
            "optimizer": optimizer.state_dict(),
            "step": step
        }
        
        # 2. Background write (doesn't block training)
        self._bg_thread = threading.Thread(
            target=self._write_to_storage,
            args=(cpu_state, f"{self.save_path}/step_{step}.pt")
        )
        self._bg_thread.start()
    
    def _write_to_storage(self, state, path):
        torch.save(state, path)  # This takes 30-60s for large models
Q3: Multi-tenancy 下如何保證公平性？

答：Hierarchical Fair-Share Scheduling (類似 YARN/Slurm)：

  1. Guaranteed quota：每個 team 有保底 GPU 配額（如 Team A: 100 GPUs），任何時候都能使用
  2. Opportunistic usage：其他 team 的閒置配額可以暫時借用（preemptible）
  3. Fair-share metric：actual_usage / guaranteed_quota。比值越低 → 優先級越高（被虧欠的 team）
  4. Decay：歷史使用量指數衰減，避免某 team 因過去多用而永遠低優先級
Q4: 大規模分散式訓練的 network topology 如何影響性能？

答：Network topology-aware scheduling 可提升 30%+ 訓練速度：

通訊層級帶寬延遲影響
Intra-GPU (NVLink)900 GB/s~1μsTensor Parallel
Intra-Node (PCIe)64 GB/s~5μsData Parallel within node
Same switch (IB)400 Gbps (50 GB/s)~5μsCross-node DP
Cross switch400 Gbps (shared)~20μsPipeline Parallel

最佳實踐：TP within node (NVLink), DP across nodes on same switch, PP across switches。

Q5: 如何監控並防止 GPU 記憶體洩漏？

答：

  • Runtime monitoring：每 N steps 記錄 GPU memory allocated/reserved → 如果持續增長 = leak
  • 常見洩漏原因：Python variable 引用了中間 tensor 不釋放、loss.backward() 後未清 intermediate activations、custom hook 累積 history
  • Platform-level 防護：設定 per-job GPU memory limit，超出 → OOM kill + checkpoint resume（比 hang 更好）
# Simple memory leak detector
class MemoryLeakDetector:
    def __init__(self, growth_threshold_mb=100):
        self.history = []
        self.threshold = growth_threshold_mb * 1e6
    
    def check(self, step: int):
        mem = torch.cuda.memory_allocated()
        self.history.append((step, mem))
        
        if len(self.history) > 100:
            # Linear regression on last 100 steps
            recent = self.history[-100:]
            growth_rate = (recent[-1][1] - recent[0][1]) / 100
            if growth_rate > self.threshold:
                print(f"⚠️ Memory leak detected: {growth_rate/1e6:.1f} MB/step")

📖 Chapter 25: Design RAG Q&A System

題目：設計一個支援 10M 文件、<3s 回答的 RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) 問答系統

🔹 Step 1: Requirements Clarification

Functional Requirements

  • 自然語言問答：用戶提問 → 從知識庫檢索相關文件 → LLM 生成答案
  • 引用標注：答案中標注來源文件 + 具體段落（可驗證）
  • Multi-hop reasoning：需要綜合多個文件才能回答的複雜問題
  • Access control：不同用戶只能存取有權限的文件
  • Multi-modal：支援文字、表格、圖片中的信息檢索
  • Conversational：支援多輪對話，追問澄清

Non-Functional Requirements

  • Corpus: 10M documents (~50B tokens total)
  • Latency: end-to-end <3s（含 retrieval + generation）
  • Accuracy: >85% factual correctness (vs 基於完整文件的 ground truth)
  • Hallucination rate: <5%（生成的信息必須有來源支撐）
  • QPS: 1000 concurrent queries
  • Freshness: 新文件 <1 hour 可被檢索到

Scale Estimation

維度數值說明
Documents10M平均 5000 tokens/doc
Chunks~100M平均 500 tokens/chunk, 10 chunks/doc
Embedding vectors100M × 768-dim~300GB raw, ~30GB quantized
Vector search<100ms for top-50IVF-PQ on 100M vectors
LLM generation~2s for 500 tokensContext: ~4000 tokens retrieved

🔹 Step 3: Deep Dive

3.1 Chunking Strategy（文件切分策略）

💡 Chunking 是 RAG 品質的關鍵：
  • Chunk 太大 → 雜訊多，LLM context 浪費在無關內容
  • Chunk 太小 → 缺少上下文，語義不完整
  • 固定字數切分 → 可能在句子中間斷開
最佳實踐：語義切分 (semantic chunking) — 根據段落結構 + embedding 相似度決定邊界。 
from typing import List, Tuple
import numpy as np

class SemanticChunker:
    """
    語義切分：基於 embedding 相似度偵測自然斷點
    
    算法：
    1. 將文件按句子拆分
    2. 計算相鄰句子的 embedding cosine similarity
    3. Similarity 突然下降的位置 = 語義邊界
    4. 在邊界處切分，同時保證 chunk size 在合理範圍
    """
    def __init__(self, embedding_model, 
                 min_chunk_size: int = 200,
                 max_chunk_size: int = 800,
                 similarity_threshold: float = 0.3):
        self.embedder = embedding_model
        self.min_size = min_chunk_size
        self.max_size = max_chunk_size
        self.threshold = similarity_threshold
    
    def chunk_document(self, text: str) -> List[dict]:
        """
        返回: [{text, start_char, end_char, metadata}, ...]
        """
        # 1. Split into sentences
        sentences = self._split_sentences(text)
        if len(sentences) <= 1:
            return [{"text": text, "start": 0, "end": len(text)}]
        
        # 2. Compute embeddings for each sentence
        embeddings = self.embedder.encode(sentences)
        
        # 3. Calculate similarity between consecutive sentences
        similarities = []
        for i in range(len(embeddings) - 1):
            sim = np.dot(embeddings[i], embeddings[i+1]) / (
                np.linalg.norm(embeddings[i]) * np.linalg.norm(embeddings[i+1])
            )
            similarities.append(sim)
        
        # 4. Find breakpoints (where similarity drops)
        breakpoints = self._find_breakpoints(similarities)
        
        # 5. Create chunks at breakpoints
        chunks = []
        current_chunk_sentences = []
        current_size = 0
        
        for i, sentence in enumerate(sentences):
            current_chunk_sentences.append(sentence)
            current_size += len(sentence)
            
            # Break if: at breakpoint AND above min size
            # OR: above max size (force break)
            should_break = (
                (i in breakpoints and current_size >= self.min_size) or
                current_size >= self.max_size
            )
            
            if should_break and i < len(sentences) - 1:
                chunk_text = " ".join(current_chunk_sentences)
                chunks.append({
                    "text": chunk_text,
                    "token_count": current_size // 4,  # rough estimate
                    "sentence_indices": list(range(
                        i - len(current_chunk_sentences) + 1, i + 1
                    ))
                })
                current_chunk_sentences = []
                current_size = 0
        
        # Don't forget last chunk
        if current_chunk_sentences:
            chunks.append({
                "text": " ".join(current_chunk_sentences),
                "token_count": current_size // 4,
                "sentence_indices": list(range(
                    len(sentences) - len(current_chunk_sentences), len(sentences)
                ))
            })
        
        return chunks
    
    def _find_breakpoints(self, similarities: List[float]) -> set:
        """找到 similarity 顯著下降的位置"""
        if not similarities:
            return set()
        
        mean_sim = np.mean(similarities)
        std_sim = np.std(similarities)
        threshold = mean_sim - std_sim  # 低於平均 1 標準差 = breakpoint
        
        breakpoints = set()
        for i, sim in enumerate(similarities):
            if sim < threshold:
                breakpoints.add(i)
        
        return breakpoints

3.2 Hybrid Search（混合檢索）

class HybridRetriever:
    """
    混合檢索：Dense (語義) + Sparse (關鍵字) 的加權組合
    
    為什麼需要 Hybrid：
    - Dense (embedding): 擅長語義理解，但對精確數字/名稱匹配差
    - Sparse (BM25): 擅長 exact match，但不理解同義詞
    - Hybrid: 取兩者之長，recall 提升 10-20%
    """
    def __init__(self, dense_index, sparse_index, 
                 alpha: float = 0.7):
        self.dense_index = dense_index    # FAISS / Pinecone
        self.sparse_index = sparse_index  # Elasticsearch / BM25
        self.alpha = alpha                # Dense weight
    
    def search(self, query: str, top_k: int = 50,
               user_acl: set = None) -> List[dict]:
        """
        混合搜索 with ACL filtering
        """
        # 1. Dense search (semantic)
        query_embedding = self.encode_query(query)
        dense_results = self.dense_index.search(
            query_embedding, top_k=top_k * 2  # Over-fetch for ACL filtering
        )
        
        # 2. Sparse search (BM25 keyword)
        sparse_results = self.sparse_index.search(
            query, top_k=top_k * 2
        )
        
        # 3. Score fusion (Reciprocal Rank Fusion)
        fused_scores = self._reciprocal_rank_fusion(
            dense_results, sparse_results
        )
        
        # 4. ACL filtering
        if user_acl:
            fused_scores = {
                doc_id: score for doc_id, score in fused_scores.items()
                if self._check_access(doc_id, user_acl)
            }
        
        # 5. Return top-K
        sorted_results = sorted(
            fused_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True
        )[:top_k]
        
        return [{"doc_id": did, "score": score} for did, score in sorted_results]
    
    def _reciprocal_rank_fusion(self, dense_results, sparse_results, 
                                 k=60) -> dict:
        """
        RRF Score = Σ 1/(k + rank_i)
        k=60 是 empirical best constant
        比 linear combination 更 robust（不需要 calibrate scores）
        """
        fused = {}
        
        for rank, result in enumerate(dense_results):
            doc_id = result["doc_id"]
            fused[doc_id] = fused.get(doc_id, 0) + 1.0 / (k + rank + 1)
        
        for rank, result in enumerate(sparse_results):
            doc_id = result["doc_id"]
            fused[doc_id] = fused.get(doc_id, 0) + 1.0 / (k + rank + 1)
        
        return fused
    
    def _check_access(self, doc_id: str, user_acl: set) -> bool:
        """Pre-filter: 只返回用戶有權限的文件"""
        doc_acl = self.acl_store.get(doc_id)  # {allowed_users, allowed_groups}
        return bool(user_acl & doc_acl)

3.3 Multi-hop Reasoning（多跳推理）

class MultiHopRAG:
    """
    Multi-hop Reasoning: 需要綜合多個文件的信息才能回答
    
    Example:
    Q: "公司 2023 年的營收成長率是多少？"
    需要: (1) 2023 年營收 (Doc A) + (2) 2022 年營收 (Doc B) → 計算成長率
    
    方法: Iterative retrieval
    1. 第一次檢索 → 找到部分信息
    2. 分析缺什麼 → 自動生成 follow-up query
    3. 第二次檢索 → 補充缺失信息
    4. 綜合所有信息 → 生成最終答案
    """
    def __init__(self, retriever: HybridRetriever, llm, max_hops: int = 3):
        self.retriever = retriever
        self.llm = llm
        self.max_hops = max_hops
    
    def answer(self, question: str, user_acl: set) -> dict:
        """
        Iterative Retrieval-Augmented Generation
        """
        all_retrieved_chunks = []
        reasoning_chain = []
        
        current_query = question
        
        for hop in range(self.max_hops):
            # Retrieve relevant chunks
            results = self.retriever.search(
                current_query, top_k=10, user_acl=user_acl
            )
            chunks = self._fetch_chunk_texts(results)
            all_retrieved_chunks.extend(chunks)
            
            # Ask LLM: can we answer now, or need more info?
            analysis = self._analyze_sufficiency(
                question, all_retrieved_chunks
            )
            
            reasoning_chain.append({
                "hop": hop + 1,
                "query": current_query,
                "retrieved": len(chunks),
                "sufficient": analysis["sufficient"]
            })
            
            if analysis["sufficient"]:
                break
            else:
                # Generate follow-up query for missing information
                current_query = analysis["follow_up_query"]
        
        # Generate final answer with citations
        answer = self._generate_with_citations(
            question, all_retrieved_chunks
        )
        
        return {
            "answer": answer["text"],
            "citations": answer["citations"],
            "reasoning_chain": reasoning_chain,
            "confidence": answer["confidence"]
        }
    
    def _analyze_sufficiency(self, question: str, 
                             chunks: List[str]) -> dict:
        """
        讓 LLM 判斷目前的信息是否足夠回答問題
        """
        prompt = f"""Based on the retrieved information, determine:
1. Can the question be fully answered? (yes/no)
2. If no, what specific information is still missing?
3. Generate a search query to find the missing information.

Question: {question}

Retrieved Information:
{self._format_chunks(chunks)}

Response (JSON):
"""
        response = self.llm.generate(prompt)
        return self._parse_json(response)
    
    def _generate_with_citations(self, question: str, 
                                  chunks: List[dict]) -> dict:
        """
        生成答案 + inline citations [1][2]...
        """
        numbered_chunks = "\n".join(
            f"[{i+1}] (Source: {c['doc_title']}, Page {c['page']})\n{c['text']}"
            for i, c in enumerate(chunks)
        )
        
        prompt = f"""Answer the question based ONLY on the provided sources.
Include citation numbers [1], [2], etc. for each claim.
If the answer cannot be determined from the sources, say "I don't have enough information."

Sources:
{numbered_chunks}

Question: {question}

Answer (with citations):"""
        
        response = self.llm.generate(prompt)
        
        # Extract and validate citations
        citations = self._extract_citations(response, chunks)
        confidence = self._calculate_confidence(response, chunks)
        
        return {
            "text": response,
            "citations": citations,
            "confidence": confidence
        }

3.4 Access Control（權限控制）

class RAGAccessControl:
    """
    RAG 系統的 Access Control 有獨特挑戰：
    
    1. 即使用戶無法直接看到文件，如果 RAG 答案中包含了
       該文件的信息 → 間接洩漏（Information Leakage）
    2. 文件可能有不同層級的 sensitivity
    3. ACL 變更後，向量索引中的結果也需要即時反映
    
    解法：Pre-filter + Post-filter + Answer sanitization
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.doc_acl_store = {}  # doc_id → {users, groups, classification}
    
    def pre_filter_search(self, query_embedding, user: str, 
                          top_k: int) -> List[str]:
        """
        Pre-filter: 在 ANN 搜索時就限制只搜用戶有權限的文件
        
        實作方式：
        - Option A: 每個 ACL group 建一個獨立 index（簡單但浪費空間）
        - Option B: 用 metadata filter（如 Pinecone, Qdrant 支援）
        - Option C: 取 top-K × 3，再 post-filter（最通用）
        """
        # Over-fetch then filter (Option C)
        candidates = self.vector_index.search(
            query_embedding, top_k=top_k * 3
        )
        
        # Filter by ACL
        user_groups = self._get_user_groups(user)
        allowed = []
        for doc_id, score in candidates:
            doc_acl = self.doc_acl_store.get(doc_id, {})
            if (user in doc_acl.get("users", set()) or
                user_groups & doc_acl.get("groups", set())):
                allowed.append((doc_id, score))
                if len(allowed) >= top_k:
                    break
        
        return allowed
    
    def sanitize_answer(self, answer: str, citations: List[dict],
                        user: str) -> str:
        """
        Post-generation check: 確保答案不包含用戶無權存取的資訊
        
        場景：LLM 可能在生成過程中「推理」出用戶無權看到的信息
        （即使 retrieved chunks 都是有權限的，LLM 可能 hallucinate
         出其他文件的內容）
        """
        # Verify each citation
        for citation in citations:
            if not self._user_has_access(user, citation["doc_id"]):
                # Remove this citation and redact related content
                answer = self._redact_citation(answer, citation)
        
        return answer

🔹 Step 4: Evaluation & Metrics

指標定義目標評估方式
Retrieval Recall@10正確答案在 top-10 chunks 中>90%Human-labeled Q&A pairs
Answer Correctness答案內容正確>85%Human evaluation
Faithfulness答案有 source 支撐（非 hallucination）>95%NLI model verification
Citation Precision引用的來源確實支持該陳述>90%Human spot-check
Latency (e2e)Query → Answer<3sp99 monitoring
ACL Violation洩漏無權限資訊0Red team testing

⚖️ Key Trade-offs

  • Chunk Size：小 chunk (200 tokens) → 精準但碎片化；大 chunk (1000 tokens) → 完整但雜訊多。Sweet spot: 400-600 tokens with overlap。
  • Retrieval Depth：更多 chunks → 更高 recall 但 LLM context 成本高。通常 5-10 chunks 最佳。
  • Multi-hop 次數：更多 hops → 更準確的複雜問答，但延遲 ×N。限制 ≤3 hops。
  • ACL Pre-filter vs Post-filter：Pre-filter 快但可能 under-recall；Post-filter 全面但 over-fetch 成本高。

🔹 Follow-up Questions

Q1: 如何減少 hallucination（幻覺）？

答：多層次防護：

  1. Retrieval verification：答案中的每個 claim 必須有對應 citation。無法找到支撐 → 不生成該 claim
  2. NLI (Natural Language Inference) filter：用 NLI 模型驗證 "claim entailed by source?" → 不 entailed 就移除
  3. Constrained generation：使用 "Only answer based on the provided sources" system prompt
  4. Confidence scoring：LLM 自評信心 → 低信心答案標注 "This answer may be incomplete"
  5. Abstention：偵測到 LLM 在 "making things up" 時主動回答 "I don't know"
def verify_faithfulness(answer: str, sources: List[str]) -> float:
    """
    用 NLI 模型驗證答案忠實度
    Returns: 0.0 (全部 hallucinated) ~ 1.0 (完全有依據)
    """
    claims = extract_claims(answer)  # 拆解答案為個別陳述
    supported_count = 0
    
    for claim in claims:
        # Check if ANY source entails this claim
        for source in sources:
            nli_result = nli_model.predict(premise=source, hypothesis=claim)
            if nli_result == "entailment":
                supported_count += 1
                break
    
    return supported_count / len(claims) if claims else 1.0
Q2: HyDE (Hypothetical Document Embedding) 如何改善檢索？

問題：用戶的 query 是問句，但文件中的答案是陳述句 → 語義空間不匹配 → embedding similarity 低。

HyDE 解法：

  1. 用 LLM 先生成一個「假設性答案」（不需要正確，只需要像答案的語調）
  2. 用這個假設答案的 embedding 去搜索 → 更接近文件的語義空間
def hyde_search(query: str, retriever, llm) -> List[dict]:
    """
    Step 1: LLM 生成假設性答案
    Step 2: 用假設答案的 embedding 搜索
    """
    # Generate hypothetical answer (doesn't need to be correct!)
    hypothetical = llm.generate(
        f"Write a short paragraph that would answer: {query}"
    )
    
    # Use hypothetical answer for retrieval (better embedding match)
    hyp_embedding = retriever.encode(hypothetical)
    
    # Also search with original query (hedging)
    query_embedding = retriever.encode(query)
    
    # Combine results
    hyp_results = retriever.search_by_embedding(hyp_embedding, top_k=20)
    query_results = retriever.search_by_embedding(query_embedding, top_k=20)
    
    # RRF fusion
    return reciprocal_rank_fusion(hyp_results, query_results)

效果：在 NQ (Natural Questions) 和 TriviaQA 上，HyDE 平均提升 recall@10 約 5-10%。

Q3: 如何處理表格和結構化數據中的信息？

問題：表格中的數字和結構化信息用 embedding similarity 很難匹配（"Q4 revenue" 和 "$2.5B" 語義相似度低）。

解法：

  1. Table serialization：將表格轉為自然語言描述（"In Q4 2023, revenue was $2.5B, up 15% YoY"）
  2. Structured queries：偵測數值/比較問題 → 自動生成 SQL query 查表格
  3. Table-specific embedding：用 TableFormer 等模型專門編碼表格結構
  4. Multi-index：文本 chunks → embedding index；表格 → structured database；query router 決定走哪條路
Q4: 新文件如何快速進入索引？（Freshness <1 hour）

答：Streaming indexing pipeline：

  1. Document ingestion：新文件到達 → Kafka event
  2. Async processing：Chunking + Embedding + ACL extraction（Flink job，~30s per doc）
  3. Incremental index update：新 vectors 加入 HNSW index（不需要 rebuild 整個 index）
  4. Eventual consistency：新文件可能需要 1-5 min 才能被搜索到（acceptable for most use cases）

HNSW 的優勢：支援 incremental insert（不像 IVF 需要 retrain centroids），新 vector 插入後立即可搜索。

Q5: 如何評估 RAG 系統的品質？

答：RAGAS framework (Retrieval Augmented Generation Assessment)：

指標衡量什麼計算方式
Context RelevancyRetrieved chunks 是否相關LLM 判斷 chunk 是否含有答案信息
Faithfulness答案是否基於 sourcesNLI: claim entailed by source?
Answer Relevancy答案是否回答了問題反向問題生成 + semantic similarity
Answer Correctness答案是否事實正確vs ground truth (human labeled)
# RAGAS-style evaluation
def evaluate_rag_response(question, answer, contexts, ground_truth):
    scores = {
        "context_relevancy": compute_context_relevancy(question, contexts),
        "faithfulness": verify_faithfulness(answer, contexts),
        "answer_relevancy": compute_answer_relevancy(question, answer),
        "correctness": compute_correctness(answer, ground_truth)
    }
    scores["overall"] = np.mean(list(scores.values()))
    return scores
Q6: 如何處理 contradictory sources（矛盾資料）？

問題：不同文件對同一問題有矛盾信息（版本不同、部門觀點不同）。

解法：

  1. Recency preference：同一主題的文件，優先使用最新版本
  2. Authority hierarchy：官方文件 > 內部 wiki > 個人筆記
  3. Explicit disclosure：偵測到矛盾時，答案中標注 "Sources disagree: Source A says X, but Source B says Y"
  4. Version tracking：文件的 supersedes 關係 → 自動排除過期版本

🔥 BONUS: 5 Quick-Fire System Design Problems

以下每題用精簡格式呈現核心設計要點，適合 15 分鐘快速討論或第二輪面試。

🔥 B1: Design Model Monitoring System

目標：偵測 production model 的 performance degradation + data drift，自動觸發 retrain。

┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ Model Monitoring System │ ├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ │ │ │ ┌──────────┐ ┌─────────────────┐ ┌──────────────────┐ │ │ │Production│───▶│ Logging Layer │───▶│ Feature Drift │ │ │ │ Model │ │ (predictions + │ │ Detector │ │ │ │ │ │ features + │ │ (PSI / KS-test) │ │ │ └──────────┘ │ ground truth) │ └────────┬─────────┘ │ │ └─────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ │ │ ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────▼──────────┐ │ │ │ Alert & Action Engine │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ ┌─────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌───────────────┐ │ │ │ │ │Performance │ │ Concept Drift│ │ Data Quality │ │ │ │ │ │ Degradation │ │ (prediction │ │ (NULL rate, │ │ │ │ │ │ (AUC drop) │ │ dist shift) │ │ outliers) │ │ │ │ │ └──────┬──────┘ └──────┬───────┘ └───────┬───────┘ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ └─────────────────┼───────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ ▼ │ │ │ │ ┌─────────────────────────┐ │ │ │ │ │ Action Router │ │ │ │ │ │ • Alert (PagerDuty) │ │ │ │ │ │ • Auto-retrain trigger │ │ │ │ │ │ • Rollback to prev ver │ │ │ │ │ │ • Shadow mode (pause) │ │ │ │ │ └─────────────────────────┘ │ │ │ └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ Key Metrics Monitored: │ │ • Prediction drift: P(ŷ) distribution shift │ │ • Feature drift: input feature distributions │ │ • Performance: AUC/F1 when ground truth available (delayed) │ │ • Latency: model serving p50/p99 │ │ • Data quality: NULL rate, type errors, range violations │ └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ 
class ModelMonitor:
    """
    Production Model Monitoring
    
    核心挑戰：Ground truth 通常有延遲
    - 推薦系統: 點擊/購買延遲 1-24 小時
    - 信用風險: 違約信號延遲 30-90 天
    
    解法: 用 proxy metrics 做即時監控 + ground truth 做回顧驗證
    """
    def __init__(self, model_name: str, baseline_metrics: dict):
        self.model_name = model_name
        self.baseline = baseline_metrics  # 上線時的 performance
        self.alert_rules = {
            "auc_drop": 0.02,         # AUC 下降 > 2% → alert
            "prediction_psi": 0.2,    # 預測分佈 PSI > 0.2 → alert
            "feature_psi": 0.25,      # 任何 feature PSI > 0.25 → alert
            "null_rate": 0.05,        # NULL 率 > 5% → alert
            "latency_p99_ms": 100     # 延遲 > 100ms → alert
        }
    
    def check_prediction_drift(self, recent_predictions: np.ndarray) -> dict:
        """
        偵測模型輸出分佈是否偏移
        - 不需要 ground truth
        - 即時偵測（無延遲）
        """
        psi = compute_psi(self.baseline["prediction_dist"], recent_predictions)
        
        return {
            "metric": "prediction_drift",
            "psi": psi,
            "alert": psi > self.alert_rules["prediction_psi"],
            "severity": "HIGH" if psi > 0.5 else "MEDIUM" if psi > 0.2 else "LOW",
            "action": "auto_retrain" if psi > 0.5 else "investigate"
        }
    
    def check_with_ground_truth(self, predictions, actuals) -> dict:
        """
        有 ground truth 時的完整 performance check
        （可能延遲數小時到數天）
        """
        from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score, f1_score
        
        current_auc = roc_auc_score(actuals, predictions)
        baseline_auc = self.baseline["auc"]
        auc_drop = baseline_auc - current_auc
        
        return {
            "current_auc": current_auc,
            "baseline_auc": baseline_auc,
            "auc_drop": auc_drop,
            "alert": auc_drop > self.alert_rules["auc_drop"],
            "recommendation": (
                "Trigger retraining pipeline" if auc_drop > 0.05
                else "Monitor closely" if auc_drop > 0.02
                else "Healthy"
            )
        }

⚖️ Key Trade-offs

  • Sensitivity vs False Alarms：閾值太低 → 太多 alert（alert fatigue）；太高 → 漏掉真正問題。解法：adaptive thresholds + multi-signal confirmation。
  • Auto-retrain vs Manual：自動重訓快但有風險（bad data → bad model）。解法：auto-retrain + shadow testing before promote。
  • Monitoring granularity：整體 AUC 可能 OK 但某個 segment 嚴重下降（Simpson's paradox）。解法：segmented monitoring by key dimensions。

🔥 B2: Design GPU Auto-Scaler

目標：根據 inference workload 動態調整 GPU serving cluster 大小，最小化成本同時滿足 SLA。

┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ GPU Auto-Scaler │ ├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ │ │ │ ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │ Metrics Collector │ │ │ │ • QPS (current + predicted) │ │ │ │ • GPU utilization per instance │ │ │ │ • Request queue depth │ │ │ │ • Latency percentiles (p50, p95, p99) │ │ │ │ • KV cache utilization (LLM specific) │ │ │ └─────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ │ ▼ │ │ ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │ Scaling Decision Engine │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ ┌──────────────┐ ┌─────────────┐ ┌─────────────┐ │ │ │ │ │ Reactive │ │ Predictive │ │ Scheduled │ │ │ │ │ │ (threshold) │ │ (ML-based) │ │ (cron) │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ if util>80% │ │ Prophet / │ │ Scale up │ │ │ │ │ │ → scale up │ │ LSTM pred │ │ before peak │ │ │ │ │ │ if util<30% │ │ 15min ahead │ │ (9am daily) │ │ │ │ │ │ → scale down │ │ → pre-scale │ │ │ │ │ │ │ └──────────────┘ └─────────────┘ └─────────────┘ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ Constraints: │ │ │ │ • Min instances: 2 (HA) │ │ │ │ • Max instances: 50 (budget cap) │ │ │ │ • Cooldown: 5 min between scale actions │ │ │ │ • Scale-up speed: <3 min (pre-warmed pool) │ │ │ │ • Scale-down: graceful drain (finish inflight reqs) │ │ │ └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ GPU-specific challenges: │ │ • Cold start: model loading = 30-60s (175B model) │ │ • Solution: keep warm pool of 2-3 standby instances │ │ • KV cache: can't migrate between instances │ │ • Cost: $30/hr per H100 → aggressive scale-down saves $$ │ └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ 
class GPUAutoScaler:
    """
    GPU Auto-Scaler: 平衡 SLA 和成本
    
    獨特挑戰 vs CPU auto-scaler:
    1. Scale-up 慢 (model loading 30-60s vs CPU 5s)
    2. 單位成本高 ($30/hr) → 過度 provision 代價大
    3. Workload bursty → 需要 predictive scaling
    4. GPU utilization 不等於 throughput (batch effect)
    """
    def __init__(self, min_instances=2, max_instances=50,
                 target_utilization=0.7, warm_pool_size=2):
        self.min_instances = min_instances
        self.max_instances = max_instances
        self.target_util = target_utilization
        self.warm_pool = warm_pool_size  # Pre-loaded standby instances
        self.cooldown_seconds = 300
        self.last_scale_time = 0
    
    def compute_desired_instances(self, metrics: dict) -> int:
        """
        綜合多種信號決定目標 instance 數
        """
        current_instances = metrics["current_instances"]
        
        # Signal 1: Utilization-based (reactive)
        avg_util = metrics["avg_gpu_utilization"]
        util_desired = int(
            current_instances * avg_util / self.target_util
        )
        
        # Signal 2: Queue-based (leading indicator)
        queue_depth = metrics["request_queue_depth"]
        throughput_per_instance = metrics["tokens_per_sec_per_instance"]
        queue_desired = int(
            queue_depth / (throughput_per_instance * 10)  # drain in 10s
        ) + current_instances
        
        # Signal 3: Latency-based (SLA)
        if metrics["p99_latency_ms"] > metrics["sla_latency_ms"] * 0.8:
            latency_desired = current_instances + 2  # Emergency scale
        else:
            latency_desired = current_instances
        
        # Signal 4: Predictive (next 15 min forecast)
        predicted_qps = metrics.get("predicted_qps_15min", None)
        if predicted_qps:
            pred_desired = int(predicted_qps / metrics["qps_per_instance"])
        else:
            pred_desired = current_instances
        
        # Take maximum (conservative: prefer over-provisioning to SLA breach)
        desired = max(util_desired, queue_desired, latency_desired, pred_desired)
        
        # Apply constraints
        desired = max(self.min_instances, min(desired, self.max_instances))
        
        return desired
    
    def scale_down_safely(self, instance_to_remove: str):
        """
        GPU scale-down 需要 graceful:
        1. Stop accepting new requests
        2. Wait for inflight requests to complete (KV cache drain)
        3. Then terminate
        """
        # Mark as draining (no new requests routed here)
        self.load_balancer.mark_draining(instance_to_remove)
        
        # Wait for inflight to complete (max 60s)
        timeout = 60
        while self.get_inflight_count(instance_to_remove) > 0 and timeout > 0:
            time.sleep(5)
            timeout -= 5
        
        # Terminate (or return to warm pool)
        if self.warm_pool_count() < self.warm_pool:
            self.move_to_warm_pool(instance_to_remove)
        else:
            self.terminate(instance_to_remove)

🔥 B3: Design Billion-Scale Embedding Search

目標：在 10 億向量中進行 <10ms 的近似最近鄰搜索。

┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ Billion-Scale ANN Search System │ ├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ │ │ │ Scale: 1B vectors × 768-dim × 4 bytes = 3 TB raw │ │ │ │ ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │ Index Strategy │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ Level 1: Coarse Quantization (IVF) │ │ │ │ • 65536 clusters (√N rule) │ │ │ │ • Each cluster: ~15K vectors │ │ │ │ • Search only top-128 clusters → 99% recall │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ Level 2: Product Quantization (PQ) │ │ │ │ • 768-dim → 96 sub-vectors × 8-dim │ │ │ │ • Each sub-vector: 256 centroids (8 bits) │ │ │ │ • Compression: 768×4 = 3072 → 96 bytes (32x) │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ Level 3: Re-ranking (optional) │ │ │ │ • Top-1000 from PQ → fetch full vectors → exact dist │ │ │ │ • 1000 × 768 × 4 = 3MB per query (fits in L2 cache) │ │ │ └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │ Sharding Strategy │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ • 1B vectors ÷ 50M per shard = 20 shards │ │ │ │ • Each shard: ~5GB (PQ compressed) │ │ │ │ • Each shard on 1 machine (fits in RAM) │ │ │ │ • Query fan-out to all 20 shards → merge top-K │ │ │ │ • 3 replicas per shard = 60 machines total │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ Memory per shard: │ │ │ │ • PQ codes: 50M × 96 bytes = 4.8 GB │ │ │ │ • IVF lists: ~1 GB │ │ │ │ • Metadata: ~2 GB │ │ │ │ Total: ~8 GB per shard (fits 16GB machine) │ │ │ └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ Performance (1B vectors, top-10): │ │ • IVF-PQ (nprobe=128): ~8ms, recall@10 ≈ 92% │ │ • + Re-ranking (top-1000): ~12ms, recall@10 ≈ 98% │ │ • HNSW (in-memory): ~5ms, recall@10 ≈ 97% (but 3TB RAM!) │ │ • DiskANN (SSD-based): ~15ms, recall@10 ≈ 95% (cheap!) │ └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ 
class BillionScaleANN:
    """
    十億級向量搜索的實際架構
    
    Key decisions:
    1. IVF-PQ for memory efficiency (32x compression)
    2. Sharding for parallelism (20 shards, fan-out query)
    3. Two-phase: PQ approximate → full vector re-rank
    4. Incremental updates via append-only + periodic rebuild
    """
    def __init__(self, dim=768, num_shards=20, pq_m=96, pq_nbits=8):
        self.dim = dim
        self.num_shards = num_shards
        self.shards = [ShardClient(i) for i in range(num_shards)]
    
    def search(self, query: np.ndarray, top_k: int = 10) -> List[dict]:
        """
        Distributed search across all shards
        Total latency: max(shard_latencies) + merge_time ≈ 10-12ms
        """
        # Fan-out to all shards (parallel)
        shard_results = parallel_execute([
            lambda s=shard: s.local_search(query, top_k=top_k * 2)
            for shard in self.shards
        ], timeout_ms=50)
        
        # Merge results from all shards
        all_candidates = []
        for result in shard_results:
            all_candidates.extend(result)
        
        # Sort by distance and take top-K
        all_candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x["distance"])
        return all_candidates[:top_k]
    
    def build_index(self, vectors: np.ndarray, ids: np.ndarray):
        """
        離線建索引 (1B vectors):
        - Step 1: Train PQ codebook on 10M sample (~1 hour)
        - Step 2: Shard vectors by hash(id) % num_shards
        - Step 3: Each shard builds local IVF-PQ index (~30 min)
        - Total: ~2 hours on 20 machines
        """
        # Train global PQ codebook (shared across shards)
        sample = vectors[np.random.choice(len(vectors), 10_000_000)]
        codebook = train_pq_codebook(sample, m=96, nbits=8)
        
        # Distribute to shards
        for shard_id in range(self.num_shards):
            mask = ids % self.num_shards == shard_id
            self.shards[shard_id].build_local_index(
                vectors[mask], ids[mask], codebook
            )

⚖️ Key Trade-offs: Memory vs Accuracy vs Latency

方案Memory (1B)LatencyRecall@10成本
Flat (brute force)3 TB~1000ms100%$$$$$
HNSW (in-memory)3 TB + graph~5ms97%$$$$$
IVF-PQ (RAM)~100 GB~8ms92%$$
IVF-PQ + re-rank~100 GB + 3TB SSD~12ms98%$$$
DiskANN (SSD)~50 GB RAM + 3TB SSD~15ms95%$$

🔥 B4: Design Data Labeling Platform

目標：支援 1000+ annotators 高效標注訓練數據，包含品質控制和主動學習。

┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ Data Labeling Platform │ ├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ │ │ │ ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │ Task Management Layer │ │ │ │ • Project creation + template library │ │ │ │ • Work distribution (load balancing across annotators) │ │ │ │ • Priority queue (active learning selects most valuable) │ │ │ │ • Deadline + throughput tracking │ │ │ └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ ┌───────────┐ ┌────────────────────┐ ┌──────────────────┐ │ │ │ Annotation│ │ Quality Control │ │ Active Learning │ │ │ │ Interface │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ • Inter-annotator │ │ • Uncertainty │ │ │ │ • Web UI │ │ agreement (IAA) │ │ sampling │ │ │ │ • Mobile │ │ • Gold standard │ │ • Diversity │ │ │ │ • API │ │ (honeypot) QA │ │ sampling │ │ │ │ │ │ • Consensus voting │ │ • Model-in-loop │ │ │ │ Templates:│ │ (3-way agree) │ │ (pre-label) │ │ │ │ • BBox │ │ • Adjudication │ │ │ │ │ │ • NER │ │ workflow │ │ Reduces labeling │ │ │ │ • Class │ │ • Performance │ │ cost by 40-60% │ │ │ │ • Segment │ │ tracking/score │ │ │ │ │ └───────────┘ └────────────────────┘ └──────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │ Data Pipeline │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ Raw Data ──▶ Pre-processing ──▶ Task Queue ──▶ Labeled │ │ │ │ (S3) (resize, sample) (Redis) Data │ │ │ │ (versioned)│ │ │ │ Feedback loop: Labeled → Train → Model → Active Learning │ │ │ │ ↓ │ │ │ │ Pre-label (reduce human work) │ │ │ └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ Key Metrics: │ │ • Throughput: annotations/hour (target: 15+) │ │ • Quality: IAA > 0.85 (Cohen's Kappa) │ │ • Cost: $/annotation (target: $0.05-0.50 depending on task) │ │ • Active Learning lift: 60% less data needed for same accuracy │ └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ 
class ActiveLearningSelector:
    """
    Active Learning: 智能選擇最有價值的樣本給人工標注
    
    價值 = 模型對該樣本的不確定性 × 該樣本的代表性
    
    效果: 相比隨機標注，只需 40% 的數據量就能達到相同模型準確度
    """
    def select_batch(self, unlabeled_pool: List[dict], 
                     model, batch_size: int = 100) -> List[dict]:
        """
        選擇下一批最有價值的樣本
        策略: Uncertainty × Diversity
        """
        # 1. Compute uncertainty for all unlabeled samples
        predictions = model.predict_proba(unlabeled_pool)
        uncertainties = self._compute_uncertainty(predictions)
        
        # 2. Pre-filter top-N most uncertain (N = batch_size * 5)
        top_uncertain_idx = np.argsort(uncertainties)[-batch_size * 5:]
        candidates = [unlabeled_pool[i] for i in top_uncertain_idx]
        
        # 3. Diversity selection from candidates (avoid redundancy)
        selected = self._diversity_sampling(candidates, batch_size)
        
        return selected
    
    def _compute_uncertainty(self, predictions: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Entropy-based uncertainty:
        H(p) = -Σ p_i × log(p_i)
        高 entropy = 模型不確定 = 最有標注價值
        """
        # Clip to avoid log(0)
        predictions = np.clip(predictions, 1e-10, 1.0)
        entropy = -np.sum(predictions * np.log(predictions), axis=1)
        return entropy


class QualityController:
    """
    標注品質控制
    """
    def compute_inter_annotator_agreement(self, 
                                           annotations: List[List[int]]) -> float:
        """
        Cohen's Kappa: 衡量標注者間的一致性
        κ = (P_o - P_e) / (1 - P_e)
        κ > 0.8: 優秀, 0.6-0.8: 良好, < 0.6: 需改進
        """
        # ... implementation
        pass
    
    def honeypot_check(self, annotator_id: str, 
                       recent_honeypots: List[dict]) -> dict:
        """
        Gold standard (honeypot) 檢查:
        - 隨機插入已知正確答案的樣本 (5-10% of tasks)
        - 標注者不知道哪些是 honeypot
        - 準確率 < 80% → 暫停 + retraining
        """
        correct = sum(1 for h in recent_honeypots if h["correct"])
        accuracy = correct / len(recent_honeypots)
        
        return {
            "annotator": annotator_id,
            "honeypot_accuracy": accuracy,
            "action": "suspend" if accuracy < 0.7 
                     else "warning" if accuracy < 0.8 
                     else "pass"
        }

🔥 B5: Design Multi-Modal ML Pipeline

目標：處理 text + image + video + audio 的統一 ML pipeline，支援訓練和推論。

┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ Multi-Modal ML Pipeline │ ├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ │ │ │ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │ Input Processing Layer │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ ┌────────┐ ┌────────┐ ┌────────┐ ┌────────────┐ │ │ │ │ │ Text │ │ Image │ │ Video │ │ Audio │ │ │ │ │ │Tokenize│ │ Resize │ │ Frames │ │ Mel-Spec │ │ │ │ │ │ BPE │ │ +Norm │ │ Sample │ │ Whisper │ │ │ │ │ │ <512 │ │ 224² │ │ 8 fps │ │ Encoder │ │ │ │ │ └───┬────┘ └───┬────┘ └───┬────┘ └─────┬──────┘ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ ▼ ▼ ▼ ▼ │ │ │ │ ┌────────┐ ┌────────┐ ┌────────┐ ┌────────────┐ │ │ │ │ │ Text │ │ Vision │ │ Video │ │ Audio │ │ │ │ │ │Encoder │ │Encoder │ │Encoder │ │ Encoder │ │ │ │ │ │(LLaMA) │ │(ViT-L) │ │(ViViT)│ │(Whisper-L) │ │ │ │ │ └───┬────┘ └───┬────┘ └───┬────┘ └─────┬──────┘ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ └──────┼────────────┼────────────┼──────────────┼──────────┘ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ ▼ ▼ ▼ ▼ │ │ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │ Fusion Layer (Cross-Attention) │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ Strategy A: Early Fusion (concat all tokens → transformer)│ │ │ │ Strategy B: Late Fusion (separate encoders → project → combine)│ │ │ Strategy C: Cross-Attention (each modality attends to others)│ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ Output: Unified multi-modal embedding [1, hidden_dim] │ │ │ └──────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ │ ▼ │ │ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │ Task Heads │ │ │ │ │ │ │ │ • Captioning (image/video → text) │ │ │ │ • VQA (image + question → answer) │ │ │ │ • Retrieval (any modality → similar items) │ │ │ │ • Classification (multi-modal input → label) │ │ │ │ • Generation (text prompt → image/audio) │ │ │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │ │ │ │ Pipeline Challenges: │ │ • Data loading: Video (100MB/sample) → GPU starvation │ │ • Solution: Pre-extracted features + WebDataset streaming │ │ • Memory: 4 encoders × 1B params = 16GB just for encoders │ │ • Solution: Frozen pre-trained encoders + train only fusion │ │ • Alignment: Different sampling rates (text:token, video:frame) │ │ • Solution: Learnable positional interpolation │ └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ 
class MultiModalPipeline:
    """
    Multi-Modal Pipeline Design Principles:
    
    1. Modular encoders: 每個 modality 獨立 encoder (可替換)
    2. Frozen backbones: 大 encoder 凍結，只訓練 fusion layer (省資源)
    3. Streaming data: 大文件(video/audio) 用 streaming 不全部載入 RAM
    4. Graceful degradation: 缺少某 modality 時仍能工作
    """
    def __init__(self):
        # Frozen pre-trained encoders (only load needed modalities)
        self.text_encoder = load_frozen("llama-7b-encoder")   # 14GB
        self.vision_encoder = load_frozen("vit-large-patch14") # 1.2GB
        self.audio_encoder = load_frozen("whisper-large")      # 3GB
        
        # Trainable fusion (small, ~500M params)
        self.fusion = CrossAttentionFusion(
            modalities=["text", "vision", "audio"],
            hidden_dim=1024,
            num_layers=6
        )
    
    def forward(self, batch: dict) -> dict:
        """
        Input batch may have any combination of modalities
        Missing modalities → skip encoder, use padding token
        """
        embeddings = {}
        
        if "text" in batch:
            with torch.no_grad():  # Frozen encoder
                embeddings["text"] = self.text_encoder(batch["text"])
        
        if "image" in batch:
            with torch.no_grad():
                embeddings["vision"] = self.vision_encoder(batch["image"])
        
        if "audio" in batch:
            with torch.no_grad():
                embeddings["audio"] = self.audio_encoder(batch["audio"])
        
        # Fusion: cross-attention across available modalities
        fused = self.fusion(embeddings)  # [B, seq_len, 1024]
        
        return fused
    
    def data_loading_optimization(self):
        """
        Video/Audio 數據太大 → 需要特殊 data loading 策略
        
        1. Pre-extract features offline (video → frame embeddings)
        2. WebDataset: tar-based streaming (no random access needed)
        3. Async prefetch: 3-stage pipeline (read → decode → augment)
        4. Dynamic batching: group by similar lengths to reduce padding
        """
        # WebDataset streaming (no need to download all data first)
        dataset = (
            wds.WebDataset("s3://bucket/shard-{000..999}.tar")
            .decode("pil")  # Lazy decode images
            .rename(image="jpg", text="txt", audio="flac")
            .to_tuple("image", "text", "audio")
            .batched(32)
        )
        return dataset

⚖️ Key Trade-offs: Multi-Modal Design

  • Early vs Late Fusion：Early (更好的跨模態理解) vs Late (模組化、缺少模態時更 robust)。GPT-4V 使用 early fusion；CLIP 使用 late fusion。
  • Frozen vs Fine-tuned Encoders：Fine-tune → 品質更好但需要更多 GPU、可能 catastrophic forgetting。Frozen → 便宜、穩定但上限低。常用策略：LoRA fine-tune。
  • Pre-extracted Features vs End-to-End：Pre-extract → 訓練快 10x 但丟失 data augmentation 能力。解法：重要任務用 end-to-end，exploration 用 pre-extracted。
  • Unified vs Separate Models：統一模型（如 GPT-4o）→ 跨模態推理強；獨立模型 → 各自最優 + 可獨立升級。

🎯 面試通用技巧（適用所有 ML System Design）

  1. 先問清楚 scale：QPS、data size、latency SLA 決定了 80% 的架構選擇
  2. 畫清楚 data flow：offline (training) vs online (serving) 的邊界
  3. 量化 trade-offs：不只說 "A 比 B 快"，要說 "A 是 5ms 但 recall 92%, B 是 12ms 但 98%"
  4. 提 monitoring：面試官希望看到你考慮 production 的 observability
  5. 提 failure modes：如果 X 壞了會怎樣？→ 顯示你的 operational maturity
